MolmoB0T: Large-Scale Simulation Enables Zero-Shot Manipulation

Abstract (English): A prevailing view in robot learning is that simulation alone is not enough; effective sim-to-real transfer is widely believed to require at least some real-world data collection or task-specific fine-tuning to bridge the gap between simulated and physical environments. We challenge that assumption. With sufficiently large-scale and diverse simulated synthetic training data, we show that zero-shot transfer to the real world is not only possible, but effective for both static and mobile manipulation. We introduce MolmoBot-Engine, a fully open-source pipeline for procedural data generation across robots, tasks, and diverse simulated environments in MolmoSpaces. With it, we release MolmoBot-Data, a dataset of 1.8 million expert trajectories for articulated object manipulation and pick-and-place tasks. We train three policy classes: MolmoBot, a Molmo2-based multi-frame vision-language model with a flow-matching action head; MolmoBot-Pi0, which replicates the $π_0$ architecture to enable direct comparison; and MolmoBot-SPOC, a lightweight policy suitable for edge deployment and amenable to RL fine-tuning. We evaluate on two robotic platforms: the Franka FR3 for tabletop manipulation tasks and the Rainbow Robotics RB-Y1 mobile manipulator for door opening, drawer manipulation, cabinet interaction, and mobile pick-and-place. Without any real-world fine-tuning, our policies achieve zero-shot transfer to unseen objects and environments. On tabletop pick-and-place, MolmoBot achieves a success rate of 79.2% in real world evaluations across 4 settings, outperforming $π_{0.5}$ at 39.2%. Our results demonstrate that procedural environment generation combined with diverse articulated assets can produce robust manipulation policies that generalize broadly to the real world. Technical Blog: https://allenai.org/blog/molmobot-robot-manipulation

摘要（中文）: 机器人学习领域的主流观点认为，仅靠仿真是不够的；要实现有效的虚实迁移，普遍认为至少需要收集一些真实世界的数据或进行特定任务的微调，以弥合模拟环境和物理环境之间的差距。我们对这一假设提出了挑战。我们表明，通过足够大规模和多样化的仿真合成训练数据，零样本迁移到真实世界不仅是可能的，而且对于静态和移动操作都是有效的。我们介绍MolmoBot-Engine，这是一个完全开源的流程，用于跨机器人、任务和MolmoSpaces中各种模拟环境进行程序化数据生成。随之，我们发布MolmoBot-Data，这是一个包含180万条专家轨迹的数据集，用于铰接物体操作和抓取放置任务。我们训练了三类策略：MolmoBot，一个基于Molmo2的多帧视觉-语言模型，带有一个流动匹配动作头；MolmoBot-Pi0，它复制了$\pi_0$架构以实现直接比较；以及MolmoBot-SPOC，一个轻量级策略，适用于边缘部署并易于进行强化学习微调。我们在两个机器人平台上进行了评估：Franka FR3用于桌面操作任务，Rainbow Robotics RB-Y1移动机械臂用于开门、抽屉操作、橱柜交互以及移动抓取放置。在没有任何真实世界微调的情况下，我们的策略实现了对未见过的物体和环境的零样本迁移。在桌面抓取放置任务中，MolmoBot在4个真实世界设置中的成功率达到79.2%，优于$\pi_{0.5}$ 的39.2%。我们的结果表明，程序化环境生成与多样化的铰接资产相结合，可以产生鲁棒的操作策略，从而广泛地推广到真实世界。技术博客：https://allenai.org/blog/molmobot-robot-manipulation

TLDR: The paper presents MolmoB0T, a system achieving zero-shot sim-to-real transfer for robotic manipulation using large-scale, procedurally generated synthetic data and vision-language models, demonstrating its effectiveness on various tasks and platforms.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了 MolmoB0T，一个通过大规模、程序化生成的合成数据和视觉语言模型实现机器人操作零样本模拟到真实的系统，并在各种任务和平台上展示了其有效性。

Authors: Abhay Deshpande, Maya Guru, Rose Hendrix, Snehal Jauhri, Ainaz Eftekhar, Rohun Tripathi, Max Argus, Jordi Salvador, Haoquan Fang, Matthew Wallingford, Wilbert Pumacay, Yejin Kim, Quinn Pfeifer, Ying-Chun Lee, Piper Wolters, Omar Rayyan, Mingtong Zhang, Jiafei Duan, Karen Farley, Winson Han, Eli Vanderbilt, Dieter Fox, Ali Farhadi, Georgia Chalvatzaki, Dhruv Shah, Ranjay Krishna

DreamPlan: Efficient Reinforcement Fine-Tuning of Vision-Language Planners via Video World Models

Abstract (English): Robotic manipulation requires sophisticated commonsense reasoning, a capability naturally possessed by large-scale Vision-Language Models (VLMs). While VLMs show promise as zero-shot planners, their lack of grounded physical understanding often leads to compounding errors and low success rates when deployed in complex real-world environments, particularly for challenging tasks like deformable object manipulation. Although Reinforcement Learning (RL) can adapt these planners to specific task dynamics, directly fine-tuning VLMs via real-world interaction is prohibitively expensive, unsafe, and sample-inefficient. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce DreamPlan, a novel framework for the reinforcement fine-tuning of VLM planners via video world models. Instead of relying on costly physical rollouts, DreamPlan first leverages the zero-shot VLM to collect exploratory interaction data. We demonstrate that this sub-optimal data is sufficient to train an action-conditioned video generation model, which implicitly captures complex real-world physics. Subsequently, the VLM planner is fine-tuned entirely within the "imagination" of this video world model using Odds Ratio Policy Optimization (ORPO). By utilizing these virtual rollouts, physical and task-specific knowledge is efficiently injected into the VLM. Our results indicate that DreamPlan bridges the gap between semantic reasoning and physical grounding, significantly improving manipulation success rates without the need for large-scale real-world data collection. Our project page is https://psi-lab.ai/DreamPlan/.

摘要（中文）: 机器人操作需要复杂的常识推理能力，而这恰恰是大型视觉-语言模型(VLMs)所自然具备的。虽然VLMs作为零样本规划器展现出潜力，但它们缺乏具身的物理理解，这通常会导致累积误差和在复杂真实世界环境中部署时的低成功率，特别是对于像可变形物体操控这样的具有挑战性的任务。虽然强化学习(RL)可以使这些规划器适应特定的任务动态，但通过真实世界的交互直接微调VLMs的代价非常高昂、不安全并且样本效率低下。为了克服这个瓶颈，我们引入了DreamPlan，一种通过视频世界模型对VLM规划器进行强化微调的新型框架。DreamPlan不是依赖于昂贵的物理实验，而是首先利用零样本VLM收集探索性交互数据。我们证明，这种次优数据足以训练一个动作条件视频生成模型，该模型隐式地捕获了复杂的真实世界物理特性。随后，VLM规划器完全在这个视频世界模型的“想象”中，使用胜率策略优化(ORPO)进行微调。通过利用这些虚拟实验，物理和任务相关的知识被有效地注入到VLM中。我们的结果表明，DreamPlan弥合了语义推理和物理具身之间的差距，在不需要大规模真实世界数据收集的情况下，显著提高了操作成功率。我们的项目页面是https://psi-lab.ai/DreamPlan/。

TLDR: DreamPlan proposes a framework to fine-tune Vision-Language Models for robotic manipulation by using a video world model trained on exploratory data to enable reinforcement learning in simulation, thereby improving real-world performance without extensive real-world data collection.

简要说明: DreamPlan 提出了一种框架，通过使用在探索性数据上训练的视频世界模型，对视觉-语言模型进行微调，以用于机器人操作，从而能够在模拟中进行强化学习，并在不需要大量真实世界数据收集的情况下提高真实世界的性能。

Authors: Emily Yue-Ting Jia, Weiduo Yuan, Tianheng Shi, Vitor Guizilini, Jiageng Mao, Yue Wang

Kinema4D: Kinematic 4D World Modeling for Spatiotemporal Embodied Simulation

Abstract (English): Simulating robot-world interactions is a cornerstone of Embodied AI. Recently, a few works have shown promise in leveraging video generations to transcend the rigid visual/physical constraints of traditional simulators. However, they primarily operate in 2D space or are guided by static environmental cues, ignoring the fundamental reality that robot-world interactions are inherently 4D spatiotemporal events that require precise interactive modeling. To restore this 4D essence while ensuring the precise robot control, we introduce Kinema4D, a new action-conditioned 4D generative robotic simulator that disentangles the robot-world interaction into: i) Precise 4D representation of robot controls: we drive a URDF-based 3D robot via kinematics, producing a precise 4D robot control trajectory. ii) Generative 4D modeling of environmental reactions: we project the 4D robot trajectory into a pointmap as a spatiotemporal visual signal, controlling the generative model to synthesize complex environments' reactive dynamics into synchronized RGB/pointmap sequences. To facilitate training, we curated a large-scale dataset called Robo4D-200k, comprising 201,426 robot interaction episodes with high-quality 4D annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method effectively simulates physically-plausible, geometry-consistent, and embodiment-agnostic interactions that faithfully mirror diverse real-world dynamics. For the first time, it shows potential zero-shot transfer capability, providing a high-fidelity foundation for advancing next-generation embodied simulation.

摘要（中文）: 模拟机器人与世界的交互是具身人工智能的基石。 近期，一些研究表明，利用视频生成技术有望超越传统模拟器的严格视觉/物理约束。 然而，它们主要在二维空间中运行，或受静态环境线索引导，忽略了机器人与世界的交互本质上是需要精确交互建模的四维时空事件这一基本现实。 为了恢复这种四维本质，同时确保精确的机器人控制，我们引入了 Kinema4D，一种新型动作条件四维生成式机器人模拟器，它将机器人与世界的交互分解为：i) 精确的机器人控制四维表示：我们通过运动学驱动基于URDF的三维机器人，生成精确的机器人控制四维轨迹。 ii) 环境反应的生成式四维建模：我们将四维机器人轨迹投影到点云图，作为时空视觉信号，控制生成模型将复杂环境的反应动态合成为同步的RGB/点云图序列。 为了方便训练，我们策划了一个名为 Robo4D-200k 的大规模数据集，其中包含 201,426 个具有高质量四维标注的机器人交互片段。 大量实验表明，我们的方法有效地模拟了物理上合理、几何一致且与具体形态无关的交互，忠实地反映了各种真实世界的动态。 它首次展现了潜在的零样本迁移能力，为推进下一代具身模拟提供了高保真的基础。

TLDR: Kinema4D is a new action-conditioned 4D generative robotic simulator leveraging precise kinematic robot control and generative 4D modeling of environmental reactions, trained on a large-scale dataset for improved embodied simulation.

简要说明: Kinema4D 是一种新的动作条件 4D 生成式机器人模拟器，它利用精确的运动学机器人控制和环境反应的生成式 4D 建模，并在大型数据集上进行训练，以改进具身模拟。

Authors: Mutian Xu, Tianbao Zhang, Tianqi Liu, Zhaoxi Chen, Xiaoguang Han, Ziwei Liu

Large Reward Models: Generalizable Online Robot Reward Generation with Vision-Language Models

Abstract (English): Reinforcement Learning (RL) has shown great potential in refining robotic manipulation policies, yet its efficacy remains strongly bottlenecked by the difficulty of designing generalizable reward functions. In this paper, we propose a framework for online policy refinement by adapting foundation VLMs into online reward generators. We develop a robust, scalable reward model based on a state-of-the-art VLM, trained on a large-scale, multi-source dataset encompassing real-world robot trajectories, human-object interactions, and diverse simulated environments. Unlike prior approaches that evaluate entire trajectories post-hoc, our method leverages the VLM to formulate a multifaceted reward signal comprising process, completion, and temporal contrastive rewards based on current visual observations. Initializing with a base policy trained via Imitation Learning (IL), we employ these VLM rewards to guide the model to correct sub-optimal behaviors in a closed-loop manner. We evaluate our framework on challenging long-horizon manipulation benchmarks requiring sequential execution and precise control. Crucially, our reward model operates in a purely zero-shot manner within these test environments. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly improves the success rate of the initial IL policy within just 30 RL iterations, demonstrating remarkable sample efficiency. This empirical evidence highlights that VLM-generated signals can provide reliable feedback to resolve execution errors, effectively eliminating the need for manual reward engineering and facilitating efficient online refinement for robot learning.

摘要（中文）: 强化学习（RL）在改进机器人操作策略方面展现出巨大潜力，但其有效性仍然受到设计具有泛化能力的奖励函数的难度所严重制约。在本文中，我们提出了一种在线策略改进框架，通过将基础视觉语言模型（VLM）适配为在线奖励生成器。我们基于最先进的VLM开发了一个鲁棒、可扩展的奖励模型，该模型在一个大规模、多来源的数据集上进行训练，该数据集包括真实世界的机器人轨迹、人-物交互以及各种模拟环境。与先前事后评估整个轨迹的方法不同，我们的方法利用VLM根据当前视觉观察构建一个多方面的奖励信号，包括过程奖励、完成奖励和时间对比奖励。在通过模仿学习（IL）训练的基础策略初始化后，我们利用这些VLM奖励来引导模型以闭环方式纠正次优行为。我们在需要顺序执行和精确控制的具有挑战性的长程操作基准上评估我们的框架。至关重要的是，我们的奖励模型在这些测试环境中以纯粹的零样本方式运行。实验结果表明，我们的方法仅在30次强化学习迭代中就能显著提高初始IL策略的成功率，表现出卓越的样本效率。该经验证据表明，VLM生成的信号可以提供可靠的反馈来解决执行错误，从而有效地消除了手动奖励工程的需要，并促进了机器人学习的有效在线改进。

TLDR: The paper proposes a framework using Vision-Language Models as online reward generators for robotic manipulation, demonstrating significant improvement in sample efficiency for policy refinement compared to imitation learning alone.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种使用视觉语言模型作为机器人操作在线奖励生成器的框架，与单独的模仿学习相比，在策略改进的样本效率方面表现出显著提升。

Authors: Yanru Wu, Weiduo Yuan, Ang Qi, Vitor Guizilini, Jiageng Mao, Yue Wang

Geometry-Aligned LLM Fine-Tuning for Sequential Narrow-Opening Planning

Abstract (English): We study rigid-body motion planning through multiple sequential narrow openings, which requires long-horizon geometric reasoning because the configuration used to traverse an early opening constrains the set of reachable configurations for subsequent ones. To achieve this, we propose a geometry-aligned large language model (LLM) fine-tuning framework that generates fixed-length, machine-readable waypoint sequences that are both geometrically feasible and coordinated across openings. Our approach uses a bi-level training pipeline. First, we perform failure-driven LoRA supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on human demonstrations, which incorporates structured failure feedback to teach the model common failure modes and enforce the output format. Second, we refine the same LoRA adapters using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with geometric verification: each sampled waypoint sequence is densified by a model-based planner and scored with a deterministic geometry-derived reward to achieve continuous-motion feasibility. To validate the effectiveness of our proposed method, we provide both quantitative and qualitative results from simulations. Our method achieves the highest success rate in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution environments and qualitatively exhibits long-horizon geometric reasoning by selecting exit poses that facilitate entry into subsequent openings.

摘要（中文）: 我们研究通过多个连续狭窄开口的刚体运动规划，这需要长程几何推理，因为用于穿过早期开口的构型约束了后续开口的可达构型集合。为了实现这一点，我们提出了一种几何对齐的大语言模型（LLM）微调框架，该框架生成固定长度的、机器可读的路点序列，这些序列在几何上可行且在不同开口之间协调一致。我们的方法采用双层训练流程。首先，我们对人类演示进行基于失败驱动的LoRA监督微调（SFT），其中包含结构化失败反馈，以教导模型常见的失败模式并强制执行输出格式。其次，我们使用带有几何验证的群组相对策略优化（GRPO）来改进相同的LoRA适配器：每个采样的路点序列都由一个基于模型的规划器进行稠密化，并使用确定性的几何推导奖励进行评分，以实现连续运动可行性。为了验证我们提出的方法的有效性，我们提供了来自模拟的定量和定性结果。我们的方法在同分布和异分布环境中均实现了最高的成功率，并且通过选择有助于进入后续开口的退出姿态，在质量上表现出长程几何推理能力。

TLDR: This paper presents a geometry-aligned LLM fine-tuning framework for rigid-body motion planning through sequential narrow openings, using a bi-level training pipeline involving supervised fine-tuning with failure feedback and group relative policy optimization with geometric verification.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种几何对齐的大型语言模型微调框架，用于通过连续狭窄开口进行刚体运动规划，采用双层训练流程，包括基于失败反馈的监督微调和基于几何验证的群体相对策略优化。

Authors: Al Jaber Mahmud, Xuan Wang

Fast-WAM: Do World Action Models Need Test-time Future Imagination?

Abstract (English): World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for embodied control because they explicitly model how visual observations may evolve under action. Most existing WAMs follow an imagine-then-execute paradigm, incurring substantial test-time latency from iterative video denoising, yet it remains unclear whether explicit future imagination is actually necessary for strong action performance. In this paper, we ask whether WAMs need explicit future imagination at test time, or whether their benefit comes primarily from video modeling during training. We disentangle the role of video modeling during training from explicit future generation during inference by proposing \textbf{Fast-WAM}, a WAM architecture that retains video co-training during training but skips future prediction at test time. We further instantiate several Fast-WAM variants to enable a controlled comparison of these two factors. Across these variants, we find that Fast-WAM remains competitive with imagine-then-execute variants, while removing video co-training causes a much larger performance drop. Empirically, Fast-WAM achieves competitive results with state-of-the-art methods both on simulation benchmarks (LIBERO and RoboTwin) and real-world tasks, without embodied pretraining. It runs in real time with 190ms latency, over 4$\times$ faster than existing imagine-then-execute WAMs. These results suggest that the main value of video prediction in WAMs may lie in improving world representations during training rather than generating future observations at test time. Project page: https://yuantianyuan01.github.io/FastWAM/

摘要（中文）: 世界行为模型（WAM）已成为具身控制领域中视觉-语言-行为（VLA）模型的一种很有前景的替代方案，因为它们明确地建模了视觉观测如何在行动下演变。大多数现有WAM遵循先想象后执行的范式，从迭代视频去噪当中产生大量的测试时延迟，但目前尚不清楚显式的未来想象是否对强大的行动性能而言是真正必要的。在本文中，我们探讨了WAM是否需要在测试时进行显式的未来想象，或者它们的优势是否主要来自训练期间的视频建模。通过提出\textbf{Fast-WAM}，一种在训练期间保留视频协同训练但在测试时跳过未来预测的WAM架构，我们将训练期间的视频建模与推理期间的显式未来生成的作用分开。我们进一步实例化了几种Fast-WAM变体，以便对这两个因素进行受控比较。在这些变体中，我们发现Fast-WAM仍然与先想象后执行的变体具有竞争力，而移除视频协同训练会导致更大的性能下降。在经验上，Fast-WAM在模拟基准测试（LIBERO和RoboTwin）和真实世界任务中都取得了与最先进方法相当的结果，而无需具身预训练。 它的运行时间为190毫秒，实现了实时运行，比现有的先想象后执行的WAM快4倍以上。这些结果表明，视频预测在WAM中的主要价值可能在于提高训练期间的世界表征，而不是在测试时生成未来观测。项目页面：https://yuantianyuan01.github.io/FastWAM/

TLDR: The paper introduces Fast-WAM, a World Action Model architecture that skips future prediction at test time, achieving competitive performance with significantly reduced latency, suggesting that the value of video prediction in WAMs primarily lies in improving world representations during training.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了Fast-WAM，一种在测试阶段跳过未来预测的世界行动模型架构，在大幅降低延迟的情况下实现了具有竞争力的性能，表明视频预测在WAM中的价值主要在于在训练期间改进世界表征。

Authors: Tianyuan Yuan, Zibin Dong, Yicheng Liu, Hang Zhao

Grounding the Score: Explicit Visual Premise Verification for Reliable Vision-Language Process Reward Models

Abstract (English): Vision-language process reward models (VL-PRMs) are increasingly used to score intermediate reasoning steps and rerank candidates under test-time scaling. However, they often function as black-box judges: a low step score may reflect a genuine reasoning mistake or simply the verifier's misperception of the image. This entanglement between perception and reasoning leads to systematic false positives (rewarding hallucinated visual premises) and false negatives (penalizing correct grounded statements), undermining both reranking and error localization. We introduce Explicit Visual Premise Verification (EVPV), a lightweight verification interface that conditions step scoring on the reliability of the visual premises a step depends on. The policy is prompted to produce a step-wise visual checklist that makes required visual facts explicit, while a constraint extractor independently derives structured visual constraints from the input image. EVPV matches checklist claims against these constraints to compute a scalar visual reliability signal, and calibrates PRM step rewards via reliability gating: rewards for visually dependent steps are attenuated when reliability is low and preserved when reliability is high. This decouples perceptual uncertainty from logical evaluation without per-step tool calls. Experiments on VisualProcessBench and six multimodal reasoning benchmarks show that EVPV improves step-level verification and consistently boosts Best-of-N reranking accuracy over strong baselines. Furthermore, injecting controlled corruption into the extracted constraints produces monotonic performance degradation, providing causal evidence that the gains arise from constraint fidelity and explicit premise verification rather than incidental prompt effects. Code is available at: https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/EVPV-PRM

摘要（中文）: 视觉-语言过程奖励模型 (VL-PRM) 越来越多地被用于对中间推理步骤进行评分，并在测试时缩放下对候选答案进行重排序。然而，它们通常充当黑盒判决者：一个较低的步骤得分可能反映了真正的推理错误，或者仅仅是验证者对图像的误解。这种感知与推理的纠缠会导致系统性的假阳性（奖励虚构的视觉前提）和假阴性（惩罚正确的、基于视觉的陈述），从而破坏重排序和错误定位。我们引入了显式视觉前提验证 (EVPV)，这是一种轻量级的验证接口，它将步骤评分建立在该步骤所依赖的视觉前提的可靠性之上。该策略被提示生成一个步骤性的视觉清单，明确所需的视觉事实，同时约束提取器独立地从输入图像中导出结构化的视觉约束。EVPV 将清单声明与这些约束进行匹配，以计算标量视觉可靠性信号，并通过可靠性门控校准 PRM 步骤奖励：当可靠性较低时，视觉依赖步骤的奖励会被衰减；而当可靠性较高时，则会被保留。这解耦了感知不确定性与逻辑评估，无需每一步都进行工具调用。在 VisualProcessBench 和六个多模态推理基准测试上的实验表明，EVPV 提高了步骤级的验证效果，并持续提升了优于强大基线的 Best-of-N 重排序准确率。此外，将受控的损坏注入到提取的约束中会导致性能单调下降，这提供了因果证据，表明增益来自约束的保真度和显式前提验证，而不是偶然的提示效应。代码可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/EVPV-PRM

TLDR: The paper introduces a method called Explicit Visual Premise Verification (EVPV) to improve the reliability of vision-language process reward models by explicitly verifying the visual premises of each step, leading to better reranking performance and error localization.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为显式视觉前提验证 (EVPV) 的方法，通过显式验证每一步的视觉前提，从而提高视觉-语言过程奖励模型的可靠性，进而提高重排序性能和误差定位。

Authors: Junxin Wang, Dai Guan, Weijie Qiu, Zhihang Li, Yongbo Gai, Zhengyi Yang, Mengyu Zhou, Erchao Zhao, Xiaoxi Jiang, Guanjun Jiang

WorldCam: Interactive Autoregressive 3D Gaming Worlds with Camera Pose as a Unifying Geometric Representation

Abstract (English): Recent advances in video diffusion transformers have enabled interactive gaming world models that allow users to explore generated environments over extended horizons. However, existing approaches struggle with precise action control and long-horizon 3D consistency. Most prior works treat user actions as abstract conditioning signals, overlooking the fundamental geometric coupling between actions and the 3D world, whereby actions induce relative camera motions that accumulate into a global camera pose within a 3D world. In this paper, we establish camera pose as a unifying geometric representation to jointly ground immediate action control and long-term 3D consistency. First, we define a physics-based continuous action space and represent user inputs in the Lie algebra to derive precise 6-DoF camera poses, which are injected into the generative model via a camera embedder to ensure accurate action alignment. Second, we use global camera poses as spatial indices to retrieve relevant past observations, enabling geometrically consistent revisiting of locations during long-horizon navigation. To support this research, we introduce a large-scale dataset comprising 3,000 minutes of authentic human gameplay annotated with camera trajectories and textual descriptions. Extensive experiments show that our approach substantially outperforms state-of-the-art interactive gaming world models in action controllability, long-horizon visual quality, and 3D spatial consistency.

摘要（中文）: 视频扩散Transformer的最新进展已经促成了交互式游戏世界模型的出现，该模型允许用户在扩展的视野范围内探索生成环境。然而，现有方法在精确动作控制和长期3D一致性方面面临挑战。大多数先前的工作将用户动作视为抽象的调节信号，忽略了动作和3D世界之间的基本几何耦合关系，即动作会引起相对相机运动，这些运动累积成3D世界中的全局相机位姿。在本文中，我们将相机位姿确立为一个统一的几何表示，以联合实现即时动作控制和长期3D一致性。首先，我们定义了一个基于物理的连续动作空间，并在李代数中表示用户输入，以推导出精确的6自由度相机位姿，这些位姿通过相机嵌入器注入到生成模型中，以确保准确的动作对齐。其次，我们使用全局相机位姿作为空间索引来检索相关的过去观测，从而能够在长期导航过程中几何一致地重新访问位置。为了支持这项研究，我们引入了一个大规模数据集，其中包含3000分钟的真实人类游戏过程，并标注了相机轨迹和文本描述。大量的实验表明，我们的方法在动作可控性、长期视觉质量和3D空间一致性方面显著优于最先进的交互式游戏世界模型。

TLDR: The paper introduces WorldCam, a novel interactive gaming world model leveraging camera pose as a unifying geometric representation for improved action control and 3D consistency in long-horizon navigation. ManiTwin: Scaling Data-Generation-Ready Digital Object Dataset to 100K

Abstract (English): Learning in simulation provides a useful foundation for scaling robotic manipulation capabilities. However, this paradigm often suffers from a lack of data-generation-ready digital assets, in both scale and diversity. In this work, we present ManiTwin, an automated and efficient pipeline for generating data-generation-ready digital object twins. Our pipeline transforms a single image into simulation-ready and semantically annotated 3D asset, enabling large-scale robotic manipulation data generation. Using this pipeline, we construct ManiTwin-100K, a dataset containing 100K high-quality annotated 3D assets. Each asset is equipped with physical properties, language descriptions, functional annotations, and verified manipulation proposals. Experiments demonstrate that ManiTwin provides an efficient asset synthesis and annotation workflow, and that ManiTwin-100K offers high-quality and diverse assets for manipulation data generation, random scene synthesis, and VQA data generation, establishing a strong foundation for scalable simulation data synthesis and policy learning. Our webpage is available at https://manitwin.github.io/.

CABTO: Context-Aware Behavior Tree Grounding for Robot Manipulation

Abstract (English): Behavior Trees (BTs) offer a powerful paradigm for designing modular and reactive robot controllers. BT planning, an emerging field, provides theoretical guarantees for the automated generation of reliable BTs. However, BT planning typically assumes that a well-designed BT system is already grounded -- comprising high-level action models and low-level control policies -- which often requires extensive expert knowledge and manual effort. In this paper, we formalize the BT Grounding problem: the automated construction of a complete and consistent BT system. We analyze its complexity and introduce CABTO (Context-Aware Behavior Tree grOunding), the first framework to efficiently solve this challenge. CABTO leverages pre-trained Large Models (LMs) to heuristically search the space of action models and control policies, guided by contextual feedback from BT planners and environmental observations. Experiments spanning seven task sets across three distinct robotic manipulation scenarios demonstrate CABTO's effectiveness and efficiency in generating complete and consistent behavior tree systems.

TLDR: This paper introduces CABTO, a framework that uses Large Models and context-aware search to automate the grounding of Behavior Trees for robotic manipulation, addressing the challenge of manually constructing complete BT systems.

Authors: Yishuai Cai, Xinglin Chen, Yunxin Mao, Kun Hu, Minglong Li, Yaodong Yang, Yuanpei Chen

DexGrasp-Zero: A Morphology-Aligned Policy for Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Grasping

Abstract (English): To meet the demands of increasingly diverse dexterous hand hardware, it is crucial to develop a policy that enables zero-shot cross-embodiment grasping without redundant re-learning. Cross-embodiment alignment is challenging due to heterogeneous hand kinematics and physical constraints. Existing approaches typically predict intermediate motion targets and retarget them to each embodiment, which may introduce errors and violate embodiment-specific limits, hindering transfer across diverse hands. To overcome these limitations, we propose DexGrasp-Zero, a policy that learns universal grasping skills from diverse embodiments, enabling zero-shot transfer to unseen hands. We first introduce a morphology-aligned graph representation that maps each hand's kinematic keypoints to anatomically grounded nodes and equips each node with tri-axial orthogonal motion primitives, enabling structural and semantic alignment across different morphologies. Relying on this graph-based representation, we design a Morphology-Aligned Graph Convolutional Network (MAGCN) to encode the graph for policy learning. MAGCN incorporates a Physical Property Injection mechanism that fuses hand-specific physical constraints into the graph features, enabling adaptive compensation for varying link lengths and actuation limits for precise and stable grasping. Our extensive simulation evaluations on the YCB dataset demonstrate that our policy, jointly trained on four heterogeneous hands (Allegro, Shadow, Schunk, Ability), achieves an 85% zero-shot success rate on unseen hardware (LEAP, Inspire), outperforming the state-of-the-art method by 59.5%. Real-world experiments further evaluate our policy on three robot platforms (LEAP, Inspire, Revo2), achieving an 82% average success rate on unseen objects.

摘要（中文）: 为满足日益多样化的灵巧手硬件需求，开发一种能够实现零样本跨具身抓取而无需冗余重新学习的策略至关重要。由于异质的手部运动学和物理约束，跨具身对齐极具挑战性。现有方法通常预测中间运动目标并将其重新映射到每个具身，这可能会引入误差并违反特定具身的限制，从而阻碍在不同手部之间的迁移。为克服这些局限性，我们提出了 DexGrasp-Zero，一种从多样化具身学习通用抓取技能的策略，从而支持零样本迁移到未见过的手部。我们首先引入了一种形态对齐的图表示，将每只手的运动学关键点映射到解剖学上相关的节点，并为每个节点配备三轴正交运动基元，从而实现不同形态之间的结构和语义对齐。基于这种基于图的表示，我们设计了一个形态对齐图卷积网络（MAGCN）来编码图以进行策略学习。MAGCN结合了一种物理属性注入机制，将特定于手部的物理约束融合到图特征中，从而能够自适应地补偿不同的连杆长度和驱动限制，以实现精确和稳定的抓取。我们在YCB数据集上的大量模拟评估表明，我们的策略在四个异构手部（Allegro、Shadow、Schunk、Ability）上联合训练，并在未见过的硬件（LEAP、Inspire）上实现了85%的零样本成功率，优于最先进的方法59.5%。真实世界的实验进一步评估了我们的策略在三个机器人平台（LEAP、Inspire、Revo2）上的性能，在未见过的物体上实现了82%的平均成功率。

TLDR: The paper introduces DexGrasp-Zero, a morphology-aligned policy using a graph-based representation and physical property injection for zero-shot cross-embodiment dexterous grasping, achieving high success rates on unseen hands and objects in simulation and real-world experiments.

Authors: Yuliang Wu, Yanhan Lin, WengKit Lao, Yuhao Lin, Yi-Lin Wei, Wei-Shi Zheng, Ancong Wu

vAccSOL: Efficient and Transparent AI Vision Offloading for Mobile Robots

Abstract (English): Mobile robots are increasingly deployed for inspection, patrol, and search-and-rescue operations, relying on computer vision for perception, navigation, and autonomous decision-making. However, executing modern vision workloads onboard is challenging due to limited compute resources and strict energy constraints. While some platforms include embedded accelerators, these are typically tied to proprietary software stacks, leaving user-defined workloads to run on resource-constrained companion computers. We present vAccSOL, a framework for efficient and transparent execution of AI-based vision workloads across heterogeneous robotic and edge platforms. vAccSOL integrates two components: SOL, a neural network compiler that generates optimized inference libraries with minimal runtime dependencies, and vAccel, a lightweight execution framework that transparently dispatches inference locally on the robot or to nearby edge infrastructure. This combination enables hardware-optimized inference and flexible execution placement without requiring modifications to robot applications. We evaluate vAccSOL on a real-world testbed with a commercial quadruped robot and twelve deep learning models covering image classification, video classification, and semantic segmentation. Compared to a PyTorch compiler baseline, SOL achieves comparable or better inference performance. With edge offloading, vAccSOL reduces robot-side power consumption by up to 80% and edge-side power by up to 60% compared to PyTorch, while increasing vision pipeline frame rate by up to 24x, extending the operating lifetime of battery-powered robots.

摘要（中文）: 移动机器人正越来越多地部署于检测、巡逻和搜救行动中，依赖计算机视觉进行感知、导航和自主决策。然而，由于计算资源有限和严格的能量约束，在板载执行现代视觉工作负载极具挑战。尽管一些平台包含嵌入式加速器，但它们通常与专有软件堆栈绑定，使得用户定义的工作负载只能在资源受限的配套计算机上运行。 我们提出了 vAccSOL，一个用于在异构机器人和边缘平台高效透明地执行基于人工智能的视觉工作负载的框架。vAccSOL集成了两个组件：SOL，一个神经网络编译器，用于生成具有最小运行时依赖的优化推理库；以及 vAccel，一个轻量级执行框架，用于透明地将推理分派到机器人本地或附近的边缘基础设施。这种组合实现了硬件优化的推理和灵活的执行位置，无需修改机器人应用程序。 我们在一个真实的测试平台上评估了 vAccSOL，该平台包含一个商业四足机器人和十二个涵盖图像分类、视频分类和语义分割的深度学习模型。与 PyTorch 编译器基线相比，SOL 实现了相当或更好的推理性能。通过边缘卸载，与 PyTorch 相比，vAccSOL 将机器人侧的功耗降低高达 80%，边缘侧的功耗降低高达 60%，同时将视觉管道的帧速率提高高达 24 倍，从而延长了电池供电机器人的运行寿命。

Authors: Adam Zahir, Michele Gucciardom Falk Selker, Anastasios Nanos, Kostis Papazafeiropoulos, Carlos J. Bernardos, Nicolas Weber, Roberto Gonzalez

When Should a Robot Think? Resource-Aware Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning for Embodied Robotic Decision-Making

Abstract (English): Embodied robotic systems increasingly rely on large language model (LLM)-based agents to support high-level reasoning, planning, and decision-making during interactions with the environment. However, invoking LLM reasoning introduces substantial computational latency and resource overhead, which can interrupt action execution and reduce system reliability. Excessive reasoning may delay actions, while insufficient reasoning often leads to incorrect decisions and task failures. This raises a fundamental question for embodied agents: when should the agent reason, and when should it act? In this work, we propose RARRL (Resource-Aware Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning), a hierarchical framework for resource-aware orchestration of embodied agents. Rather than learning low-level control policies, RARRL learns a high-level orchestration policy that operates at the agent's decision-making layer. This policy enables the agent to adaptively determine whether to invoke reasoning, which reasoning role to employ, and how much computational budget to allocate based on current observations, execution history, and remaining resources. Extensive experiments, including evaluations with empirical latency profiles derived from the ALFRED benchmark, show that RARRL consistently improves task success rates while reducing execution latency and enhancing robustness compared with fixed or heuristic reasoning strategies. These results demonstrate that adaptive reasoning control is essential for building reliable and efficient embodied robotic agents.

摘要（中文）: 具身机器人系统越来越依赖于基于大型语言模型（LLM）的智能体，以支持与环境交互过程中的高层推理、规划和决策。然而，调用 LLM 推理会引入大量的计算延迟和资源开销，这会中断行动执行并降低系统可靠性。过度推理可能会延迟行动，而推理不足通常会导致错误的决策和任务失败。这引发了具身智能体的一个根本性问题：智能体应该何时推理，以及何时行动？在这项工作中，我们提出了 RARRL（基于强化学习的资源感知推理），一个用于具身智能体的资源感知编排的分层框架。RARRL 不是学习低层控制策略，而是学习在智能体决策层运行的高层编排策略。该策略使智能体能够根据当前观察、执行历史和剩余资源，自适应地确定是否调用推理、采用何种推理角色以及分配多少计算预算。包括使用从 ALFRED 基准测试中提取的经验延迟配置文件进行的评估在内的大量实验表明，与固定或启发式推理策略相比，RARRL 始终能够提高任务成功率，同时降低执行延迟并增强鲁棒性。这些结果表明，自适应推理控制对于构建可靠高效的具身机器人智能体至关重要。

TLDR: The paper introduces RARRL, a reinforcement learning framework for adaptively deciding when and how to use LLMs for reasoning in embodied robotic agents, aiming to improve task success and reduce latency.

简要说明: 本文提出了RARRL，一个基于强化学习的框架，用于自适应地决定何时以及如何使用LLM进行具身机器人智能体的推理，旨在提高任务成功率并降低延迟。

Authors: Jun Liu, Pu Zhao, Zhenglun Kong, Xuan Shen, Peiyan Dong, Fan Yang, Lin Cui, Hao Tang, Geng Yuan, Wei Niu, Wenbin Zhang, Xue Lin, Gaowen Liu, Yanzhi Wang, Dong Huang

Conservative Offline Robot Policy Learning via Posterior-Transition Reweighting

Abstract (English): Offline post-training adapts a pretrained robot policy to a target dataset by supervised regression on recorded actions. In practice, robot datasets are heterogeneous: they mix embodiments, camera setups, and demonstrations of varying quality, so many trajectories reflect recovery behavior, inconsistent operator skill, or weakly informative supervision. Uniform post-training gives equal credit to all samples and can therefore average over conflicting or low-attribution data. We propose Posterior-Transition Reweighting (PTR), a reward-free and conservative post-training method that decides how much each training sample should influence the supervised update. For each sample, PTR encodes the observed post-action consequence as a latent target, inserts it into a candidate pool of mismatched targets, and uses a separate transition scorer to estimate a softmax identification posterior over target indices. The posterior-to-uniform ratio defines the PTR score, which is converted into a clipped-and-mixed weight and applied to the original action objective through self-normalized weighted regression. This construction requires no tractable policy likelihood and is compatible with both diffusion and flow-matching action heads. Rather than uniformly trusting all recorded supervision, PTR reallocates credit according to how attributable each sample's post-action consequence is under the current representation, improving conservative offline adaptation to heterogeneous robot data.

摘要（中文）: 离线后训练通过对记录的动作进行监督回归，使预训练的机器人策略适应目标数据集。 在实践中，机器人数据集是异构的：它们混合了不同的机器人结构、相机设置以及质量各异的演示，因此很多轨迹反映了恢复行为、操作员技能的不一致或信息量较弱的监督。 均匀的后训练给予所有样本同等的权重，因此可能平均处理冲突或低归因数据。 我们提出后验-转移重加权 (PTR)，一种无奖励且保守的后训练方法，用于决定每个训练样本应该对监督更新产生多大的影响。 对于每个样本，PTR 将观察到的动作后结果编码为一个潜在目标，将其插入到不匹配目标的一个候选池中，并使用一个单独的转移评分器来估计目标索引上的 softmax 识别后验概率。 后验概率与均匀分布的比率定义了 PTR 分数，该分数被转换为裁剪和混合的权重，并通过自归一化加权回归应用于原始动作目标。 这种构造不需要可追踪的策略似然性，并且与扩散和流匹配动作头兼容。 PTR 不是均匀地信任所有记录的监督，而是根据当前表示下每个样本的动作后结果的可归因程度重新分配权重，从而改进了对异构机器人数据的保守离线适应。

TLDR: The paper introduces Posterior-Transition Reweighting (PTR), a reward-free offline robot policy post-training method that addresses the heterogeneity of robot datasets by reweighting training samples based on the attribution of their post-action consequences, improving conservative offline adaptation.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为后验-转移重加权（PTR）的免奖励离线机器人策略后训练方法，该方法通过基于动作后结果的归因来重新加权训练样本，从而解决机器人数据集的异构性问题，并改进保守的离线适应。

Authors: Wanpeng Zhang, Hao Luo, Sipeng Zheng, Yicheng Feng, Haiweng Xu, Ziheng Xi, Chaoyi Xu, Haoqi Yuan, Zongqing Lu

Kamino: GPU-based Massively Parallel Simulation of Multi-Body Systems with Challenging Topologies

Abstract (English): We present Kamino, a GPU-based physics solver for massively parallel simulations of heterogeneous highly-coupled mechanical systems. Implemented in Python using NVIDIA Warp and integrated into the Newton framework, it enables the application of data-driven methods, such as large-scale reinforcement learning, to complex robotic systems that exhibit strongly coupled kinematic and dynamic constraints such as kinematic loops. The latter are often circumvented by practitioners; approximating the system topology as a kinematic tree and incorporating explicit loop-closure constraints or so-called mimic joints. Kamino aims at alleviating this burden by natively supporting these types of coupling. This capability facilitates high-throughput parallelized simulations that capture the true nature of mechanical systems that exploit closed kinematic chains for mechanical advantage. Moreover, Kamino supports heterogeneous worlds, allowing for batched simulation of structurally diverse robots on a single GPU. At its core lies a state-of-the-art constrained optimization algorithm that computes constraint forces by solving the constrained rigid multi-body forward dynamics transcribed as a nonlinear complementarity problem. This leads to high-fidelity simulations that can resolve contact dynamics without resorting to approximate models that simplify and/or convexify the problem. We demonstrate RL policy training on DR Legs, a biped with six nested kinematic loops, generating a feasible walking policy while simulating 4096 parallel environments on a single GPU.

摘要（中文）: 我们提出了Kamino，一个基于GPU的物理求解器，用于大规模并行仿真异构且高度耦合的机械系统。Kamino使用NVIDIA Warp在Python中实现，并集成到Newton框架中，它能够将数据驱动方法（例如大规模强化学习）应用于复杂的机器人系统，这些系统表现出强耦合的运动学和动力学约束，例如运动学环。从业者通常会规避后者，将系统拓扑近似为运动学树，并结合显式闭环约束或所谓的模仿关节。Kamino旨在通过原生支持这些类型的耦合来减轻这种负担。这种能力有助于进行高吞吐量的并行仿真，从而捕捉利用闭合运动链以获得机械优势的机械系统的真实特性。此外，Kamino支持异构世界，允许在单个GPU上批量仿真结构不同的机器人。其核心是先进的约束优化算法，该算法通过求解转录为非线性互补问题的约束刚体多体正向动力学来计算约束力。这能够实现高保真度的仿真，无需采用简化和/或凸化问题的近似模型即可解析接触动力学。我们展示了在DR Legs（一种具有六个嵌套运动学环的双足机器人）上训练强化学习策略，在单个GPU上仿真4096个并行环境的同时，生成了一个可行的行走策略。

TLDR: The paper introduces Kamino, a GPU-based physics solver that enables high-throughput reinforcement learning on complex robots with kinematic loops by natively supporting them through a constrained optimization algorithm, demonstrated on a biped robot simulation.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了Kamino，一个基于GPU的物理求解器，可以通过其约束优化算法原生支持包含运动链的复杂机器人，从而实现高吞吐量的强化学习。论文在一个双足机器人的模拟中展示了该求解器的应用。

Authors: Vassilios Tsounis, Guirec Maloisel, Christian Schumacher, Ruben Grandia, Agon Serifi, David Müller, Chris Amevor, Tobias Widmer, Moritz Bächer

Rewarding DINO: Predicting Dense Rewards with Vision Foundation Models

Abstract (English): Well-designed dense reward functions in robot manipulation not only indicate whether a task is completed but also encode progress along the way. Generally, designing dense rewards is challenging and usually requires access to privileged state information available only in simulation, not in real-world experiments. This makes reward prediction models that infer task state information from camera images attractive. A common approach is to predict rewards from expert demonstrations based on visual similarity or sequential frame ordering. However, this biases the resulting reward function towards a specific solution and leaves it undefined in states not covered by the demonstrations. In this work, we introduce Rewarding DINO, a method for language-conditioned reward modeling that learns actual reward functions rather than specific trajectories. The model's compact size allows it to serve as a direct replacement for analytical reward functions with comparatively low computational overhead. We train our model on data sampled from 24 Meta-World+ tasks using a rank-based loss and evaluate pairwise accuracy, rank correlation, and calibration. Rewarding DINO achieves competitive performance in tasks from the training set and generalizes to new settings in simulation and the real world, indicating that it learns task semantics. We also test the model with off-the-shelf reinforcement learning algorithms to solve tasks from our Meta-World+ training set.

摘要（中文）: 机器人操作中精心设计的密集奖励函数不仅指示任务是否完成，还对过程中的进展进行编码。 通常，设计密集奖励函数具有挑战性，并且通常需要访问只有在模拟中才可获得的特权状态信息，而不是在真实世界的实验中。 这使得从相机图像推断任务状态信息的奖励预测模型具有吸引力。 一种常见的方法是基于视觉相似性或顺序帧排序，从专家演示中预测奖励。 然而，这会使生成的奖励函数偏向于特定的解决方案，并且在演示未覆盖的状态下未定义。 在这项工作中，我们引入了 Rewarding DINO，一种语言条件奖励建模方法，它学习实际的奖励函数，而不是特定的轨迹。 该模型的体积小巧，使其能够直接替代分析奖励函数，且计算开销相对较低。 我们使用基于排序的损失在从 24 个 Meta-World+ 任务中采样的数据上训练我们的模型，并评估成对准确率、秩相关性和校准。 Rewarding DINO 在训练集中的任务中实现了具有竞争力的性能，并推广到模拟和真实世界中的新设置，表明它学习了任务语义。 我们还使用现成的强化学习算法测试该模型，以解决来自我们 Meta-World+ 训练集的任务。

TLDR: The paper introduces Rewarding DINO, a language-conditioned reward modeling method for robot manipulation that learns transferable reward functions from visual data, trained with rank-based loss, demonstrating good generalization and performance.

简要说明: 本文介绍了Rewarding DINO，一种用于机器人操作的语言条件奖励建模方法，它从视觉数据中学习可转移的奖励函数，使用基于排序的损失进行训练，展示了良好的泛化能力和性能。

Authors: Pierre Krack, Tobias Jülg, Wolfram Burgard, Florian Walter

Encoding Predictability and Legibility for Style-Conditioned Diffusion Policy

Abstract (English): Striking a balance between efficiency and transparent motion is a core challenge in human-robot collaboration, as highly expressive movements often incur unnecessary time and energy costs. In collaborative environments, legibility allows a human observer a better understanding of the robot's actions, increasing safety and trust. However, these behaviors result in sub-optimal and exaggerated trajectories that are redundant in low-ambiguity scenarios where the robot's goal is already obvious. To address this trade-off, we propose Style-Conditioned Diffusion Policy (SCDP), a modular framework that constrains the trajectory generation of a pre-trained diffusion model toward either legibility or efficiency based on the environment's configuration. Our method utilizes a post-training pipeline that freezes the base policy and trains a lightweight scene encoder and conditioning predictor to modulate the diffusion process. At inference time, an ambiguity detection module activates the appropriate conditioning, prioritizing expressive motion only for ambiguous goals and reverting to efficient paths otherwise. We evaluate SCDP on manipulation and navigation tasks, and results show that it enhances legibility in ambiguous settings while preserving optimal efficiency when legibility is unnecessary, all without retraining the base policy.

摘要（中文）: 在人机协作中，如何在效率和透明运动之间取得平衡是一个核心挑战，因为富有表现力的动作往往会带来不必要的时间和能量消耗。在协作环境中，易读性使人类观察者更好地理解机器人的行为，从而提高安全性和信任度。然而，这些行为会导致次优和夸张的轨迹，这些轨迹在机器人目标已经很明确的低歧义场景中是冗余的。为了解决这个权衡问题，我们提出了风格条件扩散策略（SCDP），这是一个模块化框架，它根据环境配置，约束预训练扩散模型的轨迹生成向易读性或效率方向倾斜。我们的方法利用了一个后训练流程，该流程冻结了基础策略，并训练了一个轻量级的场景编码器和条件预测器来调节扩散过程。在推理时，歧义检测模块会激活适当的条件，仅在目标不明确时优先考虑表现力强的运动，否则恢复到高效路径。我们在操作和导航任务上评估了SCDP，结果表明，它在歧义环境中提高了易读性，同时在不需要易读性时保持了最佳效率，所有这些都不需要重新训练基础策略。

TLDR: This paper introduces Style-Conditioned Diffusion Policy (SCDP) for robots, which balances legibility and efficiency in trajectory generation by modulating a pre-trained diffusion model based on environmental ambiguity, enhancing human-robot collaboration.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了风格条件扩散策略（SCDP），该策略通过根据环境模糊度调整预训练的扩散模型，平衡了机器人轨迹生成中的可读性和效率，从而加强了人机协作。

Authors: Adrien Jacquet Crétides, Mouad Abrini, Hamed Rahimi, Mohamed Chetouani

OGScene3D: Incremental Open-Vocabulary 3D Gaussian Scene Graph Mapping for Scene Understanding

Abstract (English): Open-vocabulary scene understanding is crucial for robotic applications, enabling robots to comprehend complex 3D environmental contexts and supporting various downstream tasks such as navigation and manipulation. However, existing methods require pre-built complete 3D semantic maps to construct scene graphs for scene understanding, which limits their applicability in robotic scenarios where environments are explored incrementally. To address this challenge, we propose OGScene3D, an open-vocabulary scene understanding system that achieves accurate 3D semantic mapping and scene graph construction incrementally. Our system employs a confidence-based Gaussian semantic representation that jointly models semantic predictions and their reliability, enabling robust scene modeling. Building on this representation, we introduce a hierarchical 3D semantic optimization strategy that achieves semantic consistency through local correspondence establishment and global refinement, thereby constructing globally consistent semantic maps. Moreover, we design a long-term global optimization method that leverages temporal memory of historical observations to enhance semantic predictions. By integrating 2D-3D semantic consistency with Gaussian rendering contribution, this method continuously refines the semantic understanding of the entire scene. Furthermore, we develop a progressive graph construction approach that dynamically creates and updates both nodes and semantic relationships, allowing continuous updating of the 3D scene graphs. Extensive experiments on widely used datasets and real-world scenes demonstrate the effectiveness of our OGScene3D on open-vocabulary scene understanding.

摘要（中文）: 开放词汇场景理解对于机器人应用至关重要，它使机器人能够理解复杂的3D环境上下文，并支持各种下游任务，如导航和操作。然而，现有方法需要预先构建完整的3D语义地图来构建场景图以用于场景理解，这限制了它们在机器人环境中（机器人以增量方式探索环境）的应用。 为了解决这个挑战，我们提出OGScene3D，一个开放词汇场景理解系统，它可以增量地实现精确的3D语义建图和场景图构建。 我们的系统采用基于置信度的 Gaussian 语义表示，它联合建模语义预测及其可信度，从而实现鲁棒的场景建模。 在此表示的基础上，我们引入了一种分层3D语义优化策略，通过局部对应关系建立和全局细化来实现语义一致性，从而构建全局一致的语义地图。 此外，我们设计了一种长期全局优化方法，该方法利用历史观测的时间记忆来增强语义预测。 通过将2D-3D语义一致性与 Gaussian 渲染贡献相结合，该方法不断改进对整个场景的语义理解。 更进一步，我们开发了一种渐进式图构建方法，该方法动态创建和更新节点及语义关系，从而允许3D场景图的持续更新。 在广泛使用的数据集和真实场景中进行的大量实验表明，我们的 OGScene3D 在开放词汇场景理解方面的有效性。

TLDR: The paper introduces OGScene3D, a system for incremental open-vocabulary 3D scene graph mapping that uses a confidence-based Gaussian representation and hierarchical optimization to build robust and consistent semantic maps, which is useful for robotics in dynamically explored environments.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了OGScene3D，一个用于增量式开放词汇3D场景图映射的系统。该系统使用基于置信度的Gaussian表示和分层优化，以构建鲁棒和一致的语义地图，这对于在动态探索环境中运行的机器人非常有用。

Authors: Siting Zhu, Ziyun Lu, Guangming Wang, Chenguang Huang, Yongbo Chen, I-Ming Chen, Wolfram Burgard, Hesheng Wang

Enabling Dynamic Tracking in Vision-Language-Action Models via Time-Discrete and Time-Continuous Velocity Feedforward

Abstract (English): While vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown great promise for robot manipulation, their deployment on rigid industrial robots remains challenging due to the inherent trade-off between compliance and responsiveness. Standard Behavior Cloning (BC) approaches predict discrete poses at low frequencies, omitting the velocity and acceleration feedforward terms typically used by low-level compliant controllers. This requires to rely on high stiffness for accurate tracking, thereby sacrificing safe contact dynamics. In this paper, we demonstrate the importance of integrating velocity feedforward terms into VLA policies to resolve this trade-off. We propose two methods for extracting velocity targets from VLAs: a time-discrete finite-difference approximation that serves as a highly effective bridge for existing models, and a continuous Cubic B-Spline action space that natively yields $C^2$ continuous trajectories for high-frequency control. Crucially, both approaches are strictly model-agnostic and compatible with any standard action-chunking architecture, requiring modifications only to teleoperation, data processing, and the low-level controller. We fine-tune the $π_{0.5}$ model and evaluate both of our approaches on a demanding, contact-rich cube-in-hole task. Our results indicate that incorporating the velocity feedforward term via finite differences significantly improves task execution speed, while the continuous B-Spline approach maintains high overall success rates and provides a foundation for smoother higher-order derivatives without compromising compliance.

摘要（中文）: 虽然视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型在机器人操作方面展现出巨大潜力，但由于顺应性和响应性之间固有的权衡，将其部署在刚性工业机器人上仍然具有挑战性。标准行为克隆 (BC) 方法以低频率预测离散姿态，忽略了低级顺应性控制器通常使用的速度和加速度前馈项。这需要依赖高刚度来实现精确跟踪，从而牺牲了安全的接触动力学。在本文中，我们证明了将速度前馈项集成到VLA策略中的重要性，以解决这种权衡。我们提出了两种从VLA中提取速度目标的方法：一种是时间离散有限差分近似，可以作为现有模型的高效桥梁；另一种是连续三次B样条动作空间，可以原生产生 $C^2$ 连续轨迹，以实现高频控制。至关重要的是，这两种方法都严格与模型无关，并且与任何标准动作分块架构兼容，只需要对遥操作、数据处理和低级控制器进行修改。我们对 $π_{0.5}$ 模型进行了微调，并在一个要求苛刻、接触丰富的魔方入孔任务中评估了我们的两种方法。结果表明，通过有限差分合并速度前馈项可显著提高任务执行速度，而连续B样条方法保持了较高的总体成功率，并为更平滑的高阶导数提供了基础，同时不会损害顺应性。

TLDR: This paper introduces two model-agnostic methods for incorporating velocity feedforward into Vision-Language-Action models to improve robot manipulation in contact-rich tasks, addressing the compliance vs. responsiveness trade-off.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了两种模型无关的方法，将速度前馈整合到视觉-语言-动作模型中，以改善机器人操作在具有丰富接触的任务中的表现，解决了顺应性和响应性之间的权衡问题。

Authors: Johannes Hechtl, Philipp Schmitt, Georg von Wichert, Wolfram Burgard

S-VAM: Shortcut Video-Action Model by Self-Distilling Geometric and Semantic Foresight

Abstract (English): Video action models (VAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot learning, owing to their powerful visual foresight for complex manipulation tasks. However, current VAMs, typically relying on either slow multi-step video generation or noisy one-step feature extraction, cannot simultaneously guarantee real-time inference and high-fidelity foresight. To address this limitation, we propose S-VAM, a shortcut video-action model that foresees coherent geometric and semantic representations via a single forward pass. Serving as a stable blueprint, these foreseen representations significantly simplify the action prediction. To enable this efficient shortcut, we introduce a novel self-distillation strategy that condenses structured generative priors of multi-step denoising into one-step inference. Specifically, vision foundation model (VFM) representations extracted from the diffusion model's own multi-step generated videos provide teacher targets. Lightweight decouplers, as students, learn to directly map noisy one-step features to these targets. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate that our S-VAM outperforms state-of-the-art methods, enabling efficient and precise manipulation in complex environments. Our project page is https://haodong-yan.github.io/S-VAM/

摘要（中文）: 视频动作模型（VAMs）因其对复杂操控任务强大的视觉预测能力，已成为一种有前景的机器人学习范式。然而，当前的VAMs通常依赖于缓慢的多步视频生成或嘈杂的单步特征提取，无法同时保证实时推理和高保真预测。为了解决这个局限性，我们提出了S-VAM，一种通过单次前向传播预测连贯的几何和语义表示的捷径视频-动作模型。这些预测的表示作为稳定的蓝图，显著简化了动作预测。为了实现这种高效的捷径，我们引入了一种新颖的自蒸馏策略，将多步去噪的结构化生成先验提炼到单步推理中。具体来说，从扩散模型自身的多步生成视频中提取的视觉基础模型（VFM）表示提供了教师目标。轻量级的解耦器作为学生，学习直接将嘈杂的单步特征映射到这些目标。在模拟和真实世界中进行的大量实验表明，我们的S-VAM优于最先进的方法，能够在复杂环境中实现高效和精确的操控。我们的项目主页是https://haodong-yan.github.io/S-VAM/

TLDR: The paper introduces S-VAM, a shortcut video-action model that efficiently predicts geometric and semantic representations for robot manipulation by distilling multi-step diffusion model priors into a one-step inference process, achieving real-time performance and high-fidelity foresight.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为S-VAM的快捷视频动作模型，通过将多步扩散模型的先验知识提炼成单步推理过程，高效地预测机器人操作的几何和语义表示，从而实现实时性能和高保真度的预测。

Authors: Haodong Yan, Zhide Zhong, Jiaguan Zhu, Junjie He, Weilin Yuan, Wenxuan Song, Xin Gong, Yingjie Cai, Guanyi Zhao, Xu Yan, Bingbing Liu, Ying-Cong Chen, Haoang Li

SignNav: Leveraging Signage for Semantic Visual Navigation in Large-Scale Indoor Environments

Abstract (English): Humans routinely leverage semantic hints provided by signage to navigate to destinations within novel Large-Scale Indoor (LSI) environments, such as hospitals and airport terminals. However, this capability remains underexplored within the field of embodied navigation. This paper introduces a novel embodied navigation task, SignNav, which requires the agent to interpret semantic hint from signage and reason about the subsequent action based on current observation. To facilitate research in this domain, we construct the LSI-Dataset for the training and evaluation of various SignNav agents. Dynamically changing semantic hints and sparse placement of signage in LSI environments present significant challenges to the SignNav task. To address these challenges, we propose the Spatial-Temporal Aware Transformer (START) model for end-to-end decision-making. The spatial-aware module grounds the semantic hint of signage into physical world, while the temporal-aware module captures long-range dependencies between historical states and current observation. Leveraging a two-stage training strategy with Dataset Aggregation (DAgger), our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, recording an 80% Success Rate (SR) and 0.74 NDTW on val-unseen split. Real-world deployment further demonstrates the practicality of our method in physical environment without pre-built map.

摘要（中文）: 人类通常会利用标识提供的语义提示，在新颖的大规模室内（LSI）环境中导航至目的地，例如医院和机场航站楼。然而，这种能力在具身导航领域尚未得到充分探索。本文介绍了一种新的具身导航任务SignNav，该任务要求智能体从标识中解读语义提示，并根据当前观测结果推断后续行动。为了促进该领域的研究，我们构建了LSI数据集，用于训练和评估各种SignNav智能体。LSI环境中动态变化的语义提示和稀疏的标识放置给SignNav任务带来了重大挑战。为了应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种用于端到端决策的空间-时间感知Transformer（START）模型。空间感知模块将标识的语义提示锚定到物理世界，而时间感知模块则捕捉历史状态和当前观测之间的长程依赖关系。通过采用带有数据集聚合（DAgger）的两阶段训练策略，我们的方法实现了最先进的性能，在val-unseen数据集分割上记录了80%的成功率（SR）和0.74的NDTW。真实世界的部署进一步证明了我们的方法在没有预构建地图的物理环境中的实用性。

TLDR: This paper introduces the SignNav task, where an agent must navigate large indoor environments using semantic information from signage, along with a dataset and a Spatial-Temporal Aware Transformer (START) model that achieves state-of-the-art performance.

简要说明: 这篇论文介绍了SignNav任务，要求智能体利用标识牌的语义信息在大型室内环境中导航。同时，论文还提供了一个数据集和一个名为Spatial-Temporal Aware Transformer (START)的模型，该模型实现了最先进的性能。

Authors: Jian Sun, Yuming Huang, He Li, Shuqi Xiao, Shenyan Guo, Maani Ghaffari, Qingbiao Li, Chengzhong Xu, Hui Kong

Towards the Vision-Sound-Language-Action Paradigm: The HEAR Framework for Sound-Centric Manipulation

Abstract (English): While recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have begun to incorporate audio, they typically treat sound as static pre-execution prompts or focus exclusively on human speech. This leaves a significant gap in real-time, sound-centric manipulation where fleeting environmental acoustics provide critical state verification during task execution. Consequently, key sounds are easily missed due to low-frequency updates or system latency. This problem is exacerbated by action chunking with open-loop execution, which creates a Blind Execution Interval where acoustic events are lost between discrete audio observation windows. Recognizing the necessity of continuous auditory awareness, we formalize Vision-Sound-Language-Action (VSLA) as a continuous control paradigm conditioned on vision, streaming audio, language, and proprioception under delayed decision loops. As an instantiation, we introduce HEAR, a VSLA framework integrating four components: (i) a streaming Historizer to maintain a compact, causal audio context across execution gaps; (ii) an Envisioner adapted from omni foundation models to reason over multi-sensory inputs; (iii) an Advancer, formulated as an audio world model, to learn temporal dynamics by predicting near-future audio codes; and (iv) a flow-matching Realizer policy to generate smooth action chunks. To address the scarcity of pretraining data and evaluations for VSLA, we construct OpenX-Sound for pretraining, alongside HEAR-Bench, the first sound-centric manipulation benchmark with strict causal timing rules. Our results suggest that robust sound-centric manipulation necessitates causal persistence and explicit temporal learning. This framework provides a practical step toward multi-sensory foundation models for embodied agents, enabling robots to perceive and interact with dynamic environments. Code and videos are available at https://hear.irmv.top.

摘要（中文）: 尽管近期的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型已开始融入音频，但它们通常将声音视为静态的预执行提示，或仅专注于人类语音。这在实时、以声音为中心的操作中留下了一个显著的空白，即在此类操作中，转瞬即逝的环境声学信息为任务执行期间的关键状态验证提供了依据。因此，由于低频更新或系统延迟，关键声音很容易被错过。在采用开放环路执行的动作分块后，此问题会进一步加剧，从而产生盲执行间隔，导致声音事件在离散音频观察窗口之间丢失。认识到持续听觉感知的必要性，我们将视觉-声音-语言-动作（VSLA）形式化为一种连续控制范式，该范式以视觉、流式音频、语言和本体感觉为条件，在延迟决策循环下运行。作为一种实例化，我们引入了HEAR，一个集成四个组件的VSLA框架：（i）一个流式记录器（Historizer），用于在执行间隔中保持一个紧凑的、因果的音频上下文；（ii）一个改编自全能基础模型的可视器（Envisioner），用于推理多感觉输入；（iii）一个推进器（Advancer），被形式化为一个音频世界模型，通过预测近未来音频编码来学习时间动态；以及（iv）一个流匹配实现器（Realizer）策略，用于生成平滑的动作块。为了解决VSLA的预训练数据和评估的稀缺性问题，我们构建了用于预训练的OpenX-Sound，以及HEAR-Bench，这是第一个具有严格因果时序规则的以声音为中心的操作基准。我们的结果表明，鲁棒的以声音为中心的操作需要因果持久性和显式的时间学习。该框架为具身智能体的多感觉基础模型提供了一个实际步骤，使机器人能够感知动态环境并与之交互。代码和视频见https://hear.irmv.top。

TLDR: This paper introduces HEAR, a framework for Vision-Sound-Language-Action (VSLA) that emphasizes continuous auditory awareness in robotic manipulation, along with a new benchmark (HEAR-Bench) and pretraining dataset (OpenX-Sound) to address the lack of training data in this space.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了HEAR，一个视觉-声音-语言-动作（VSLA）框架，强调机器人操作中持续的声音感知，并提出了一个新的基准(HEAR-Bench)和预训练数据集(OpenX-Sound)，以解决该领域缺乏训练数据的问题。

Authors: Chang Nie, Tianchen Deng, Guangming Wang, Zhe Liu, Hesheng Wang

The Era of End-to-End Autonomy: Transitioning from Rule-Based Driving to Large Driving Models

Abstract (English): Autonomous driving is undergoing a shift from modular rule based pipelines toward end to end (E2E) learning systems. This paper examines this transition by tracing the evolution from classical sense perceive plan control architectures to large driving models (LDMs) capable of mapping raw sensor input directly to driving actions. We analyze recent developments including Tesla's Full Self Driving (FSD) V12 V14, Rivian's Unified Intelligence platform, NVIDIA Cosmos, and emerging commercial robotaxi deployments, focusing on architectural design, deployment strategies, safety considerations and industry implications. A key emerging product category is supervised E2E driving, often referred to as FSD (Supervised) or L2 plus plus, which several manufacturers plan to deploy from 2026 onwards. These systems can perform most of the Dynamic Driving Task (DDT) in complex environments while requiring human supervision, shifting the driver's role to safety oversight. Early operational evidence suggests E2E learning handles the long tail distribution of real world driving scenarios and is becoming a dominant commercial strategy. We also discuss how similar architectural advances may extend beyond autonomous vehicles (AV) to other embodied AI systems, including humanoid robotics.

摘要（中文）: 自动驾驶正经历从模块化规则管线向端到端(E2E)学习系统的转变。本文通过追溯从经典感知-规划-控制架构到能够直接将原始传感器输入映射到驾驶行为的大型驾驶模型(LDM)的演变来考察这一过程。我们分析了近期的发展，包括特斯拉的完全自动驾驶(FSD) V12 V14、Rivian的统一智能平台、NVIDIA Cosmos以及新兴的商业化无人出租车部署，重点关注架构设计、部署策略、安全考量和行业影响。一个关键的新兴产品类别是受监督的E2E驾驶，通常被称为FSD（受监督）或L2++，多个制造商计划从2026年起部署该系统。这些系统能够在复杂环境中执行大部分动态驾驶任务(DDT)，同时需要人类监督，从而将驾驶员的角色转变为安全监督。早期运营证据表明，E2E学习能够处理真实驾驶场景的长尾分布，并正成为一种主要的商业策略。我们还讨论了类似的架构进步如何扩展到自动驾驶汽车(AV)之外的其他具身人工智能系统，包括人形机器人。

TLDR: The paper reviews the shift from rule-based autonomous driving pipelines to end-to-end learning systems and analyzes the architectural designs, deployment strategies, safety considerations, and industry implications of emerging Large Driving Models, including supervised E2E driving systems.

简要说明: 本文回顾了自动驾驶从基于规则的流水线向端到端学习系统的转变，并分析了新兴的大型驾驶模型的架构设计、部署策略、安全考虑以及行业影响，包括监督式端到端驾驶系统。

Authors: Eduardo Nebot, Julie Stephany Berrio Perez

FlatLands: Generative Floormap Completion From a Single Egocentric View

Abstract (English): A single egocentric image typically captures only a small portion of the floor, yet a complete metric traversability map of the surroundings would better serve applications such as indoor navigation. We introduce FlatLands, a dataset and benchmark for single-view bird's-eye view (BEV) floor completion. The dataset contains 270,575 observations from 17,656 real metric indoor scenes drawn from six existing datasets, with aligned observation, visibility, validity, and ground-truth BEV maps, and the benchmark includes both in- and out-of-distribution evaluation protocols. We compare training-free approaches, deterministic models, ensembles, and stochastic generative models. Finally, we instantiate the task as an end-to-end monocular RGB-to-floormaps pipeline. FlatLands provides a rigorous testbed for uncertainty-aware indoor mapping and generative completion for embodied navigation.

摘要（中文）: 单个第一人称图像通常仅捕获地板的一小部分，然而，周围环境的完整度量可通行性地图将更好地服务于室内导航等应用。我们推出了FlatLands，一个用于单视图鸟瞰图（BEV）楼层补全的数据集和基准。该数据集包含来自六个现有数据集的17,656个真实度量室内场景的270,575个观测，其中包括对齐的观测、可见性、有效性和地面真值BEV地图，并且该基准包含分布内和分布外的评估协议。我们比较了免训练方法、确定性模型、集成模型和随机生成模型。最后，我们将该任务实例化为端到端单目RGB到楼层地图的流水线。FlatLands为具身导航的不确定性感知室内地图绘制和生成式补全提供了一个严格的测试平台。

TLDR: The paper introduces FlatLands, a new dataset and benchmark for single-view bird's-eye view floorplan completion, providing a testbed for uncertainty-aware indoor mapping and generative completion relevant to embodied navigation.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了FlatLands，一个新的数据集和基准，用于单视角鸟瞰图楼层平面图补全，为不确定性感知室内地图绘制和与具身导航相关的生成补全提供了一个测试平台。

Authors: Subhransu S. Bhattacharjee, Dylan Campbell, Rahul Shome

Safety Case Patterns for VLA-based driving systems: Insights from SimLingo

Abstract (English): Vision-Language-Action (VLA)-based driving systems represent a significant paradigm shift in autonomous driving since, by combining traffic scene understanding, linguistic interpretation, and action generation, these systems enable more flexible, adaptive, and instruction-responsive driving behaviors. However, despite their growing adoption and potential to support socially responsible autonomous driving as well as understanding high-level human instructions, VLA-based driving systems may exhibit new types of hazardous behaviors. For instance, the integration of open-ended natural language inputs (e.g., user or navigation instructions) into the multimodal control loop, may lead to unpredictable and unsafe behaviors that could endanger vehicle occupants and pedestrians. Hence, assuring the safety of these systems is crucial to help build trust in their operations. To support this, we propose a novel safety case design approach called RAISE. Our approach introduces novel patterns tailored to instruction-based driving systems such as VLA-based driving systems, an extension of Hazard Analysis and Risk Assessment (HARA) detailing safe scenarios and their outcomes, and a design technique to create the safety cases of VLA-based driving systems. A case study on SimLingo illustrates how our approach can be used to construct rigorous, evidence-based safety claims for this emerging class of autonomous driving systems.

摘要（中文）: 基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）的驾驶系统代表了自动驾驶领域的一次重大范式转变，通过结合交通场景理解、语言解释和动作生成，这些系统能够实现更灵活、自适应和响应指令的驾驶行为。 然而，尽管它们的应用日益广泛，并且具有支持对社会负责的自动驾驶以及理解高级人类指令的潜力，但基于VLA的驾驶系统也可能表现出新型的危险行为。例如，将开放式的自然语言输入（例如，用户或导航指令）集成到多模态控制环路中，可能会导致不可预测和不安全的行为，从而危及车辆乘员和行人。 因此，确保这些系统的安全性对于帮助建立对其操作的信任至关重要。 为此，我们提出了一种名为RAISE的全新安全案例设计方法。 我们的方法引入了专为基于指令的驾驶系统（如基于VLA的驾驶系统）量身定制的新模式，对危害分析和风险评估（HARA）进行扩展，详细说明安全场景及其结果，以及一种用于创建VLA驾驶系统安全案例的设计技术。 一个关于SimLingo的案例研究说明了我们的方法如何被用于为这类新兴的自动驾驶系统构建严谨的、基于证据的安全声明。

TLDR: This paper proposes a novel safety case design approach (RAISE) with tailored patterns for Vision-Language-Action (VLA)-based driving systems, aiming to ensure their safety by addressing potential hazards arising from natural language inputs.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种新的安全案例设计方法（RAISE），该方法针对基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）的驾驶系统定制了安全模式，旨在通过解决自然语言输入可能产生的潜在危险来确保其安全性。

Authors: Gerhard Yu, Fuyuki Ishikawa, Oluwafemi Odu, Alvine Boaye Belle

ExpertGen: Scalable Sim-to-Real Expert Policy Learning from Imperfect Behavior Priors

Abstract (English): Learning generalizable and robust behavior cloning policies requires large volumes of high-quality robotics data. While human demonstrations (e.g., through teleoperation) serve as the standard source for expert behaviors, acquiring such data at scale in the real world is prohibitively expensive. This paper introduces ExpertGen, a framework that automates expert policy learning in simulation to enable scalable sim-to-real transfer. ExpertGen first initializes a behavior prior using a diffusion policy trained on imperfect demonstrations, which may be synthesized by large language models or provided by humans. Reinforcement learning is then used to steer this prior toward high task success by optimizing the diffusion model's initial noise while keep original policy frozen. By keeping the pretrained diffusion policy frozen, ExpertGen regularizes exploration to remain within safe, human-like behavior manifolds, while also enabling effective learning with only sparse rewards. Empirical evaluations on challenging manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that ExpertGen reliably produces high-quality expert policies with no reward engineering. On industrial assembly tasks, ExpertGen achieves a 90.5% overall success rate, while on long-horizon manipulation tasks it attains 85% overall success, outperforming all baseline methods. The resulting policies exhibit dexterous control and remain robust across diverse initial configurations and failure states. To validate sim-to-real transfer, the learned state-based expert policies are further distilled into visuomotor policies via DAgger and successfully deployed on real robotic hardware.

摘要（中文）: 学习可泛化且鲁棒的行为克隆策略需要大量的优质机器人数据。虽然人类演示（例如，通过遥操作）是专家行为的标准来源，但在现实世界中大规模获取此类数据的成本高得令人望而却步。本文介绍了ExpertGen，一个自动化仿真环境中专家策略学习的框架，以实现可扩展的从仿真到实际的迁移。ExpertGen首先使用扩散策略初始化行为先验，该扩散策略基于不完美的演示进行训练，这些演示可以由大型语言模型合成或由人类提供。然后，强化学习被用于通过优化扩散模型初始噪声来引导该先验趋向高任务成功率，同时保持原始策略冻结。通过保持预训练的扩散策略冻结，ExpertGen正则化探索，使其保持在安全、类人的行为流形内，同时也使得仅使用稀疏奖励进行有效学习成为可能。在具有挑战性的操作基准上的实证评估表明，ExpertGen可靠地生成高质量的专家策略，而无需奖励工程。在工业装配任务中，ExpertGen的总体成功率达到90.5%，而在长程操作任务中，其总体成功率达到85%，优于所有基线方法。由此产生的策略展现出灵巧的控制，并在不同的初始配置和失败状态下保持鲁棒性。为了验证从仿真到实际的迁移，学习到的基于状态的专家策略通过DAgger进一步提炼成视觉运动策略，并成功部署在真实的机器人硬件上。

TLDR: ExpertGen combines diffusion policies with reinforcement learning to create high-quality expert policies in simulation, demonstrating successful sim-to-real transfer on challenging robotic manipulation tasks, even from imperfect priors.

简要说明: ExpertGen结合扩散策略和强化学习，在模拟环境中创建高质量的专家策略，展示了在具有挑战性的机器人操作任务中成功的模拟到真实世界的迁移，即使从不完善的先验条件开始。

Authors: Zifan Xu, Ran Gong, Maria Vittoria Minniti, Ahmet Salih Gundogdu, Eric Rosen, Kausik Sivakumar, Riedana Yan, Zixing Wang, Di Deng, Peter Stone, Xiaohan Zhang, Karl Schmeckpeper

Embodied Foundation Models at the Edge: A Survey of Deployment Constraints and Mitigation Strategies

Abstract (English): Deploying foundation models in embodied edge systems is fundamentally a systems problem, not just a problem of model compression. Real-time control must operate within strict size, weight, and power constraints, where memory traffic, compute latency, timing variability, and safety margins interact directly. The Deployment Gauntlet organizes these constraints into eight coupled barriers that determine whether embodied foundation models can run reliably in practice. Across representative edge workloads, autoregressive Vision-Language-Action policies are constrained primarily by memory bandwidth, whereas diffusion-based controllers are limited more by compute latency and sustained execution cost. Reliable deployment therefore depends on system-level co-design across memory, scheduling, communication, and model architecture, including decompositions that separate fast control from slower semantic reasoning.

摘要（中文）: 在具身边缘系统中部署基础模型本质上是一个系统性问题，而不仅仅是模型压缩的问题。实时控制必须在严格的尺寸、重量和功耗约束下运行，其中内存流量、计算延迟、时序变化以及安全裕度直接相互影响。“部署挑战”将这些约束组织成八个耦合的障碍，这些障碍决定了具身基础模型是否能在实践中可靠运行。在具有代表性的边缘工作负载中，自回归的视觉-语言-动作策略主要受内存带宽的限制，而基于扩散的控制器更多地受到计算延迟和持续执行成本的限制。因此，可靠的部署依赖于跨内存、调度、通信和模型架构的系统级协同设计，包括将快速控制与较慢的语义推理分离的分解方法。

TLDR: This survey paper examines the challenges of deploying embodied foundation models on edge devices, highlighting system-level constraints and potential mitigation strategies through co-design.

简要说明: 这篇综述论文探讨了在边缘设备上部署具身基础模型的挑战，强调了系统层面的约束以及通过协同设计实现缓解策略的潜力。

Authors: Utkarsh Grover, Ravi Ranjan, Mingyang Mao, Trung Tien Dong, Satvik Praveen, Zhenqi Wu, J. Morris Chang, Tinoosh Mohsenin, Yi Sheng, Agoritsa Polyzou, Eiman Kanjo, Xiaomin Lin

AsgardBench - Evaluating Visually Grounded Interactive Planning Under Minimal Feedback

Abstract (English): With AsgardBench we aim to evaluate visually grounded, high-level action sequence generation and interactive planning, focusing specifically on plan adaptation during execution based on visual observations rather than navigation or low-level manipulation. In the landscape of embodied AI benchmarks, AsgardBench targets the capability category of interactive planning, which is more sophisticated than offline high-level planning as it requires agents to revise plans in response to environmental feedback, yet remains distinct from low-level execution. Unlike prior embodied AI benchmarks that conflate reasoning with navigation or provide rich corrective feedback that substitutes for perception, AsgardBench restricts agent input to images, action history, and lightweight success/failure signals, isolating interactive planning in a controlled simulator without low-level control noise. The benchmark contains 108 task instances spanning 12 task types, each systematically varied through object state, placement, and scene configuration. These controlled variations create conditional branches in which a single instruction can require different action sequences depending on what the agent observes, emphasizing conditional branching and plan repair during execution. Our evaluations of leading vision language models show that performance drops sharply without visual input, revealing weaknesses in visual grounding and state tracking that ultimately undermine interactive planning. Our benchmark zeroes in on a narrower question: can a model actually use what it sees to adapt a plan when things do not go as expected?

摘要（中文）: 通过AsgardBench，我们旨在评估视觉 grounding 的高级动作序列生成和交互式规划，特别关注基于视觉观察而非导航或低级操作的执行期间的计划调整。 在具身AI基准测试领域，AsgardBench 针对交互式规划的能力类别，它比离线高级规划更复杂，因为它要求智能体响应环境反馈来修改计划，但又不同于低级执行。 与先前将推理与导航混淆或提供丰富的纠正性反馈来替代感知的具身AI基准测试不同，AsgardBench 将智能体的输入限制为图像、动作历史和轻量级的成功/失败信号，从而在没有低级控制噪声的受控模拟器中隔离交互式规划。 该基准测试包含 108 个任务实例，跨越 12 种任务类型，每种任务类型都通过对象状态、放置和场景配置进行系统性变化。 这些受控的变化创建了条件分支，其中单个指令可能需要不同的动作序列，具体取决于智能体观察到的内容，从而强调了执行期间的条件分支和计划修复。 我们对领先的视觉语言模型的评估表明，在没有视觉输入的情况下，性能会急剧下降，这揭示了视觉 grounding 和状态跟踪方面的弱点，最终破坏了交互式规划。 我们的基准测试集中在一个更窄的问题上：当情况不如预期时，模型是否能够真正利用它所看到的来调整计划？

TLDR: AsgardBench is a new embodied AI benchmark designed to evaluate visually grounded interactive planning, specifically focusing on how agents adapt plans based on visual observations with minimal feedback. The benchmark highlights the weaknesses of current vision language models in visually grounding and state tracking.

简要说明: AsgardBench是一个新的具身智能基准，旨在评估视觉基础的交互规划，特别关注智能体如何在最小反馈下根据视觉观察调整计划。该基准强调了当前视觉语言模型在视觉基础和状态跟踪方面的弱点。

Authors: Andrea Tupini, Lars Liden, Reuben Tan, Yu Wang, Jianfeng Gao

Regularized Latent Dynamics Prediction is a Strong Baseline For Behavioral Foundation Models

Abstract (English): Behavioral Foundation Models (BFMs) produce agents with the capability to adapt to any unknown reward or task. These methods, however, are only able to produce near-optimal policies for the reward functions that are in the span of some pre-existing state features, making the choice of state features crucial to the expressivity of the BFM. As a result, BFMs are trained using a variety of complex objectives and require sufficient dataset coverage, to train task-useful spanning features. In this work, we examine the question: are these complex representation learning objectives necessary for zero-shot RL? Specifically, we revisit the objective of self-supervised next-state prediction in latent space for state feature learning, but observe that such an objective alone is prone to increasing state-feature similarity, and subsequently reducing span. We propose an approach, Regularized Latent Dynamics Prediction (RLDP), that adds a simple orthogonality regularization to maintain feature diversity and can match or surpass state-of-the-art complex representation learning methods for zero-shot RL. Furthermore, we empirically show that prior approaches perform poorly in low-coverage scenarios where RLDP still succeeds.

摘要（中文）: 行为基础模型（BFM）生成的智能体能够适应任何未知的奖励或任务。然而，这些方法只能针对位于某些预先存在的状态特征跨度内的奖励函数产生近似最优策略，这使得状态特征的选择对于BFM的表达能力至关重要。因此，BFM的训练需要各种复杂的优化目标和足够的数据集覆盖，以训练出对任务有用的跨越特征。在这项工作中，我们研究的问题是：这些复杂的表征学习目标对于零样本强化学习是否是必要的？ 具体来说，我们重新审视了在潜在空间中进行自监督的下一状态预测，以进行状态特征学习的目标，但发现仅此目标易于增加状态特征的相似性，从而降低跨度。我们提出了一种方法，正则化潜在动力学预测（RLDP），它增加了一个简单的正交性正则化来保持特征多样性，并且可以匹配或超越最先进的复杂表征学习方法，用于零样本强化学习。此外，我们通过实验表明，先前的策略在低覆盖率场景下表现不佳，而RLDP仍然可以成功。

TLDR: The paper introduces Regularized Latent Dynamics Prediction (RLDP), a simple next-state prediction method with orthogonality regularization, which outperforms complex representation learning methods in zero-shot RL, especially in low-coverage scenarios.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了正则化潜在动力学预测（RLDP），一种带有正交正则化的简单下一状态预测方法，在零样本强化学习中优于复杂的表征学习方法，尤其是在低覆盖率的情况下。

Authors: Pranaya Jajoo, Harshit Sikchi, Siddhant Agarwal, Amy Zhang, Scott Niekum, Martha White

FEEL (Force-Enhanced Egocentric Learning): A Dataset for Physical Action Understanding

Abstract (English): We introduce FEEL (Force-Enhanced Egocentric Learning), the first large-scale dataset pairing force measurements gathered from custom piezoresistive gloves with egocentric video. Our gloves enable scalable data collection, and FEEL contains approximately 3 million force-synchronized frames of natural unscripted manipulation in kitchen environments, with 45% of frames involving hand-object contact. Because force is the underlying cause that drives physical interaction, it is a critical primitive for physical action understanding. We demonstrate the utility of force for physical action understanding through application of FEEL to two families of tasks: (1) contact understanding, where we jointly perform temporal contact segmentation and pixel-level contacted object segmentation; and, (2) action representation learning, where force prediction serves as a self-supervised pretraining objective for video backbones. We achieve state-of-the-art temporal contact segmentation results and competitive pixel-level segmentation results without any need for manual contacted object segmentation annotations. Furthermore we demonstrate that action representation learning with FEEL improves transfer performance on action understanding tasks without any manual labels over EPIC-Kitchens, SomethingSomething-V2, EgoExo4D and Meccano.

摘要（中文）: 我们引入了FEEL（力增强的以自我为中心的学习），这是首个大规模数据集，将定制压阻手套采集的力测量数据与以自我为中心的视频配对。我们的手套能够实现可扩展的数据收集，FEEL包含约300万个力同步的帧，记录了厨房环境中自然、非脚本化的操作，其中45%的帧涉及手-物体接触。由于力是驱动物理交互的根本原因，它是物理行为理解的关键原语。我们通过将FEEL应用于两类任务来展示力在物理行为理解中的效用：（1）接触理解，其中我们联合执行时间接触分割和像素级接触物体分割；（2）动作表征学习，其中力预测作为视频骨干网络的自监督预训练目标。我们实现了最先进的时间接触分割结果，以及具有竞争力的像素级分割结果，而无需任何手动接触物体分割标注。此外，我们证明了使用FEEL进行动作表征学习可以提高动作理解任务的迁移性能，而无需任何来自EPIC-Kitchens、SomethingSomething-V2、EgoExo4D和Meccano的手动标签。

TLDR: The paper introduces FEEL, a large-scale dataset of egocentric videos paired with force measurements from instrumented gloves for physical action understanding. It demonstrates the dataset's utility for contact understanding and self-supervised action representation learning.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了FEEL，一个包含第一人称视角视频和力传感器手套测量数据的大型数据集，用于物理动作理解。它展示了该数据集在接触理解和自监督动作表征学习方面的效用。

Authors: Eadom Dessalene, Botao He, Michael Maynord, Yonatan Tussa, Pavan Mantripragada, Yianni Karabati, Nirupam Roy, Yiannis Aloimonos

Emergent Dexterity via Diverse Resets and Large-Scale Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): Reinforcement learning in massively parallel physics simulations has driven major progress in sim-to-real robot learning. However, current approaches remain brittle and task-specific, relying on extensive per-task engineering to design rewards, curricula, and demonstrations. Even with this engineering, they often fail on long-horizon, contact-rich manipulation tasks and do not meaningfully scale with compute, as performance quickly saturates when training revisits the same narrow regions of state space. We introduce \Method, a simple and scalable framework that enables on-policy reinforcement learning to robustly solve a broad class of dexterous manipulation tasks using a single reward function, fixed algorithm hyperparameters, no curricula, and no human demonstrations. Our key insight is that long-horizon exploration can be dramatically simplified by using simulator resets to systematically expose the RL algorithm to the diverse set of robot-object interactions which underlie dexterous manipulation. \Method\ programmatically generates such resets with minimal human input, converting additional compute directly into broader behavioral coverage and continued performance gains. We show that \Method\ gracefully scales to long-horizon dexterous manipulation tasks beyond the capabilities of existing approaches and is able to learn robust policies over significantly wider ranges of initial conditions than baselines. Finally, we distill \Method \ into visuomotor policies which display robust retrying behavior and substantially higher success rates than baselines when transferred to the real world zero-shot. Project webpage: https://omnireset.github.io

摘要（中文）: 在海量并行物理模拟中应用的强化学习推动了从模拟到现实机器人学习的重大进展。 然而，目前的方法仍然脆弱且特定于任务，依赖于广泛的每任务工程来设计奖励、课程和演示。 即使有了这些工程，它们通常在长时程、富接触式操作任务中也会失败，并且不会随着计算能力的增加而有意义地扩展，因为当训练重新访问状态空间的相同狭窄区域时，性能会迅速饱和。 我们提出了\Method，一个简单且可扩展的框架，它可以使用单一奖励函数、固定算法超参数、无课程和无人工演示，从而使on-policy强化学习能够稳健地解决广泛的灵巧操作任务。 我们的关键见解是，通过使用模拟器重置来系统地将RL算法暴露于构成灵巧操作基础的各种机器人-对象交互中，可以极大地简化长时程探索。\Method通过最少的人工输入以编程方式生成此类重置，从而将额外的计算能力直接转化为更广泛的行为覆盖范围和持续的性能提升。 我们表明，\Method可以优雅地扩展到超出现有方法能力范围的长时程灵巧操作任务，并且能够学习比基线方法在更广泛的初始条件下更鲁棒的策略。 最后，我们将\Method提炼成视觉运动策略，这些策略表现出鲁棒的重试行为，并且零样本迁移到现实世界时，成功率远高于基线方法。 项目网页：https://omnireset.github.io

TLDR: The paper introduces a reinforcement learning framework called OmniReset for dexterous manipulation that uses diverse simulator resets to improve exploration and achieve robust sim-to-real transfer without task-specific engineering.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一个名为 OmniReset 的强化学习框架，用于灵巧操作，它使用各种模拟器重置来改进探索，并实现稳健的从模拟到现实的迁移，而无需特定于任务的工程。

Authors: Patrick Yin, Tyler Westenbroek, Zhengyu Zhang, Joshua Tran, Ignacio Dagnino, Eeshani Shilamkar, Numfor Mbiziwo-Tiapo, Simran Bagaria, Xinlei Liu, Galen Mullins, Andrey Kolobov, Abhishek Gupta

CorrectionPlanner: Self-Correction Planner with Reinforcement Learning in Autonomous Driving

Abstract (English): Autonomous driving requires safe planning, but most learning-based planners lack explicit self-correction ability: once an unsafe action is proposed, there is no mechanism to correct it. Thus, we propose CorrectionPlanner, an autoregressive planner with self-correction that models planning as motion-token generation within a propose, evaluate, and correct loop. At each planning step, the policy proposes an action, namely a motion token, and a learned collision critic predicts whether it will induce a collision within a short horizon. If the critic predicts a collision, we retain the sequence of historical unsafe motion tokens as a self-correction trace, generate the next motion token conditioned on it, and repeat this process until a safe motion token is proposed or the safety criterion is met. This self-correction trace, consisting of all unsafe motion tokens, represents the planner's correction process in motion-token space, analogous to a reasoning trace in language models. We train the planner with imitation learning followed by model-based reinforcement learning using rollouts from a pretrained world model that realistically models agents' reactive behaviors. Closed-loop evaluations show that CorrectionPlanner reduces collision rate by over 20% on Waymax and achieves state-of-the-art planning scores on nuPlan.

摘要（中文）: 自动驾驶需要安全规划，但大多数基于学习的规划器缺乏显式的自我纠正能力：一旦提出了不安全的动作，就没有任何机制来纠正它。因此，我们提出了CorrectionPlanner，一种具有自我纠正能力的自回归规划器，它将规划建模为在提出、评估和纠正循环内的运动令牌生成。在每个规划步骤中，策略提出一个动作，即一个运动令牌，一个学习到的碰撞评价器预测它是否会在短时间内导致碰撞。如果评价器预测发生碰撞，我们将保留历史不安全运动令牌的序列作为自我纠正轨迹，基于该轨迹生成下一个运动令牌，并重复此过程，直到提出安全运动令牌或满足安全标准。这个由所有不安全运动令牌组成的自我纠正轨迹，代表规划器在运动令牌空间中的纠正过程，类似于语言模型中的推理轨迹。我们采用模仿学习训练规划器，然后使用来自预训练的世界模型的推演，进行基于模型的强化学习，该世界模型能够真实地模拟智能体的反应行为。闭环评估表明，CorrectionPlanner在Waymax上将碰撞率降低了20%以上，并在nuPlan上实现了最先进的规划分数。

TLDR: The paper introduces CorrectionPlanner, a self-correcting, autoregressive planner for autonomous driving that uses a propose, evaluate, and correct loop trained with imitation and reinforcement learning to reduce collisions.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为 CorrectionPlanner 的自动驾驶自校正自回归规划器，该规划器使用提议、评估和校正循环，通过模仿学习和强化学习进行训练，以减少碰撞。
