Authors: Carmen Scheidemann, Andrei Cramariuc, Changan Chen, Jia-Ruei Chiu, Marco Hutter

Learning Whole-Body Control for a Salamander Robot

Abstract (English): Amphibious legged robots inspired by salamanders are promising in applications in complex amphibious environments. However, despite the significant success of training controllers that achieve diverse locomotion behaviors in conventional quadrupedal robots, most salamander robots relied on central-pattern-generator (CPG)-based and model-based coordination strategies for locomotion control. Learning unified joint-level whole-body control that reliably transfers from simulation to highly articulated physical salamander robots remains relatively underexplored. In addition, few legged robots have tried learning-based controllers in amphibious environments. In this work, we employ Reinforcement Learning to map proprioceptive observations and commanded velocities to joint-level actions, allowing coordinated locomotor behaviors to emerge. To deploy these policies on hardware, we adopt a system-level real-to-sim matching and sim-to-real transfer strategy. The learned controller achieves stable and coordinated walking on both flat and uneven terrains in the real world. Beyond terrestrial locomotion, the framework enables transitions between walking and swimming in simulation, highlighting a phenomenon of interest for understanding locomotion across distinct physical modes.

摘要（中文）: 摘要： 受蝾螈启发的两栖足式机器人在复杂的两栖环境中具有广阔的应用前景。然而，尽管在训练控制器以实现传统四足机器人多样化运动行为方面取得了显著成功，但大多数蝾螈机器人仍然依赖于基于中央模式发生器（CPG）和基于模型的协调策略进行运动控制。学习统一的、关节层面的全身控制，并将其可靠地从仿真迁移到高度铰接的物理蝾螈机器人上，仍然相对未被充分探索。此外，鲜有足式机器人在两栖环境中尝试基于学习的控制器。在这项工作中，我们采用强化学习来将本体感受观测和指令速度映射到关节层面的动作，从而实现协调的运动行为。为了在硬件上部署这些策略，我们采用了一种系统级的实物到仿真匹配和仿真到实物迁移策略。所学习的控制器在真实环境中实现了在平坦和不平坦地形上的稳定和协调的行走。除了陆地运动，该框架还支持在仿真中行走和游泳之间的转换，突出了理解跨不同物理模式的运动的一个有趣现象。

TLDR: This paper presents a reinforcement learning approach for controlling a salamander robot, enabling it to walk on various terrains and transition between walking and swimming, with a real-to-sim transfer strategy for hardware deployment.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的方法来控制蝾螈机器人，使其能够在各种地形上行走并在行走和游泳之间转换，并采用了一种实物到仿真的迁移策略来进行硬件部署。

Authors: Mengze Tian, Qiyuan Fu, Chuanfang Ning, Javier Jia Jie Pey, Auke Ijspeert

Reconciling distributed compliance with high-performance control in continuum soft robotics

Abstract (English): High-performance closed-loop control of truly soft continuum manipulators has remained elusive. Experimental demonstrations have largely relied on sufficiently stiff, piecewise architectures in which each actuated segment behaves as a distributed yet effectively rigid element, while deformation modes beyond simple bending are suppressed. This strategy simplifies modeling and control, but sidesteps the intrinsic complexity of a fully compliant body and makes the system behave as a serial kinematic chain, much like a conventional articulated robot. An implicit conclusion has consequently emerged within the community: distributed softness and dynamic precision are incompatible. Here we show this trade-off is not fundamental. We present a highly compliant, fully continuum robotic arm - without hardware discretization or stiffness-based mode suppression - that achieves fast, precise task-space convergence under dynamic conditions. The platform integrates direct-drive actuation, a tendon routing scheme enabling coupled bending and twisting, and a structured nonlinear control architecture grounded in reduced-order strain modeling of underactuated systems. Modeling, actuation, and control are co-designed to preserve essential mechanical complexity while enabling high-bandwidth loop closure. Experiments demonstrate accurate, repeatable execution of dynamic Cartesian tasks, including fast positioning and interaction. The proposed system achieves the fastest reported task-execution speed among soft robots. At millimetric precision, execution speed increases nearly fourfold compared with prior approaches, while operating on a fully compliant continuum body. These results show that distributed compliance and high-performance dynamic control can coexist, opening a path toward truly soft manipulators approaching the operational capabilities of rigid robots without sacrificing morphological richness.

摘要（中文）: 真正柔软连续体机械臂的高性能闭环控制一直难以实现。实验演示主要依赖于足够坚硬、分段式结构，其中每个驱动段表现为分布式的但实际上是刚性的元素，同时抑制了超出简单弯曲之外的变形模式。这种策略简化了建模和控制，但回避了完全顺应性物体的内在复杂性，并使系统表现得像一个串联运动学链，非常类似于传统的铰接式机器人。因此，在业界已逐渐形成一个隐含的结论：分布式柔软性和动态精度是不可兼得的。本文表明这种权衡并非根本性的。我们提出了一种高度顺应性的、完全连续体的机器人手臂——没有硬件离散化或基于刚度的模式抑制——在动态条件下能够实现快速、精确的任务空间收敛。该平台集成了直接驱动、一种实现耦合弯曲和扭转的肌腱布线方案，以及一种基于欠驱动系统降阶应变建模的结构化非线性控制架构。建模、驱动和控制经过协同设计，以保留必要的机械复杂性，同时实现高带宽的闭环。实验证明了动态笛卡尔任务的准确、可重复执行，包括快速定位和交互。所提出的系统实现了已报道的软体机器人中最快的任务执行速度。在毫米级精度下，执行速度比之前的方法提高了近四倍，同时在完全顺应性的连续体上运行。这些结果表明，分布式顺应性和高性能动态控制可以共存，从而为真正柔软的机械臂接近刚性机器人的操作能力开辟了一条道路，而不牺牲形态丰富的特性。

TLDR: This paper presents a fully compliant continuum robot arm that achieves fast and precise task-space control, demonstrating that distributed compliance and high-performance dynamic control are not fundamentally incompatible. It achieves the fastest task-execution speed among soft robots at millimetric precision.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种完全柔顺的连续体机器人手臂，实现了快速而精确的任务空间控制，证明了分布式柔顺性和高性能动态控制并非根本上不相容。该机器人以毫米级的精度实现了软体机器人中最快的任务执行速度。

Authors: Vito Daniele Perfetta, Daniel Feliu Talegon, Ebrahim Shahabi, Cosimo Della Santina

Dexterous grasp data augmentation based on grasp synthesis with fingertip workspace cloud and contact-aware sampling

Abstract (English): Robotic grasping is a fundamental yet crucial component of robotic applications, as effective grasping often serves as the starting point for various tasks. With the rapid advancement of neural networks, data-driven approaches for robotic grasping have become mainstream. However, efficiently generating grasp datasets for training remains a bottleneck. This is compounded by the diverse structures of robotic hands, making the design of generalizable grasp generation methods even more complex. In this work, we propose a teleoperation-based framework to collect a small set of grasp pose demonstrations, which are augmented using FSG--a Fingertip-contact-aware Sampling-based Grasp generator. Based on the demonstrated grasp poses, we propose AutoWS, which automatically generates structured workspace clouds of robotic fingertips, embedding the hand structure information directly into the clouds to eliminate the need for inverse kinematics calculations. Experiments on grasping the YCB objects show that our method significantly outperforms existing approaches in both speed and valid pose generation rate. Our framework enables real-time grasp generation for hands with arbitrary structures and produces human-like grasps when combined with demonstrations, providing an efficient and robust data augmentation tool for data-driven grasp training.

摘要（中文）: 机器人抓取是机器人应用中一个基本而关键的组成部分，因为有效的抓取通常是许多任务的起点。 随着神经网络的快速发展，基于数据的机器人抓取方法已成为主流。 然而，高效地生成用于训练的抓取数据集仍然是一个瓶颈。 机器人手部的多样化结构加剧了这一问题，使得通用抓取生成方法的设计更为复杂。 在这项工作中，我们提出了一种基于遥操作的框架，用于收集一小部分抓取姿态演示，并使用FSG（一种指尖接触感知的基于采样的抓取生成器）对其进行扩充。 基于演示的抓取姿态，我们提出了AutoWS，它可以自动生成机器人指尖的结构化工作空间点云，并将手部结构信息直接嵌入到点云中，从而无需进行逆运动学计算。 在抓取YCB物体的实验表明，我们的方法在速度和有效姿态生成率方面均显著优于现有方法。 我们的框架能够为具有任意结构的机械手实时生成抓取姿态，并且在与演示结合使用时，能够产生类似人类的抓取，为基于数据的抓取训练提供了一种高效而强大的数据增强工具。

TLDR: This paper presents a method for augmenting robotic grasp datasets using a teleoperation-based framework and a novel grasp generator (FSG) that leverages fingertip workspace clouds (AutoWS) for efficient and structure-aware grasp pose generation.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种基于遥操作框架的数据增强方法，利用一种新型的抓取生成器(FSG)，该生成器利用指尖工作空间云(AutoWS)进行高效的、结构感知的抓取姿势生成，从而增强机器人抓取数据集。

Authors: Liqi Wu, Haoyu Jia, Kento Kawaharazuka, Hirokazu Ishida, Kei Okada

Designing for Disagreement: Front-End Guardrails for Assistance Allocation in LLM-Enabled Robots

Abstract (English): LLM-enabled robots prioritizing scarce assistance in social settings face pluralistic values and LLM behavioral variability: reasonable people can disagree about who is helped first, while LLM-mediated interaction policies vary across prompts, contexts, and groups in ways that are difficult to anticipate or verify at contact point. Yet user-facing guardrails for real-time, multi-user assistance allocation remain under-specified. We propose bounded calibration with contestability, a procedural front-end pattern that (i) constrains prioritization to a governance-approved menu of admissible modes, (ii) keeps the active mode legible in interaction-relevant terms at the point of deferral, and (iii) provides an outcome-specific contest pathway without renegotiating the global rule. Treating pluralism and LLM uncertainty as standing conditions, the pattern avoids both silent defaults that hide implicit value skews and wide-open user-configurable "value settings" that shift burden under time pressure. We illustrate the pattern with a public-concourse robot vignette and outline an evaluation agenda centered on legibility, procedural legitimacy, and actionability, including risks of automation bias and uneven usability of contest channels.

摘要（中文）: 在社交环境中，启用大型语言模型（LLM）的机器人对稀缺援助进行优先级排序时，面临着多元价值观和LLM行为可变性的挑战：对于首先帮助谁，合理的个体之间可能存在分歧，而LLM介导的交互策略会因提示、情境和群体而异，难以在接触点上预测或验证。然而，针对实时、多用户援助分配的用户界面保护机制仍有待充分明确。我们提出具有可争议性的有界校准，这是一种程序性的前端模式，它（i）将优先级排序约束在治理机构批准的可接受模式菜单中，（ii）在推迟操作时，保持活动模式在交互相关术语中的可读性，以及（iii）提供特定结果的可争议途径，而无需重新协商全局规则。将多元主义和LLM不确定性视为常态，该模式避免了隐藏内隐价值倾斜的沉默默认，以及在时间压力下转移负担的开放式用户可配置“价值设置”。我们通过一个公共集会机器人示例来说明该模式，并概述了一个以可读性、程序性合法性和可操作性为中心的评估议程，包括自动化偏见和争议渠道可用性不均的风险。

TLDR: This paper proposes a framework for designing user-facing guardrails for LLM-enabled robots in social settings where assistance allocation may be contentious, focusing on calibration, legibility, and contestability to address pluralism and LLM uncertainty.

Authors: Carmen Ng

Onboard MuJoCo-based Model Predictive Control for Shipboard Crane with Double-Pendulum Sway Suppression

Abstract (English): Transferring heavy payloads in maritime settings relies on efficient crane operation, limited by hazardous double-pendulum payload sway. This sway motion is further exacerbated in offshore environments by external perturbations from wind and ocean waves. Manual suppression of these oscillations on an underactuated crane system by human operators is challenging. Existing control methods struggle in such settings, often relying on simplified analytical models, while deep reinforcement learning (RL) approaches tend to generalise poorly to unseen conditions. Deploying a predictive controller onto compute-constrained, highly non-linear physical systems without relying on extensive offline training or complex analytical models remains a significant challenge. Here we show a complete real-time control pipeline centered on the MuJoCo MPC framework that leverages a cross-entropy method planner to evaluate candidate action sequences directly within a physics simulator. By using simulated rollouts, this sampling-based approach successfully reconciles the conflicting objectives of dynamic target tracking and sway damping without relying on complex analytical models. We demonstrate that the controller can run effectively on a resource-constrained embedded hardware, while outperforming traditional PID and RL baselines in counteracting external base perturbations. Furthermore, our system demonstrates robustness even when subjected to unmodeled physical discrepancies like the introduction of a second payload.

摘要（中文）: 在海事环境中转移重型载荷依赖于高效的起重机操作，但受到危险的双摆载荷摇摆的限制。在离岸环境中，风和海浪的外部扰动会进一步加剧这种摇摆运动。人工操作员手动抑制欠驱动起重机系统上的这些振荡极具挑战。现有的控制方法在这种情况下表现不佳，通常依赖于简化的分析模型，而深度强化学习 (RL) 方法往往难以泛化到未见条件。在不依赖于大量的离线训练或复杂的分析模型的情况下，将预测控制器部署到计算受限、高度非线性的物理系统上仍然是一个重大挑战。本文展示了一个完整的实时控制管道，该管道以 MuJoCo MPC 框架为中心，利用交叉熵方法规划器直接在物理模拟器中评估候选动作序列。通过使用模拟推演，这种基于采样的方法成功地协调了动态目标跟踪和摇摆阻尼的冲突目标，而无需依赖复杂的分析模型。我们证明了该控制器可以在资源受限的嵌入式硬件上有效地运行，同时在抵抗外部基座扰动方面优于传统的 PID 和 RL 基线。此外，我们的系统还展示了鲁棒性，即使受到未建模的物理差异的影响，例如引入第二个载荷。

TLDR: This paper introduces a MuJoCo-based MPC framework for controlling shipboard cranes to suppress double-pendulum sway, demonstrating real-time performance on embedded hardware while outperforming PID and RL baselines in robustness and handling unmodeled discrepancies.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种基于 MuJoCo 的 MPC 框架，用于控制船载起重机以抑制双摆摆动。 实验表明，该框架可在嵌入式硬件上实现实时性能， 并在鲁棒性和处理未建模差异方面优于传统的 PID 和 RL 基线。

Authors: Oscar Pang, Lisa Coiffard, Paul Templier, Luke Beddow, Kamil Dreczkowski, Antoine Cully

ADAPT: Adaptive Dual-projection Architecture for Perceptive Traversal

Abstract (English): Agile humanoid locomotion in complex 3D en- vironments requires balancing perceptual fidelity with com- putational efficiency, yet existing methods typically rely on rigid sensing configurations. We propose ADAPT (Adaptive dual-projection architecture for perceptive traversal), which represents the environment using a horizontal elevation map for terrain geometry and a vertical distance map for traversable- space constraints. ADAPT further treats its spatial sensing range as a learnable action, enabling the policy to expand its perceptual horizon during fast motion and contract it in cluttered scenes for finer local resolution. Compared with voxel-based baselines, ADAPT drastically reduces observation dimensionality and computational overhead while substantially accelerating training. Experimentally, it achieves successful zero-shot transfer to a Unitree G1 Humanoid and signifi- cantly outperforms fixed-range baselines, yielding highly robust traversal across diverse 3D environtmental challenges.

摘要（中文）: 在复杂的3D环境中实现敏捷的人形机器人运动，需要在感知保真度和计算效率之间取得平衡，然而现有方法通常依赖于刚性的传感配置。我们提出ADAPT（用于感知遍历的自适应双投影架构），它使用水平高度图表示地形几何，使用垂直距离图表示可通行空间约束。ADAPT进一步将空间感知范围视为可学习的动作，使策略能够在快速运动时扩展其感知范围，并在拥挤场景中缩小感知范围以获得更精细的局部分辨率。与基于体素的基线相比，ADAPT显著降低了观察维度和计算开销，同时大大加速了训练。实验表明，ADAPT成功实现了向宇树G1人形机器人的零样本迁移，并显著优于固定范围的基线，从而在多样化的3D环境挑战中实现了高度鲁棒的遍历。

Authors: Shuo Shao, Tianchen Huang, Wei Gao, Shiwu Zhang

Agile Interception of a Flying Target using Competitive Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): This article presents a solution to intercept an agile drone by another agile drone carrying a catching net. We formulate the interception as a Competitive Reinforcement Learning problem, where the interceptor and the target drone are controlled by separate policies trained with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO). We introduce a high-fidelity simulation environment that integrates a realistic quadrotor dynamics model and a low-level control architecture implemented in JAX, which allows for fast parallelized execution on GPUs. We train the agents using low-level control, collective thrust and body rates, to achieve agile flights both for the interceptor and the target. We compare the performance of the trained policies in terms of catch rate, time to catch, and crash rate, against common heuristic baselines and show that our solution outperforms these baselines for interception of agile targets. Finally, we demonstrate the performance of the trained policies in a scaled real-world scenario using agile drones inside an indoor flight arena.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出了一种利用携带捕网的敏捷无人机拦截另一架敏捷无人机的解决方案。我们将拦截过程建模为一个竞争性强化学习问题，拦截机和目标无人机分别由使用近端策略优化（PPO）训练的独立策略控制。我们引入了一个高保真仿真环境，该环境集成了逼真的四旋翼动力学模型和用JAX实现的底层控制架构，从而可以在GPU上实现快速并行执行。我们使用底层控制、总推力和机体角速率来训练智能体，以实现拦截机和目标的敏捷飞行。我们通过捕获率、捕获时间和坠毁率等指标，将训练好的策略与常见的启发式基线进行比较，结果表明，我们的解决方案在拦截敏捷目标方面优于这些基线。最后，我们在室内飞行场地中使用敏捷无人机，在一个缩小的真实场景中展示了训练好的策略的性能。

TLDR: This paper uses Competitive Reinforcement Learning with PPO to train agile drones to intercept other drones with a net, demonstrating superior performance in simulation and a scaled real-world experiment compared to heuristic baselines.

Authors: Timothée Gavin, Simon Lacroix, Murat Bronz

MG-Grasp: Metric-Scale Geometric 6-DoF Grasping Framework with Sparse RGB Observations

Abstract (English): Single-view RGB-D grasp detection remains a common choice in 6-DoF robotic grasping systems, which typically requires a depth sensor. While RGB-only 6-DoF grasp methods has been studied recently, their inaccurate geometric representation is not directly suitable for physically reliable robotic manipulation, thereby hindering reliable grasp generation. To address these limitations, we propose MG-Grasp, a novel depth-free 6-DoF grasping framework that achieves high-quality object grasping. Leveraging two-view 3D foundation model with camera intrinsic/extrinsic, our method reconstructs metric-scale and multi-view consistent dense point clouds from sparse RGB images and generates stable 6-DoF grasp. Experiments on GraspNet-1Billion dataset and real world demonstrate that MG-Grasp achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) grasp performance among RGB-based 6-DoF grasping methods.

Authors: Kangxu Wang, Siang Chen, Chenxing Jiang, Shaojie Shen, Yixiang Dai, Guijin Wang

Ground Reaction Inertial Poser: Physics-based Human Motion Capture from Sparse IMUs and Insole Pressure Sensors

Abstract (English): We propose Ground Reaction Inertial Poser (GRIP), a method that reconstructs physically plausible human motion using four wearable devices. Unlike conventional IMU-only approaches, GRIP combines IMU signals with foot pressure data to capture both body dynamics and ground interactions. Furthermore, rather than relying solely on kinematic estimation, GRIP uses a digital twin of a person, in the form of a synthetic humanoid in a physics simulator, to reconstruct realistic and physically plausible motion. At its core, GRIP consists of two modules: KinematicsNet, which estimates body poses and velocities from sensor data, and DynamicsNet, which controls the humanoid in the simulator using the residual between the KinematicsNet prediction and the simulated humanoid state. To enable robust training and fair evaluation, we introduce a large-scale dataset, Pressure and Inertial Sensing for Human Motion and Interaction (PRISM), that captures diverse human motions with synchronized IMUs and insole pressure sensors. Experimental results show that GRIP outperforms existing IMU-only and IMU-pressure fusion methods across all evaluated datasets, achieving higher global pose accuracy and improved physical consistency.

摘要（中文）: 我们提出了一种名为地面反作用力惯性姿态估计器(GRIP)的方法，该方法使用四个可穿戴设备重建物理上合理的真人运动。与传统的仅使用IMU的方法不同，GRIP将IMU信号与足底压力数据相结合，以捕捉人体动力学以及与地面的交互作用。此外，GRIP没有仅仅依赖于运动学估计，而是采用人的数字孪生体，即物理模拟器中的合成人形，来重建真实且物理上合理的运动。GRIP的核心包含两个模块：KinematicsNet，它从传感器数据估计身体姿态和速度；以及DynamicsNet，它通过KinematicsNet预测与模拟人形状态之间的残差来控制模拟器中的人形。为了实现稳健的训练和公平的评估，我们引入了一个大规模数据集，即用于人类运动和交互的压力和惯性传感(PRISM)，该数据集捕捉了各种人类运动，并同步了IMU和鞋垫压力传感器。实验结果表明，在所有评估的数据集上，GRIP优于现有的仅使用IMU和IMU-压力融合的方法，实现了更高的全局姿态精度并改进了物理一致性。

TLDR: The paper introduces GRIP, a method for reconstructing physically plausible human motion using IMUs and insole pressure sensors, leveraging a physics simulator and a new dataset (PRISM). It claims to outperform existing methods in pose accuracy and physical consistency.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为GRIP的方法，利用IMU和鞋垫压力传感器，结合物理模拟器和一个新的数据集（PRISM）来重建符合物理规律的人体运动。该方法据称在姿态精度和物理一致性方面优于现有方法。

PanguMotion: Continuous Driving Motion Forecasting with Pangu Transformers

Abstract (English): Motion forecasting is a core task in autonomous driving systems, aiming to accurately predict the future trajectories of surrounding agents to ensure driving safety. Authors: Quanhao Ren, Yicheng Li, Nan Song

Enforcing Task-Specified Compliance Bounds for Humanoids via Anisotropic Lipschitz-Constrained Policies

Abstract (English): Reinforcement learning (RL) has demonstrated substantial potential for humanoid bipedal locomotion and the control of complex motions. To cope with oscillations and impacts induced by environmental interactions, compliant control is widely regarded as an effective remedy. However, the model-free nature of RL makes it difficult to impose task-specified and quantitatively verifiable compliance objectives, and classical model-based stiffness designs are not directly applicable. Lipschitz-Constrained Policies (LCP), which regularize the local sensitivity of a policy via gradient penalties, have recently been used to smooth humanoid motions. Nevertheless, existing LCP-based methods typically employ a single scalar Lipschitz budget and lack an explicit connection to physically meaningful compliance specifications in real-world systems. In this study, we propose an anisotropic Lipschitz-constrained policy (ALCP) that maps a task-space stiffness upper bound to a state-dependent Lipschitz-style constraint on the policy Jacobian. The resulting constraint is enforced during RL training via a hinge-squared spectral-norm penalty, preserving physical interpretability while enabling direction-dependent compliance. Experiments on humanoid robots show that ALCP improves locomotion stability and impact robustness, while reducing oscillations and energy usage.

摘要（中文）: 强化学习（RL）已展现出在人形双足运动和复杂运动控制方面的巨大潜力。为了应对环境交互引起的振荡和冲击，柔顺控制被广泛认为是有效的补救措施。然而，强化学习的无模型特性使其难以施加任务指定的、可定量验证的柔顺性目标，并且经典模型下的刚度设计也无法直接应用。Lipschitz约束策略（LCP）通过梯度惩罚来规范策略的局部敏感性，近来已被用于平滑人形运动。然而，现有的基于LCP的方法通常采用单一标量Lipschitz预算，并且缺乏与真实世界系统中具有物理意义的柔顺性规范的明确联系。在本研究中，我们提出了一种各向异性Lipschitz约束策略（ALCP），该策略将任务空间刚度上界映射到策略雅可比矩阵上一个状态相关的Lipschitz风格约束。在强化学习训练期间，通过铰链平方谱范数惩罚来强制执行该约束，从而保留了物理可解释性，同时实现了方向相关的柔顺性。在人形机器人上的实验表明，ALCP提高了运动稳定性、抗冲击能力，同时减少了振荡和能量消耗。

TLDR: This paper introduces an anisotropic Lipschitz-constrained policy (ALCP) for humanoid robot control, enabling task-specified compliance bounds during RL training, leading to improved stability and robustness.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种用于人形机器人控制的各向异性 Lipschitz 约束策略（ALCP），可在强化学习训练期间实现任务指定的柔顺性边界，从而提高稳定性和鲁棒性。

Authors: Zewen He, Yoshihiko Nakamura

Ultrafast Sampling-based Kinodynamic Planning via Differential Flatness

Abstract (English): Motion planning under dynamics constraints, i.e., kinodynamic planning, enables safe robot operation by generating dynamically feasible trajectories that the robot can accurately track. For high-\dof robots such as manipulators, sampling-based motion planners are commonly used, especially for complex tasks in cluttered environments. However, enforcing constraints on robot dynamics in such planners requires solving either challenging two-point boundary value problems (BVPs) or propagating robot dynamics over time, both of which are computational bottlenecks that drastically increase planning times. Meanwhile, recent efforts have shown that sampling-based motion planners can generate plans in microseconds using parallelization, but are limited to geometric paths. This paper develops AkinoPDF, a fast parallelized sampling-based kinodynamic motion planning technique for a broad class of differentially flat robot systems, including manipulators, ground and aerial vehicles, and more. Differential flatness allows us to transform the motion planning problem from the original state space to a flat output space, where an analytical time-parameterized solution of the BVP and dynamics integration can be obtained. A trajectory in the flat output space is then converted back to a closed-form dynamically feasible trajectory in the original state space, enabling fast validation via ``single instruction, multiple data" parallelism. Our method is fast, exact, and compatible with any sampling-based motion planner. We extensively verify the effectiveness of our approach in both simulated benchmarks and real experiments with cluttered and dynamic environments, requiring mere microseconds to milliseconds of planning time.

摘要（中文）: 动态约束下的运动规划，即运动动力学规划，通过生成机器人能够精确跟踪的、动力学上可行的轨迹，实现安全机器人操作。对于高自由度机器人，如机械臂，通常使用基于采样的运动规划器，尤其是在杂乱环境中的复杂任务中。然而，在这种规划器中强制执行机器人动力学约束需要求解具有挑战性的两点边界值问题 (BVPs) 或随时间推移传播机器人动力学，这两种方法都是计算瓶颈，会大大增加规划时间。同时，最近的研究表明，基于采样的运动规划器可以使用并行化在微秒级生成规划，但仅限于几何路径。本文开发了 AkinoPDF，一种快速并行化的基于采样的运动动力学运动规划技术，适用于广泛的微分平坦机器人系统，包括机械臂、地面和空中车辆等。微分平坦性使我们能够将运动规划问题从原始状态空间转换到平坦输出空间，在平坦输出空间中，可以获得 BVP 的解析时间参数化解和动力学积分。然后，将平坦输出空间中的轨迹转换回原始状态空间中的闭式动力学可行轨迹，从而可以通过“单指令，多数据”并行性进行快速验证。我们的方法快速、精确，并且与任何基于采样的运动规划器兼容。我们在模拟基准和现实试验（包括杂乱和动态环境）中广泛验证了我们方法的有效性，规划时间仅需微秒到毫秒级。

TLDR: This paper introduces AkinoPDF, a parallelized sampling-based kinodynamic motion planning technique for differentially flat robot systems, achieving microsecond to millisecond planning times through analytical solutions in a flat output space.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为AkinoPDF的并行采样式运动动力学规划技术，适用于微分平坦的机器人系统。该方法通过在平坦输出空间中获得解析解，实现了微秒到毫秒级的规划时间。

Authors: Thai Duong, Clayton W. Ramsey, Zachary Kingston, Wil Thomason, Lydia E. Kavraki

Robust Dynamic Object Detection in Cluttered Indoor Scenes via Learned Spatiotemporal Cues

Abstract (English): Reliable dynamic object detection in cluttered environments remains a critical challenge for autonomous navigation. Purely geometric LiDAR pipelines that rely on clustering and heuristic filtering can miss dynamic obstacles when they move in close proximity to static structure or are only partially observed. Vision-augmented approaches can provide additional semantic cues, but are often limited by closed-set detectors and camera field-of-view constraints, reducing robustness to novel obstacles and out-of-frustum events. In this work, we present a LiDAR-only framework that fuses temporal occupancy-grid-based motion segmentation with a learned bird's-eye-view (BEV) dynamic prior. A fusion module prioritizes 3D detections when available, while using the learned dynamic grid to recover detections that would otherwise be lost due to proximity-induced false negatives. Experiments with motion-capture ground truth show our method achieves 28.67% higher recall and 18.50% higher F1 score than the state-of-the-art in substantially cluttered environments while maintaining comparable precision and position error.

摘要（中文）: 在杂乱环境中可靠的动态物体检测仍然是自主导航面临的关键挑战。纯粹依赖于聚类和启发式过滤的几何激光雷达流程，当动态障碍物在静态结构附近运动或仅被部分观测时，可能会遗漏这些障碍物。视觉增强的方法可以提供额外的语义线索，但通常受限于封闭集检测器和相机视野的限制，从而降低了对新颖障碍物和视锥体外事件的鲁棒性。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种仅使用激光雷达的框架，该框架融合了基于时间占用栅格的运动分割和一个学习到的鸟瞰图（BEV）动态先验。融合模块在3D检测可用时优先考虑它们，同时利用学习到的动态栅格来恢复由于邻近性导致的假阴性而被遗漏的检测。使用运动捕捉真值的实验表明，在高度杂乱的环境中，我们的方法比最先进的方法实现了高出28.67%的召回率和18.50%的F1分数，同时保持了相当的精度和位置误差。

TLDR: This paper introduces a LiDAR-only framework for robust dynamic object detection in cluttered indoor environments, leveraging temporal occupancy grids and a learned BEV dynamic prior to improve recall and F1 score compared to state-of-the-art methods.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种仅使用激光雷达的框架，用于在杂乱室内环境中实现鲁棒的动态物体检测。该框架利用时间占用网格和学习到的鸟瞰视角 (BEV) 动态先验，相对于最先进的方法，提高了召回率和 F1 分数。

Authors: Juan Rached, Yixuan Jia, Kota Kondo, Jonathan P. How

You've Got a Golden Ticket: Improving Generative Robot Policies With A Single Noise Vector

Abstract (English): What happens when a pretrained generative robot policy is provided a constant initial noise as input, rather than repeatedly sampling it from a Gaussian? We demonstrate that the performance of a pretrained, frozen diffusion or flow matching policy can be improved with respect to a downstream reward by swapping the sampling of initial noise from the prior distribution (typically isotropic Gaussian) with a well-chosen, constant initial noise input -- a golden ticket. We propose a search method to find golden tickets using Monte-Carlo policy evaluation that keeps the pretrained policy frozen, does not train any new networks, and is applicable to all diffusion/flow matching policies (and therefore many VLAs). Our approach to policy improvement makes no assumptions beyond being able to inject initial noise into the policy and calculate (sparse) task rewards of episode rollouts, making it deployable with no additional infrastructure or models. Our method improves the performance of policies in 38 out of 43 tasks across simulated and real-world robot manipulation benchmarks, with relative improvements in success rate by up to 58% for some simulated tasks, and 60% within 50 search episodes for real-world tasks. We also show unique benefits of golden tickets for multi-task settings: the diversity of behaviors from different tickets naturally defines a Pareto frontier for balancing different objectives (e.g., speed, success rates); in VLAs, we find that a golden ticket optimized for one task can also boost performance in other related tasks. We release a codebase with pretrained policies and golden tickets for simulation benchmarks using VLAs, diffusion policies, and flow matching policies.

摘要（中文）: 当预训练的生成式机器人策略被赋予一个恒定的初始噪声作为输入，而不是重复地从高斯分布中采样时，会发生什么？我们证明，通过将初始噪声的采样从先验分布（通常是各向同性高斯分布）替换为精心选择的恒定初始噪声输入——即“金票”，可以提高预训练的、冻结的扩散或流匹配策略在下游奖励方面的性能。“金票”是一个绝佳的策略。我们提出了一种搜索方法，利用蒙特卡洛策略评估来寻找金票，该方法保持预训练策略冻结，不训练任何新的网络，并且适用于所有扩散/流匹配策略（因此也适用于许多VLA）。我们的策略改进方法除了能够将初始噪声注入策略并计算Episode轨迹的（稀疏）任务奖励之外，不做任何假设，因此无需额外的基础设施或模型即可部署。我们的方法在模拟和真实机器人操作基准测试的43个任务中的38个任务中提高了策略的性能，对于某些模拟任务，成功率的相对提升高达58%，对于真实任务，在50个搜索Episode内提升高达60%。我们还展示了金票在多任务环境中的独特优势：来自不同金票的行为多样性自然地定义了一条用于平衡不同目标（例如，速度、成功率）的帕累托前沿；在VLA中，我们发现为一项任务优化的金票也可以提高其他相关任务的性能。我们发布了一个代码库，其中包含使用VLA、扩散策略和流匹配策略的模拟基准测试的预训练策略和金票。

TLDR: The paper proposes a method to improve the performance of pretrained generative robot policies by optimizing a single, constant noise vector (a "golden ticket") instead of sampling from a Gaussian, achieving improved success rates in several robot manipulation tasks.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种通过优化单个恒定噪声向量（“金票”）而不是从高斯分布中采样来提高预训练生成机器人策略性能的方法，从而在多个机器人操作任务中实现了更高的成功率。

Authors: Omkar Patil, Ondrej Biza, Thomas Weng, Karl Schmeckpeper, Wil Thomason, Xiaohan Zhang, Robin Walters, Nakul Gopalan, Sebastian Castro, Eric Rosen

Learning to Present: Inverse Specification Rewards for Agentic Slide Generation

Abstract (English): Automated presentation generation remains a challenging task requiring coherent content creation, visual design, and audience-aware communication. This work proposes an OpenEnv-compatible reinforcement learning environment where LLM agents learn to research topics, plan content, and generate professional HTML slide presentations through tool use. We introduce a multi-component reward system combining structural validation, render quality assessment, LLM-based aesthetic scoring, content quality metrics, and an inverse specification reward that measures how faithfully generated slides convey their intended purpose. The inverse specification reward, an "inverse task" where an LLM attempts to recover the original specification from generated slides, provides a holistic quality signal. Our approach fine-tunes Qwen2.5-Coder-7B via GRPO, training only 0.5% of parameters on prompts derived from expert demonstrations collected using Claude Opus 4.6. Experiments on 48 diverse business briefs across six models demonstrate that our fine-tuned 7B model achieves 91.2% of Claude Opus 4.6's quality while improving 33.1% over the base model. The six-model comparison reveals that instruction adherence and tool-use compliance, rather than raw parameter count, determine agentic task performance. We contribute SlideRL, an open-source dataset of 288 multi-turn rollout trajectories across all six models: https://huggingface.co/datasets/KarthikRagunathAnandaKumar/sliderl-multi-turn-rollouts Code: https://github.com/pushing-the-frontier/slide-forge-llm

摘要（中文）: 自动演示文稿生成仍然是一项具有挑战性的任务，需要连贯的内容创建、视觉设计和面向受众的沟通。 本文提出了一种 OpenEnv 兼容的强化学习环境，其中 LLM 智能体学习研究主题、规划内容，并通过工具使用生成专业的 HTML 幻灯片演示文稿。 我们引入了一个多组件奖励系统，该系统结合了结构验证、渲染质量评估、基于 LLM 的美学评分、内容质量指标以及逆规范奖励，该奖励衡量生成的幻灯片是否忠实地传达了其预期目的。 逆规范奖励，即 LLM 尝试从生成的幻灯片中恢复原始规范的“逆向任务”，提供了一个整体性的质量信号。 我们的方法通过 GRPO 对 Qwen2.5-Coder-7B 进行微调，仅在从使用 Claude Opus 4.6 收集的专家演示中派生的提示上训练 0.5% 的参数。 对六个模型上的 48 个不同商业简报进行的实验表明，我们微调后的 7B 模型达到了 Claude Opus 4.6 质量的 91.2%，并且比基础模型提高了 33.1%。 这六个模型的比较表明，指令遵循和工具使用合规性，而不是原始参数数量，决定了代理任务的性能。 我们贡献了 SlideRL，一个包含六个模型中 288 个多轮 rollout 轨迹的开源数据集：https://huggingface.co/datasets/KarthikRagunathAnandaKumar/sliderl-multi-turn-rollouts 代码：https://github.com/pushing-the-frontier/slide-forge-llm

TLDR: This paper introduces a reinforcement learning environment (SlideRL) for LLM agents to generate slide presentations, using an inverse specification reward to improve content adherence and achieving impressive results with a fine-tuned 7B model. They also release a dataset of 288 multi-turn rollout trajectories.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一个强化学习环境 (SlideRL)，用于LLM智能体生成幻灯片演示文稿，使用逆向规范奖励来提高内容一致性，并通过微调的7B模型取得了令人印象深刻的结果。他们还发布了一个包含288个多回合轨迹的数据集。

Authors: Karthik Ragunath Ananda Kumar, Subrahmanyam Arunachalam

IOSVLM: A 3D Vision-Language Model for Unified Dental Diagnosis from Intraoral Scans

Abstract (English): 3D intraoral scans (IOS) are increasingly adopted in routine dentistry due to abundant geometric evidence, and unified multi-disease diagnosis is desirable for clinical documentation and communication. While recent works introduce dental vision-language models (VLMs) to enable unified diagnosis and report generation on 2D images or multi-view images rendered from IOS, they do not fully leverage native 3D geometry. Such work is necessary and also challenging, due to: (i) heterogeneous scan forms and the complex IOS topology, (ii) multi-disease co-occurrence with class imbalance and fine-grained morphological ambiguity, (iii) limited paired 3D IOS-text data. Thus, we present IOSVLM, an end-to-end 3D VLM that represents scans as point clouds and follows a 3D encoder-projector-LLM design for unified diagnosis and generative visual question-answering (VQA), together with IOSVQA, a large-scale multi-source IOS diagnosis VQA dataset comprising 19,002 cases and 249,055 VQA pairs over 23 oral diseases and heterogeneous scan types. To address the distribution gap between color-free IOS data and color-dependent 3D pre-training, we propose a geometry-to-chromatic proxy that stabilizes fine-grained geometric perception and cross-modal alignment. A two-stage curriculum training strategy further enhances robustness. IOSVLM consistently outperforms strong baselines, achieving gains of at least +9.58% macro accuracy and +1.46% macro F1, indicating the effectiveness of direct 3D geometry modeling for IOS-based diagnosis.

摘要（中文）: 3D口内扫描（IOS）由于其丰富的几何证据，在常规牙科中日益普及，并且统一的多病种诊断对于临床记录和交流至关重要。虽然最近的研究引入了牙科视觉-语言模型（VLM），以实现对2D图像或从IOS渲染的多视角图像的统一诊断和报告生成，但它们并未充分利用原生3D几何信息。鉴于以下原因，这项工作既有必要也充满挑战：（i）异构扫描形式和复杂的IOS拓扑结构，（ii）伴随类不平衡和细粒度形态模糊的多病共存，（iii）有限的配对3D IOS-文本数据。因此，我们提出了IOSVLM，一个端到端的3D VLM，它将扫描表示为点云，并遵循3D编码器-投影器-LLM设计，用于统一诊断和生成式视觉问答（VQA），以及IOSVQA，一个大规模多源IOS诊断VQA数据集，包含19,002个病例和249,055个VQA对，涵盖23种口腔疾病和异构扫描类型。为了解决无颜色IOS数据与依赖颜色的3D预训练之间的分布鸿沟，我们提出了一种几何-色度代理，以稳定细粒度的几何感知和跨模态对齐。一种两阶段课程学习策略进一步增强了鲁棒性。IOSVLM始终优于强大的基线，至少实现了+9.58%的宏精度和+1.46%的宏F1增益，表明直接3D几何建模对于基于IOS的诊断的有效性。

TLDR: The paper introduces IOSVLM, a 3D vision-language model for unified dental diagnosis from intraoral scans, addressing the limitations of existing 2D and multi-view approaches by directly leveraging 3D geometry with a novel dataset and training strategy.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了IOSVLM，一种用于从口腔内扫描进行统一牙科诊断的3D视觉语言模型，通过直接利用3D几何，并结合新的数据集和训练策略，解决了现有2D和多视图方法的局限性。

Authors: Huimin Xiong, Zijie Meng, Tianxiang Hu, Chenyi Zhou, Yang Feng, Zuozhu Liu

What if Pinocchio Were a Reinforcement Learning Agent: A Normative End-to-End Pipeline

Abstract (English): In the past decade, artificial intelligence (AI) has developed quickly. With this rapid progression came the need for systems capable of complying with the rules and norms of our society so that they can be successfully and safely integrated into our daily lives. Inspired by the story of Pinocchio in ``Le avventure di Pinocchio - Storia di un burattino'', this thesis proposes a pipeline that addresses the problem of developing norm compliant and context-aware agents. Building on the AJAR, Jiminy, and NGRL architectures, the work introduces \pino, a hybrid model in which reinforcement learning agents are supervised by argumentation-based normative advisors. In order to make this pipeline operational, this thesis also presents a novel algorithm for automatically extracting the arguments and relationships that underlie the advisors' decisions. Finally, this thesis investigates the phenomenon of \textit{norm avoidance}, providing a definition and a mitigation strategy within the context of reinforcement learning agents. Each component of the pipeline is empirically evaluated. The thesis concludes with a discussion of related work, current limitations, and directions for future research.

摘要（中文）: 在过去的十年中，人工智能（AI）发展迅速。随着这种快速发展，需要能够遵守我们社会规则和规范的系统，以便它们能够成功且安全地融入我们的日常生活。受到《木偶奇遇记》中匹诺曹故事的启发，本文提出了一种流水线，旨在解决开发符合规范且具有上下文感知能力智能体的问题。基于AJAR、Jiminy和NGRL架构，本文引入了\pino，这是一个混合模型，其中强化学习智能体受到基于论证的规范性顾问的监督。为了使该流水线能够运行，本文还提出了一种新的算法，用于自动提取支持顾问决策的论据和关系。最后，本文研究了\textit{规范规避}现象，在强化学习智能体的背景下提供了定义和缓解策略。流水线的每个组件都经过了实证评估。论文最后讨论了相关工作、当前局限性和未来研究方向。

TLDR: This thesis introduces a pipeline called \pino for developing norm-compliant reinforcement learning agents supervised by argumentation-based normative advisors, including an algorithm for automatically extracting arguments and a norm avoidance mitigation strategy.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一个名为\pino的管线，用于开发符合规范的强化学习智能体，该智能体由基于论证的规范顾问监督，包括一个自动提取论证的算法和一种规范规避缓解策略。

Authors: Benoît Alcaraz

ExpressMind: A Multimodal Pretrained Large Language Model for Expressway Operation

Abstract (English): The current expressway operation relies on rule-based and isolated models, which limits the ability to jointly analyze knowledge across different systems. Meanwhile, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly applied in intelligent transportation, advancing traffic models from algorithmic to cognitive intelligence. However, general LLMs are unable to effectively understand the regulations and causal relationships of events in unconventional scenarios in the expressway field. Therefore, this paper constructs a pre-trained multimodal large language model (MLLM) for expressways, ExpressMind, which serves as the cognitive core for intelligent expressway operations. This paper constructs the industry's first full-stack expressway dataset, encompassing traffic knowledge texts, emergency reasoning chains, and annotated video events to overcome data scarcity. This paper proposes a dual-layer LLM pre-training paradigm based on self-supervised training and unsupervised learning. Additionally, this study introduces a Graph-Augmented RAG framework to dynamically index the expressway knowledge base. To enhance reasoning for expressway incident response strategies, we develop a RL-aligned Chain-of-Thought (RL-CoT) mechanism that enforces consistency between model reasoning and expert problem-solving heuristics for incident handling. Finally, ExpressMind integrates a cross-modal encoder to align the dynamic feature sequences under the visual and textual channels, enabling it to understand traffic scenes in both video and image modalities. Extensive experiments on our newly released multi-modal expressway benchmark demonstrate that ExpressMind comprehensively outperforms existing baselines in event detection, safety response generation, and complex traffic analysis. The code and data are available at: https://wanderhee.github.io/ExpressMind/.

摘要（中文）: 当前高速公路运营依赖于基于规则的孤立模型，这限制了跨不同系统联合分析知识的能力。与此同时，大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地应用于智能交通领域，推动交通模型从算法智能向认知智能发展。然而，通用LLM无法有效地理解高速公路领域非常规场景中的规则和事件因果关系。因此，本文构建了一个面向高速公路的预训练多模态大型语言模型（MLLM），ExpressMind，作为智能高速公路运营的认知核心。本文构建了业界首个全栈高速公路数据集，涵盖交通知识文本、应急推理链和标注视频事件，以克服数据稀缺问题。本文提出了一种基于自监督训练和无监督学习的双层LLM预训练范式。此外，本研究引入了一个图增强RAG框架，以动态索引高速公路知识库。为了增强高速公路突发事件响应策略的推理能力，我们开发了一种RL对齐的思维链（RL-CoT）机制，该机制强制模型推理与专家在事件处理方面的解决问题的启发式方法保持一致。最后，ExpressMind集成了一个跨模态编码器，用于对齐视觉和文本通道下的动态特征序列，使其能够理解视频和图像模态下的交通场景。在我们新发布的多模态高速公路基准测试上的大量实验表明，ExpressMind在事件检测、安全响应生成和复杂交通分析方面全面优于现有基线。代码和数据可在：https://wanderhee.github.io/ExpressMind/ 获取。

TLDR: ExpressMind is a multimodal LLM pretrained on a newly constructed expressway dataset, incorporating graph-augmented RAG and RL-aligned CoT for improved performance on event detection, safety response, and traffic analysis in expressway operation.

简要说明: ExpressMind 是一个多模态LLM，基于新建的高速公路数据集进行预训练，结合了图增强 RAG 和 RL 对齐的 CoT，从而提高了在高速公路运营中的事件检测、安全响应和交通分析的性能。

Authors: Zihe Wang, Yihuan Wang, Haiyang Yu. Zhiyong Cui, Xiaojian Liao, Chengcheng Wang, Yonglin Tian, Yongxin Tong

Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Counteracts Delayed CSI in Multi-Satellite Systems

Abstract (English): The integration of satellite communication networks with next-generation (NG) technologies is a promising approach towards global connectivity. However, the quality of services is highly dependant on the availability of accurate channel state information (CSI). Channel estimation in satellite communications is challenging due to the high propagation delay between terrestrial users and satellites, which results in outdated CSI observations on the satellite side. In this paper, we study the downlink transmission of multiple satellites acting as distributed base stations (BS) to mobile terrestrial users. We propose a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithm which aims for maximising the sum-rate of the users, while coping with the outdated CSI. We design a novel bi-level optimisation, procedure themes as dual stage proximal policy optimisation (DS-PPO), for tackling the problem of large continuous action spaces as well as of independent and non-identically distributed (non-IID) environments in MARL. Specifically, the first stage of DS-PPO maximises the sum-rate for an individual satellite and the second stage maximises the sum-rate when all the satellites cooperate to form a distributed multi-antenna BS. Our numerical results demonstrate the robustness of DS-PPO to CSI imperfections as well as the sum-rate improvement attached by the use of DS-PPO. In addition, we provide the convergence analysis for the DS-PPO along with the computational complexity.

摘要（中文）: 将卫星通信网络与下一代(NG)技术集成是实现全球连接的一种有前景的方法。然而，服务质量高度依赖于精确的信道状态信息(CSI)的可用性。由于地面用户和卫星之间的高传播延迟，导致卫星端CSI观测值过时，使得卫星通信中的信道估计具有挑战性。在本文中，我们研究了多个卫星作为分布式基站(BS)向移动地面用户进行的下行传输。我们提出了一种多智能体强化学习(MARL)算法，旨在最大化用户的总速率，同时应对过时的CSI。我们设计了一种新颖的双层优化过程，主题为双阶段近端策略优化(DS-PPO)，用于解决MARL中大型连续动作空间以及独立且非同分布(non-IID)环境的问题。具体而言，DS-PPO的第一阶段最大化单个卫星的总速率，第二阶段则在所有卫星合作形成分布式多天线BS时最大化总速率。我们的数值结果表明了DS-PPO对CSI不完善的鲁棒性，以及使用DS-PPO带来的总速率提升。此外，我们还提供了DS-PPO的收敛性分析以及计算复杂度。

TLDR: This paper proposes a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithm called DS-PPO to maximize the sum-rate of terrestrial users in a multi-satellite system, addressing the challenge of outdated Channel State Information (CSI). The results demonstrate robustness to CSI imperfections and improved sum-rate.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种名为DS-PPO的多智能体强化学习算法，旨在最大化多卫星系统中地面用户的总速率，解决了信道状态信息（CSI）过时的问题。结果表明该算法对CSI的不完善具有鲁棒性，并提高了总速率。

Authors: Marios Aristodemou, Yasaman Omid, Sangarapillai Lambotharan, Mahsa Derakhshan, Lajos Hanzo

Continual Multimodal Egocentric Activity Recognition via Modality-Aware Novel Detection

Abstract (English): Multimodal egocentric activity recognition integrates visual and inertial cues for robust first-person behavior understanding. However, deploying such systems in open-world environments requires detecting novel activities while continuously learning from non-stationary streams. Existing methods rely on the main logits for novelty scoring, without fully exploiting the complementary evidence available from individual modalities. Because these logits are often dominated by RGB, cues from other modalities, particularly IMU, remain underutilized, and this imbalance worsens over time under catastrophic forgetting. To address this, we propose MAND, a modality-aware framework for multimodal egocentric open-world continual learning. At inference, Modality-aware Adaptive Scoring (MoAS) estimates sample-wise modality reliability from energy scores and adaptively integrates modality logits to better exploit complementary modality cues for novelty detection. During training, Modality-wise Representation Stabilization Training (MoRST) preserves modality-specific discriminability across tasks via auxiliary heads and modality-wise logit distillation. Experiments on a public multimodal egocentric benchmark show that MAND improves novel activity detection AUC by up to 10\% and known-class classification accuracy by up to 2.8\% over state-of-the-art baselines.

摘要（中文）: 多模态自我中心活动识别整合视觉和惯性线索，以实现稳健的第一人称行为理解。然而，在开放世界环境中部署此类系统需要在连续学习非平稳数据流的同时检测新颖活动。现有方法依赖于主要logits进行新颖性评分，而没有充分利用来自各个模态的互补证据。由于这些logits通常由RGB主导，来自其他模态（特别是IMU）的线索仍然未得到充分利用，并且这种不平衡会随着时间的推移因灾难性遗忘而加剧。为了解决这个问题，我们提出MAND，一个用于多模态自我中心开放世界持续学习的模态感知框架。在推理时，模态感知自适应评分（MoAS）从能量分数估计样本级别的模态可靠性，并自适应地整合模态logits，以更好地利用互补的模态线索进行新颖性检测。在训练期间，模态化表示稳定性训练（MoRST）通过辅助头和模态化logit蒸馏来保持跨任务的模态特定可区分性。在公共多模态自我中心基准上的实验表明，与最先进的基线相比，MAND将新颖活动检测AUC提高了高达10％，并将已知类分类准确率提高了高达2.8％。

TLDR: This paper introduces MAND, a modality-aware framework for continual multimodal egocentric activity recognition, addressing the challenge of novelty detection and catastrophic forgetting by adaptively integrating modality logits and preserving modality-specific discriminability.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一个名为MAND的模态感知框架，用于持续多模态自我中心活动识别，通过自适应地整合模态logits并保留模态特异性区分性来解决新颖性检测和灾难性遗忘的难题。

Authors: Wonseon Lim, Hyejeong Im, Dae-Won Kim

SQL-ASTRA: Alleviating Sparse Feedback in Agentic SQL via Column-Set Matching and Trajectory Aggregation

Abstract (English): Agentic Reinforcement Learning (RL) shows promise for complex tasks, but Text-to-SQL remains mostly restricted to single-turn paradigms. A primary bottleneck is the credit assignment problem. In traditional paradigms, rewards are determined solely by the final-turn feedback, which ignores the intermediate process and leads to ambiguous credit evaluation. To address this, we propose Agentic SQL, a framework featuring a universal two-tiered reward mechanism designed to provide effective trajectory-level evaluation and dense step-level signals. First, we introduce Aggregated Trajectory Reward (ATR) to resolve multi-turn credit assignment. Using an asymmetric transition matrix, ATR aggregates process-oriented scores to incentivize continuous improvement. Leveraging Lyapunov stability theory, we prove ATR acts as an energy dissipation operator, guaranteeing a cycle-free policy and monotonic convergence. Second, Column-Set Matching Reward (CSMR) provides immediate step-level rewards to mitigate sparsity. By executing queries at each turn, CSMR converts binary (0/1) feedback into dense [0, 1] signals based on partial correctness. Evaluations on BIRD show a 5% gain over binary-reward GRPO. Notably, our approach outperforms SOTA Arctic-Text2SQL-R1-7B on BIRD and Spider 2.0 using identical models, propelling Text-to-SQL toward a robust multi-turn agent paradigm.

摘要（中文）: 智能体强化学习（RL）在复杂任务中展现了潜力，但文本到SQL的转换仍主要局限于单轮范式。一个主要的瓶颈是信用分配问题。在传统范式中，奖励仅由最终轮次的反馈决定，这忽略了中间过程，导致模糊的信用评估。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了智能体SQL，一个具有通用两层奖励机制的框架，旨在提供有效的轨迹级别评估和密集的步骤级别信号。首先，我们引入了聚合轨迹奖励（ATR）来解决多轮信用分配问题。ATR使用非对称转移矩阵，聚合面向过程的分数，以激励持续改进。利用李雅普诺夫稳定性理论，我们证明了ATR充当能量耗散算子，保证无环策略和单调收敛。其次，列集匹配奖励（CSMR）提供即时的步骤级别奖励以缓解稀疏性。通过在每一轮执行查询，CSMR基于部分正确性将二元（0/1）反馈转换为密集的[0, 1]信号。在BIRD上的评估显示，相比于二元奖励的GRPO，有5%的增益。值得注意的是，我们的方法在使用相同模型的情况下，在BIRD和Spider 2.0上优于SOTA的Arctic-Text2SQL-R1-7B，从而推动文本到SQL的转换朝着稳健的多轮智能体范式发展。

TLDR: The paper introduces SQL-ASTRA, a framework for agentic SQL that addresses the sparse feedback problem in Text-to-SQL tasks using aggregated trajectory rewards and column-set matching rewards, demonstrating significant improvements over existing methods on BIRD and Spider 2.0.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了SQL-ASTRA，一个用于Agentic SQL的框架，通过聚合轨迹奖励和列集匹配奖励解决Text-to-SQL任务中稀疏反馈问题，并在BIRD和Spider 2.0上展示了相对于现有方法的显著改进。

Safety is Non-Compositional: A Formal Framework for Capability-Based AI Systems

Abstract (English): This paper contains the first formal proof that safety is non-compositional in the presence of conjunctive capability dependencies: two agents each individually inca- pable of reaching any forbidden capability can, when combined, collectively reach a forbidden goal through an emergent conjunctive dependency.

摘要（中文）: 本文首次正式证明了在存在合取能力依赖关系的情况下，安全性不具备可组合性：两个独立的个体各自无法获得任何被禁止的能力，但当组合在一起时，可以通过涌现的合取依赖关系共同达到一个被禁止的目标。

TLDR: The paper formally proves that safety is non-compositional in AI systems with conjunctive capability dependencies, meaning combining safe agents can lead to unsafe outcomes.

Authors: Cosimo Spera

The Internet of Physical AI Agents: Interoperability, Longevity, and the Cost of Getting It Wrong

Abstract (English): The Internet has evolved by progressively expanding what humanity connects: first computers, then people, and later billions of devices through the Internet of Things (IoT). While IoT succeeded in digitizing perception at scale, it also exposed fundamental limitations, including fragmentation, weak security, limited autonomy, and poor long-term sustainability. Today, advances in edge hardware, sensing, connectivity, and artificial intelligence enable a new phase: the Internet of Physical AI Agents. Unlike IoT devices that primarily sense and report, Physical AI Agents perceive, reason, and act in real time, operating autonomously and cooperatively across safety-critical domains such as disaster response, healthcare, industrial automation, and mobility. However, embedding fast-evolving AI capabilities into long-lived physical infrastructure introduces new architectural risks, particularly around interoperability, lifecycle management, and premature ossification. This article revisits lessons from IoT and Internet evolution, and articulates design principles for building resilient, evolvable, and trustworthy agentic systems. We present an architectural blueprint encompassing agentic identity, secure agent-to-agent communication, semantic interoperability, policy-governed runtimes, and observability-driven governance. We argue that treating evolution, trust, and interoperability as first-class requirements is essential to avoid hard-coding today's assumptions into tomorrow's intelligent infrastructure, and to prevent the high technical and economic cost of getting it wrong.

摘要（中文）: 互联网通过逐步扩展人类连接的对象而不断演进：起初是计算机，然后是人，后来是通过物联网（IoT）连接的数十亿设备。虽然物联网成功地大规模实现了感知的数字化，但它也暴露了根本性的局限性，包括碎片化、安全性薄弱、自主性有限以及长期的可持续性不足。如今，边缘硬件、传感、连接和人工智能的进步促成了一个新阶段：物理人工智能代理互联网。与主要进行感知和报告的物联网设备不同，物理人工智能代理能够实时地感知、推理和行动，在灾难响应、医疗保健、工业自动化和移动出行等安全关键领域中自主和协作地运行。然而，将快速发展的人工智能能力嵌入到长期存在的物理基础设施中会带来新的架构风险，尤其是在互操作性、生命周期管理和过早僵化方面。本文回顾了物联网和互联网演进的经验教训，并阐述了构建具有弹性的、可演进的和可信赖的代理系统的设计原则。我们提出了一个架构蓝图，其中包括代理身份、安全的代理间通信、语义互操作性、策略驱动的运行时以及可观察性驱动的治理。我们认为，将演进、信任和互操作性视为首要需求至关重要，以避免将当今的假设硬编码到未来的智能基础设施中，并防止因出错而产生高昂的技术和经济成本。

TLDR: This paper proposes an architectural blueprint for the 'Internet of Physical AI Agents', emphasizing interoperability, longevity, and trustworthiness to avoid the pitfalls of IoT development, particularly regarding premature ossification of AI capabilities in physical infrastructure.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一个“物理人工智能代理互联网”的架构蓝图，强调互操作性、寿命和可信度，以避免 IoT 开发的陷阱，尤其是在物理基础设施中人工智能能力的过早僵化方面。

Authors: Roberto Morabito, Mallik Tatipamula

Don't Trust Stubborn Neighbors: A Security Framework for Agentic Networks

Abstract (English): Large Language Model (LLM)-based Multi-Agent Systems (MASs) are increasingly deployed for agentic tasks, such as web automation, itinerary planning, and collaborative problem solving. Yet, their interactive nature introduces new security risks: malicious or compromised agents can exploit communication channels to propagate misinformation and manipulate collective outcomes. In this paper, we study how such manipulation can arise and spread by borrowing the Friedkin-Johnsen opinion formation model from social sciences to propose a general theoretical framework to study LLM-MAS. Remarkably, this model closely captures LLM-MAS behavior, as we verify in extensive experiments across different network topologies and attack and defense scenarios. Theoretically and empirically, we find that a single highly stubborn and persuasive agent can take over MAS dynamics, underscoring the systems' high susceptibility to attacks by triggering a persuasion cascade that reshapes collective opinion. Our theoretical analysis reveals three mechanisms to increase system security: a) increasing the number of benign agents, b) increasing the innate stubbornness or peer-resistance of agents, or c) reducing trust in potential adversaries. Because scaling is computationally expensive and high stubbornness degrades the network's ability to reach consensus, we propose a new mechanism to mitigate threats by a trust-adaptive defense that dynamically adjusts inter-agent trust to limit adversarial influence while maintaining cooperative performance. Extensive experiments confirm that this mechanism effectively defends against manipulation.

摘要（中文）: 基于大型语言模型(LLM)的多智能体系统(MAS)正日益广泛地应用于自主任务，如网页自动化、行程规划和协同问题解决。然而，其交互性带来新的安全风险：恶意或受损的智能体可能利用通信渠道传播虚假信息并操纵集体结果。 本文中，我们借鉴社会科学中的Friedkin-Johnsen意见形成模型，提出一个通用的理论框架来研究LLM-MAS，从而研究此类操纵如何产生和传播。值得注意的是，该模型能够紧密捕捉LLM-MAS的行为，我们在不同网络拓扑以及攻击和防御场景下的大量实验中验证了这一点。从理论和实验两方面，我们发现一个高度顽固和具有说服力的智能体可以主导MAS的动态，突显了系统极易受到攻击，触发一种说服级联来重塑集体意见。我们的理论分析揭示了三种提高系统安全性的机制：a) 增加良性智能体的数量；b) 增加智能体固有的顽固性或同伴抵抗力；或者 c) 降低对潜在对手的信任。由于扩展在计算上代价高昂，且高顽固性会降低网络达成共识的能力，我们提出一种新的机制，即通过信任自适应防御来缓解威胁，该机制动态调整智能体间的信任，以限制对抗性影响，同时保持协作性能。大量实验证实，该机制能有效防御操纵。

TLDR: This paper studies the security vulnerabilities of LLM-based multi-agent systems to adversarial manipulation using the Friedkin-Johnsen model and proposes a trust-adaptive defense mechanism to mitigate these threats.

简要说明: 本文研究了基于LLM的多智能体系统在对抗性操纵下的安全漏洞，使用Friedkin-Johnsen模型，并提出了一种信任自适应防御机制来缓解这些威胁。

Authors: Samira Abedini, Sina Mavali, Lea Schönherr, Martin Pawelczyk, Rebekka Burkholz

M^3: Dense Matching Meets Multi-View Foundation Models for Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM

Abstract (English): Streaming reconstruction from uncalibrated monocular video remains challenging, as it requires both high-precision pose estimation and computationally efficient online refinement in dynamic environments. While coupling 3D foundation models with SLAM frameworks is a promising paradigm, a critical bottleneck persists: most multi-view foundation models estimate poses in a feed-forward manner, yielding pixel-level correspondences that lack the requisite precision for rigorous geometric optimization. To address this, we present M^3, which augments the Multi-view foundation model with a dedicated Matching head to facilitate fine-grained dense correspondences and integrates it into a robust Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM. M^3 further enhances tracking stability by incorporating dynamic area suppression and cross-inference intrinsic alignment. Extensive experiments on diverse indoor and outdoor benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art accuracy in both pose estimation and scene reconstruction. Notably, M^3 reduces ATE RMSE by 64.3% compared to VGGT-SLAM 2.0 and outperforms ARTDECO by 2.11 dB in PSNR on the ScanNet++ dataset.

摘要（中文）: 从无标定单目视频进行流式重建仍然具有挑战性，因为它既需要高精度的姿态估计，也需要在动态环境中进行计算高效的在线优化。虽然将3D基础模型与SLAM框架结合是一个有前景的范式，但一个关键的瓶颈仍然存在：大多数多视图基础模型以一种前馈的方式估计姿态，产生的像素级对应关系缺乏严格几何优化所需的精度。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了M^3，它通过一个专用的匹配头来增强多视图基础模型，以促进精细的稠密对应关系，并将其集成到一个鲁棒的单目高斯溅射SLAM中。M^3通过引入动态区域抑制和交叉推理的内参对齐来进一步提高跟踪稳定性。在各种室内和室外基准上的大量实验表明，M^3在姿态估计和场景重建方面都达到了最先进的精度。值得注意的是，与VGGT-SLAM 2.0相比，M^3将ATE RMSE降低了64.3%，并且在ScanNet++数据集上，PSNR比ARTDECO高出2.11 dB。

TLDR: The paper presents M^3, a monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM system that integrates a multi-view foundation model with a matching head for fine-grained dense correspondences, achieving state-of-the-art accuracy in pose estimation and scene reconstruction.

简要说明: 该论文提出了M^3，一个单目高斯溅射SLAM系统，它集成了一个多视图基础模型和一个匹配头，以实现精细的稠密对应，从而在姿态估计和场景重建方面实现了最先进的精度。

Authors: Kerui Ren, Guanghao Li, Changjian Jiang, Yingxiang Xu, Tao Lu, Linning Xu, Junting Dong, Jiangmiao Pang, Mulin Yu, Bo Dai

Deep Reinforcement Learning-driven Edge Offloading for Latency-constrained XR pipelines

Abstract (English): Immersive extended reality (XR) applications introduce latency-critical workloads that must satisfy stringent real-time responsiveness while operating on energy- and battery-constrained devices, making execution placement between end devices and nearby edge servers a fundamental systems challenge. Existing approaches to adaptive execution and computation offloading typically optimize average performance metrics and do not fully capture the sustained interaction between real-time latency requirements and device battery lifetime in closed-loop XR workloads. In this paper, we present a battery-aware execution management framework for edge-assisted XR systems that jointly considers execution placement, workload quality, latency requirements, and battery dynamics. We design an online decision mechanism based on a lightweight deep reinforcement learning policy that continuously adapts execution decisions under dynamic network conditions while maintaining high motion-to-photon latency compliance. Experimental results show that the proposed approach extends the projected device battery lifetime by up to 163% compared to latency-optimal local execution while maintaining over 90% motion-to-photon latency compliance under stable network conditions. Such compliance does not fall below 80% even under significantly limited network bandwidth availability, thereby demonstrating the effectiveness of explicitly managing latency-energy trade-offs in immersive XR systems.

摘要（中文）: 沉浸式扩展现实(XR)应用引入了对延迟高度敏感的工作负载，这些工作负载必须在能量和电池受限的设备上运行，同时满足严格的实时响应性要求，这使得终端设备和附近边缘服务器之间的执行位置选择成为一个根本的系统挑战。 现有的自适应执行和计算卸载方法通常优化平均性能指标，而不能完全捕捉到闭环XR工作负载中实时延迟需求和设备电池续航时间之间的持续交互。 在本文中，我们提出了一种用于边缘辅助XR系统的电池感知执行管理框架，该框架联合考虑了执行位置、工作负载质量、延迟需求和电池动态。 我们设计了一种基于轻量级深度强化学习策略的在线决策机制，该机制在动态网络条件下持续调整执行决策，同时保持较高的动-光延迟符合性。实验结果表明，与延迟最优的本地执行相比，所提出的方法可将预计的设备电池续航时间延长高达163%，同时在稳定的网络条件下保持90%以上的动-光延迟符合性。即使在网络带宽可用性受到显著限制的情况下，这种符合性也不会低于80%，从而证明了在沉浸式XR系统中显式管理延迟-能量权衡的有效性。

TLDR: This paper proposes a deep reinforcement learning-driven edge offloading framework to manage latency and battery life for XR applications, achieving significant improvements in battery life while maintaining high latency compliance.

Authors: Sourya Saha, Saptarshi Debroy

Search2Motion: Training-Free Object-Level Motion Control via Attention-Consensus Search

Abstract (English): We present Search2Motion, a training-free framework for object-level motion editing in image-to-video generation. Unlike prior methods requiring trajectories, bounding boxes, masks, or motion fields, Search2Motion adopts target-frame-based control, leveraging first-last-frame motion priors to realize object relocation while preserving scene stability without fine-tuning. Reliable target-frame construction is achieved through semantic-guided object insertion and robust background inpainting. We further show that early-step self-attention maps predict object and camera dynamics, offering interpretable user feedback and motivating ACE-Seed (Attention Consensus for Early-step Seed selection), a lightweight search strategy that improves motion fidelity without look-ahead sampling or external evaluators. Noting that existing benchmarks conflate object and camera motion, we introduce S2M-DAVIS and S2M-OMB for stable-camera, object-only evaluation, alongside FLF2V-obj metrics that isolate object artifacts without requiring ground-truth trajectories. Search2Motion consistently outperforms baselines on FLF2V-obj and VBench.

摘要（中文）: 我们提出了Search2Motion，一种无需训练的框架，用于图像到视频生成中的物体级运动编辑。与之前需要轨迹、边界框、掩码或运动场的方法不同，Search2Motion 采用基于目标帧的控制，利用首末帧运动先验来实现物体重定位，同时保持场景稳定性，且无需微调。可靠的目标帧构建是通过语义引导的物体插入和鲁棒的背景修复实现的。我们进一步表明，早期的自注意力图预测了物体和相机的动态，提供了可解释的用户反馈，并驱动了ACE-Seed（用于早期种子选择的注意力共识），这是一种轻量级的搜索策略，可提高运动保真度，而无需前瞻采样或外部评估器。注意到现有基准测试混淆了物体和相机运动，我们引入了S2M-DAVIS和S2M-OMB，用于稳定的相机，仅物体评估，以及FLF2V-obj指标，该指标隔离了物体伪影，而无需真实轨迹。Search2Motion在FLF2V-obj和VBench上始终优于基线。

TLDR: Search2Motion is a training-free framework for object-level motion editing in image-to-video generation using attention maps for motion prediction and a novel search strategy (ACE-Seed) to improve motion fidelity. It introduces new benchmarks (S2M-DAVIS, S2M-OMB) and metrics (FLF2V-obj) for object-only motion evaluation.

简要说明: Search2Motion是一个无需训练的框架，用于图像到视频生成中的对象级别运动编辑，它利用注意力图进行运动预测，并使用一种新的搜索策略（ACE-Seed）来提高运动保真度。它还引入了新的基准（S2M-DAVIS、S2M-OMB）和指标（FLF2V-obj），用于仅对象运动评估。

Authors: Sainan Liu, Tz-Ying Wu, Hector A Valdez, Subarna Tripathi

DriveFix: Spatio-Temporally Coherent Driving Scene Restoration

Abstract (English): Recent advancements in 4D scene reconstruction, particularly those leveraging diffusion priors, have shown promise for novel view synthesis in autonomous driving. However, these methods often process frames independently or in a view-by-view manner, leading to a critical lack of spatio-temporal synergy. This results in spatial misalignment across cameras and temporal drift in sequences. We propose DriveFix, a novel multi-view restoration framework that ensures spatio-temporal coherence for driving scenes. Our approach employs an interleaved diffusion transformer architecture with specialized blocks to explicitly model both temporal dependencies and cross-camera spatial consistency. By conditioning the generation on historical context and integrating geometry-aware training losses, DriveFix enforces that the restored views adhere to a unified 3D geometry. This enables the consistent propagation of high-fidelity textures and significantly reduces artifacts. Extensive evaluations on the Waymo, nuScenes, and PandaSet datasets demonstrate that DriveFix achieves state-of-the-art performance in both reconstruction and novel view synthesis, marking a substantial step toward robust 4D world modeling for real-world deployment.

摘要（中文）: 近期在 4D 场景重建方面的进展，尤其是利用扩散先验的方法，已显示出在自动驾驶中进行新视角合成的潜力。然而，这些方法通常独立地或逐视角地处理帧，导致时空协同效应的严重缺失。这导致了跨相机空间上的不对齐以及序列中的时间漂移。我们提出了 DriveFix，一种新颖的多视角修复框架，用于确保驾驶场景的时空一致性。我们的方法采用了一种交错的扩散 Transformer 架构，该架构具有专门的块，以显式地建模时间依赖关系和跨相机空间一致性。通过以历史上下文为条件进行生成，并集成几何感知训练损失，DriveFix 确保修复后的视角遵循统一的 3D 几何结构。这使得高保真纹理能够一致地传播，并显著减少伪影。在 Waymo、nuScenes 和 PandaSet 数据集上的广泛评估表明，DriveFix 在重建和新视角合成方面均实现了最先进的性能，标志着朝着为真实世界部署而构建的鲁棒 4D 世界建模迈出了实质性的一步。

TLDR: DriveFix addresses the spatio-temporal incoherence issues in 4D driving scene reconstruction by introducing a diffusion transformer architecture that enforces cross-camera consistency and temporal dependencies, achieving state-of-the-art performance on multiple datasets.

简要说明: DriveFix通过引入扩散Transformer架构解决了4D驾驶场景重建中的时空不一致问题，该架构强制执行跨相机一致性和时间依赖性，并在多个数据集上实现了最先进的性能。

Authors: Heyu Si, Brandon James Denis, Muyang Sun, Dragos Datcu, Yaoru Li, Xin Jin, Ruiju Fu, Yuliia Tatarinova, Federico Landi, Jie Song, Mingli Song, Qi Guo

VIGOR: VIdeo Geometry-Oriented Reward for Temporal Generative Alignment

Abstract (English): Video diffusion models lack explicit geometric supervision during training, leading to inconsistency artifacts such as object deformation, spatial drift, and depth violations in generated videos. To address this limitation, we propose a geometry-based reward model that leverages pretrained geometric foundation models to evaluate multi-view consistency through cross-frame reprojection error. Unlike previous geometric metrics that measure inconsistency in pixel space, where pixel intensity may introduce additional noise, our approach conducts error computation in a pointwise fashion, yielding a more physically grounded and robust error metric. Furthermore, we introduce a geometry-aware sampling strategy that filters out low-texture and non-semantic regions, focusing evaluation on geometrically meaningful areas with reliable correspondences to improve robustness. We apply this reward model to align video diffusion models through two complementary pathways: post-training of a bidirectional model via SFT or Reinforcement Learning and inference-time optimization of a Causal Video Model (e.g., Streaming video generator) via test-time scaling with our reward as a path verifier. Experimental results validate the effectiveness of our design, demonstrating that our geometry-based reward provides superior robustness compared to other variants. By enabling efficient inference-time scaling, our method offers a practical solution for enhancing open-source video models without requiring extensive computational resources for retraining.

摘要（中文）: 视频扩散模型在训练期间缺乏显式的几何监督，导致生成的视频中出现对象变形、空间漂移和深度违反等不一致性伪影。为了解决这个局限性，我们提出了一种基于几何的奖励模型，该模型利用预训练的几何基础模型通过跨帧重投影误差评估多视角一致性。与之前在像素空间中测量不一致性的几何指标不同（像素强度可能会引入额外的噪声），我们的方法以逐点方式进行误差计算，产生更符合物理规律且鲁棒的误差度量。此外，我们引入了一种几何感知采样策略，可以过滤掉低纹理和非语义区域，并将评估集中在具有可靠对应关系的几何意义区域上，从而提高鲁棒性。我们将此奖励模型应用于通过两种互补途径对齐视频扩散模型：通过 SFT 或强化学习对双向模型进行后训练，以及通过测试时缩放将我们的奖励作为路径验证器对因果视频模型（例如，流媒体视频生成器）进行推理时优化。实验结果验证了我们设计的有效性，表明我们的基于几何的奖励比其他变体提供了卓越的鲁棒性。通过实现高效的推理时缩放，我们的方法为增强开源视频模型提供了一个实用的解决方案，而无需大量的重新训练计算资源。

TLDR: The paper introduces a geometry-based reward model (VIGOR) that leverages pretrained geometric foundation models to improve the consistency of video diffusion models by addressing object deformation and spatial drift. It aligns video diffusion models via SFT/RL post-training or test-time scaling, demonstrating enhanced robustness and efficiency.

Authors: Tengjiao Yin, Jinglei Shi, Heng Guo, Xi Wang

EFF-Grasp: Energy-Field Flow Matching for Physics-Aware Dexterous Grasp Generation

Abstract (English): Denoising generative models have recently become the dominant paradigm for dexterous grasp generation, owing to their ability to model complex grasp distributions from large-scale data. However, existing diffusion-based methods typically formulate generation as a stochastic differential equation (SDE), which often requires many sequential denoising steps and introduces trajectory instability that can lead to physically infeasible grasps. In this paper, we propose EFF-Grasp, a novel Flow-Matching-based framework for physics-aware dexterous grasp generation. Specifically, we reformulate grasp synthesis as a deterministic ordinary differential equation (ODE) process, which enables efficient and stable generation through smooth probability flows. To further enforce physical feasibility, we introduce a training-free physics-aware energy guidance strategy. Our method defines an energy-guided target distribution using adapted explicit physical energy functions that capture key grasp constraints, and estimates the corresponding guidance term via a local Monte Carlo approximation during inference. In this way, EFF-Grasp dynamically steers the generation trajectory toward physically feasible regions without requiring additional physics-based training or simulation feedback. Extensive experiments on five benchmark datasets show that EFF-Grasp achieves superior performance in grasp quality and physical feasibility, while requiring substantially fewer sampling steps than diffusion-based baselines.

摘要（中文）: 去噪生成模型近期已成为灵巧抓取生成的主流范式，这归功于它们能够从大规模数据中建模复杂的抓取分布。然而，现有的基于扩散的方法通常将生成过程公式化为随机微分方程(SDE)，这通常需要许多连续的去噪步骤，并引入可能导致物理上不可行的抓取的轨迹不稳定性。在本文中，我们提出EFF-Grasp，一种用于物理感知灵巧抓取生成的新型基于流匹配的框架。具体来说，我们将抓取合成重构为确定性的常微分方程(ODE)过程，这可以通过平滑的概率流实现高效且稳定的生成。为了进一步加强物理可行性，我们引入了一种免训练的物理感知能量引导策略。我们的方法使用适应性的显式物理能量函数定义一个能量引导的目标分布，该函数捕获关键的抓取约束，并在推理过程中通过局部蒙特卡洛近似估计相应的引导项。通过这种方式，EFF-Grasp动态地引导生成轨迹朝向物理上可行的区域，而无需额外的基于物理的训练或仿真反馈。在五个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，EFF-Grasp在抓取质量和物理可行性方面实现了卓越的性能，同时比基于扩散的基线方法需要明显更少的采样步骤。

TLDR: The paper introduces EFF-Grasp, a Flow-Matching-based framework for generating physically feasible dexterous grasps by using deterministic ODEs for efficient generation and a training-free physics-aware energy guidance strategy.

简要说明: 本文介绍 EFF-Grasp，一个基于流匹配的框架，通过使用确定性 ODE 实现高效生成，并采用免训练的物理感知能量引导策略，来生成物理上可行的灵巧抓取。

Authors: Yukun Zhao, Zichen Zhong, Yongshun Gong, Yilong Yin, Haoliang Sun

Out-of-Distribution Object Detection in Street Scenes via Synthetic Outlier Exposure and Transfer Learning

Abstract (English): Out-of-distribution (OOD) object detection is an important yet underexplored task. A reliable object detector should be able to handle OOD objects by localizing and correctly classifying them as OOD. However, a critical issue arises when such atypical objects are completely missed by the object detector and incorrectly treated as background. Existing OOD detection approaches in object detection often rely on complex architectures or auxiliary branches and typically do not provide a framework that treats in-distribution (ID) and OOD in a unified way. In this work, we address these limitations by enabling a single detector to detect OOD objects, that are otherwise silently overlooked, alongside ID objects. We present \textbf{SynOE-OD}, a \textbf{Syn}thetic \textbf{O}utlier-\textbf{E}xposure-based \textbf{O}bject \textbf{D}etection framework, that leverages strong generative models, like Stable Diffusion, and Open-Vocabulary Object Detectors (OVODs) to generate semantically meaningful, object-level data that serve as outliers during training. The generated data is used for transfer-learning to establish strong ID task performance and supplement detection models with OOD object detection robustness. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art average precision on an established OOD object detection benchmark, where OVODs, such as GroundingDINO, show limited zero-shot performance in detecting OOD objects in street-scenes.

摘要（中文）: 非分布外 (OOD) 目标检测是一项重要但尚未充分探索的任务。一个可靠的目标检测器应该能够处理 OOD 目标，通过定位并正确地将它们分类为 OOD。然而，一个关键问题是当这些非典型目标完全被目标检测器遗漏并被错误地视为背景时出现。现有的目标检测 OOD 检测方法通常依赖于复杂的架构或辅助分支，并且通常不提供以统一的方式处理分布内 (ID) 和 OOD 的框架。在这项工作中，我们通过使单个检测器能够检测 OOD 目标（否则会被默默忽略）以及 ID 目标，来解决这些限制。我们提出了 \textbf{SynOE-OD}，一个基于 \textbf{Syn} 合成 \textbf{O}utlier-\textbf{E}xposure 的 \textbf{O}bject \textbf{D}etection 目标 \textbf{D}etecion 框架，它利用强大的生成模型（如 Stable Diffusion）和开放词汇目标检测器 (OVOD) 来生成语义上有意义的、目标级别的训练数据，作为训练期间的异常值。生成的数据用于迁移学习，以建立强大的 ID 任务性能，并增强检测模型对 OOD 目标检测的鲁棒性。我们的方法在一个已建立的 OOD 目标检测基准上实现了最先进的平均精度，在该基准上，像 GroundingDINO 这样的 OVOD 在检测街景中的 OOD 目标时表现出有限的零样本性能。

TLDR: This paper introduces SynOE-OD, a framework for out-of-distribution object detection in street scenes that utilizes synthetic outlier exposure via Stable Diffusion and transfer learning to improve OOD object detection performance, surpassing existing approaches and zero-shot OVODs.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了SynOE-OD，一个用于街景中分布外物体检测的框架，该框架利用Stable Diffusion的合成异常值暴露和迁移学习来提高OOD物体检测的性能，超越了现有的方法和零样本OVOD。

Authors: Sadia Ilyas, Annika Mütze, Klaus Friedrichs, Thomas Kurbiel, Matthias Rottmann

UMO: Unified In-Context Learning Unlocks Motion Foundation Model Priors

Abstract (English): Large-scale foundation models (LFMs) have recently made impressive progress in text-to-motion generation by learning strong generative priors from massive 3D human motion datasets and paired text descriptions. However, how to effectively and efficiently leverage such single-purpose motion LFMs, i.e., text-to-motion synthesis, in more diverse cross-modal and in-context motion generation downstream tasks remains largely unclear. Prior work typically adapts pretrained generative priors to individual downstream tasks in a task-specific manner. In contrast, our goal is to unlock such priors to support a broad spectrum of downstream motion generation tasks within a single unified framework. To bridge this gap, we present UMO, a simple yet general unified formulation that casts diverse downstream tasks into compositions of atomic per-frame operations, enabling in-context adaptation to unlock the generative priors of pretrained DiT-based motion LFMs. Specifically, UMO introduces three learnable frame-level meta-operation embeddings to specify per-frame intent and employs lightweight temporal fusion to inject in-context cues into the pretrained backbone, with negligible runtime overhead compared to the base model. With this design, UMO finetunes the pretrained model, originally limited to text-to-motion generation, to support diverse previously unsupported tasks, including temporal inpainting, text-guided motion editing, text-serialized geometric constraints, and multi-identity reaction generation. Experiments demonstrate that UMO consistently outperforms task-specific and training-free baselines across a wide range of benchmarks, despite using a single unified model. Code and model will be publicly available. Project Page: https://oliver-cong02.github.io/UMO.github.io/

摘要（中文）: 大规模基础模型 (LFMs) 近来在文本到动作生成方面取得了显著进展，这归功于它们从海量的 3D 人体动作数据集和配对文本描述中学到的强大的生成先验知识。然而，如何有效且高效地利用这种单用途的动作 LFMs (即，文本到动作合成) ，以支持更多样化的跨模态和上下文动作生成下游任务，在很大程度上仍不清楚。以往的工作通常以特定于任务的方式将预训练的生成先验知识适配到各个下游任务。与此相反，我们的目标是释放这些先验知识，从而在一个统一的框架内支持广泛的下游动作生成任务。为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了 UMO，一个简单而通用的统一形式化方法，它将各种下游任务转化为原子级的逐帧操作的组合，从而实现上下文自适应，进而释放预训练的基于 DiT 的动作 LFMs 的生成先验知识。具体而言，UMO 引入了三个可学习的帧级元操作嵌入来指定逐帧意图，并采用轻量级的时间融合将上下文线索注入到预训练的主干网络中，与基础模型相比运行时开销可忽略不计。通过这种设计，UMO 对预训练模型进行微调，使其从最初仅限于文本到动作的生成，扩展到支持先前未支持的各种任务，包括时间补全、文本引导的动作编辑、文本序列化几何约束和多身份反应生成。实验表明，尽管使用单个统一模型，UMO 在各种基准测试中始终优于特定于任务的和免训练的基线方法。代码和模型将公开提供。项目主页：https://oliver-cong02.github.io/UMO.github.io/

TLDR: This paper introduces UMO, a unified framework that leverages in-context learning to adapt a text-to-motion foundation model for various downstream motion generation tasks like inpainting and editing, showcasing superior performance compared to task-specific baselines.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了 UMO，一个统一的框架，利用上下文学习来调整文本到动作的预训练模型，使其能够用于各种下游动作生成任务，例如补全和编辑，并且性能优于特定任务的基线方法。

Authors: Xiaoyan Cong, Zekun Li, Zhiyang Dou, Hongyu Li, Omid Taheri, Chuan Guo, Abhay Mittal, Sizhe An, Taku Komura, Wojciech Matusik, Michael J. Black, Srinath Sridhar

Stochastic Resetting Accelerates Policy Convergence in Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): Stochastic resetting, where a dynamical process is intermittently returned to a fixed reference state, has emerged as a powerful mechanism for optimizing first-passage properties. Existing theory largely treats static, non-learning processes. Here we ask how stochastic resetting interacts with reinforcement learning, where the underlying dynamics adapt through experience. In tabular grid environments, we find that resetting accelerates policy convergence even when it does not reduce the search time of a purely diffusive agent, indicating a novel mechanism beyond classical first-passage optimization. In a continuous control task with neural-network-based value approximation, we show that random resetting improves deep reinforcement learning when exploration is difficult and rewards are sparse. Unlike temporal discounting, resetting preserves the optimal policy while accelerating convergence by truncating long, uninformative trajectories to enhance value propagation. Our results establish stochastic resetting as a simple, tunable mechanism for accelerating learning, translating a canonical phenomenon of statistical mechanics into an optimization principle for reinforcement learning.

摘要（中文）: 随机重置是一种动力学过程，其间歇性地返回到固定的参考状态，已经成为优化首次穿越性质的强大机制。现有理论主要关注静态、非学习过程。本文研究了随机重置如何与强化学习相互作用，在强化学习中，基础动力学通过经验进行调整。在表格型网格环境中，我们发现即使重置没有减少纯扩散代理的搜索时间，它也能加速策略收敛，这表明存在一种超越经典首次穿越优化的新机制。在一个基于神经网络的价值逼近的连续控制任务中，我们表明当探索困难且奖励稀疏时，随机重置可以改善深度强化学习。与时间折扣不同，重置在加速收敛的同时保留了最优策略，通过截断冗长且无信息的轨迹来增强价值传播。我们的研究结果表明，随机重置是一种用于加速学习的简单、可调机制，将统计力学的经典现象转化为强化学习的优化原则。

TLDR: The paper introduces stochastic resetting, a technique from statistical mechanics, into reinforcement learning to accelerate policy convergence by truncating uninformative trajectories and enhancing value propagation, particularly in environments with sparse rewards and difficult exploration.

简要说明: 该论文将随机重置（一种来自统计力学的技术）引入强化学习，通过截断无信息的轨迹并增强价值传播来加速策略收敛，尤其是在奖励稀疏且探索困难的环境中。

Authors: Jello Zhou, Vudtiwat Ngampruetikorn, David J. Schwab

From the Inside Out: Progressive Distribution Refinement for Confidence Calibration

Abstract (English): Leveraging the model's internal information as the self-reward signal in Reinforcement Learning (RL) has received extensive attention due to its label-free nature. While prior works have made significant progress in applying the Test-Time Scaling (TTS) strategies to RL, the discrepancy in internal information between test and training remains inadequately addressed. Moreover, Test-Time Training based on voting-based TTS strategies often suffers from reward hacking problems. To address these issues, we propose DistriTTRL, which leverages the distribution prior of the model's confidence during RL to progressively optimize the reward signal, rather than relying solely on single-query rollouts. Additionally, we mitigate the phenomenon of consistent reward hacking caused by the voting-based TTS strategies through diversity-targeted penalties. Benefiting from this training mechanism where model capability and self-reward signals complement each other, and the mitigation of reward hacking, DistriTTRL has achieved significant performance improvements across multiple models and benchmarks.

摘要（中文）: 利用模型的内部信息作为强化学习（RL）中的自奖励信号，因其无标签特性受到了广泛关注。虽然先前的工作在将测试时缩放（TTS）策略应用于RL方面取得了重大进展，但测试和训练之间内部信息的差异仍未得到充分解决。此外，基于投票法的TTS策略的测试时训练常常遭受奖励操纵问题。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了DistriTTRL，它利用模型在RL期间的置信度分布先验来逐步优化奖励信号，而不是仅仅依赖于单次查询的展开。此外，我们通过面向多样性的惩罚措施来减轻由基于投票法的TTS策略引起的持续奖励操纵现象。受益于模型能力和自奖励信号相互补充的训练机制，以及奖励操纵的缓解，DistriTTRL在多个模型和基准测试中都取得了显著的性能提升。

TLDR: The paper introduces DistriTTRL, a novel reinforcement learning method that leverages the model's confidence distribution to refine reward signals, addressing the discrepancy between training and deployment environments and mitigating reward hacking issues in Test-Time Training.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了DistriTTRL，一种新颖的强化学习方法，利用模型置信度分布来改进奖励信号，解决训练和部署环境之间的差异，并减轻测试时训练中的奖励利用问题。

Authors: Xizhong Yang, Yinan Xia, Huiming Wang, Mofei Song

Execution-Grounded Credit Assignment for GRPO in Code Generation

Abstract (English): Critic-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves code generation by optimizing unit-test pass rates, but GRPO-style updates suffer from coarse credit assignment: a single outcome signal is spread uniformly across long programs even when failure stems from a localized semantic error. We propose Execution-Grounded Credit Assignment (EGCA), which localizes GRPO updates using execution traces. For programs that satisfy algorithmic constraints but fail tests, EGCA executes the candidate and a canonical reference solution (curated once offline; used for analysis, not supervision) under identical instrumentation, identifies the earliest semantic divergence, and assigns advantage only to the corresponding token span while masking downstream tokens. EGCA is a drop-in modification requiring no critic, auxiliary loss, or learned verifier, yielding 82.1% pass@1 on HumanEval (+3.1 over GRPO) and 68.9% on MBPP (+1.5) with 18% wall-clock overhead.

摘要（中文）: 具有可验证奖励的无批评强化学习(RLVR)通过优化单元测试通过率来改进代码生成，但GRPO式的更新受到粗糙信用分配的影响：即使失败源于局部的语义错误，单一的结果信号也会均匀地分配到长程序中。我们提出了执行基础信用分配(EGCA)，它利用执行跟踪来定位GRPO更新。对于满足算法约束但测试失败的程序，EGCA在相同的检测下执行候选程序和一个规范的参考解（离线一次性整理；用于分析，而非监督），识别最早的语义分歧，并将优势只分配给相应的token跨度，同时屏蔽下游的token。EGCA是一个即插即用的修改，不需要批评家、辅助损失或学习的验证器，在HumanEval上产生82.1%的pass@1（比GRPO提高3.1%），在MBPP上产生68.9%的pass@1（提高1.5%），并且具有18%的实际运行时间开销。

TLDR: The paper introduces Execution-Grounded Credit Assignment (EGCA), a method to improve critic-free reinforcement learning for code generation by localizing reward signals based on execution traces, leading to better pass rates on HumanEval and MBPP.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了执行导向的信用分配（EGCA），一种通过基于执行轨迹定位奖励信号来改进无评论者强化学习用于代码生成的方法，从而在HumanEval和MBPP上实现了更高的通过率。
