Authors: Abhijit Kumar, Natalya Kumar, Shikhar Gupta

Communication-Aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Decentralized Cooperative UAV Deployment

Abstract (English): Autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) swarms are increasingly used as rapidly deployable aerial relays and sensing platforms, yet practical deployments must operate under partial observability and intermittent peer-to-peer links. We present a graph-based multi-agent reinforcement learning framework trained under centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE): a centralized critic and global state are available only during training, while each UAV executes a shared policy using local observations and messages from nearby neighbors. Our architecture encodes local agent state and nearby entities with an agent-entity attention module, and aggregates inter-UAV messages with neighbor self-attention over a distance-limited communication graph. We evaluate primarily on a cooperative relay deployment task (DroneConnect) and secondarily on an adversarial engagement task (DroneCombat). In DroneConnect, the proposed method achieves high coverage under restricted communication and partial observation (e.g. 74% coverage with M = 5 UAVs and N = 10 nodes) while remaining competitive with a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) optimization-based offline upper bound, and it generalizes to unseen team sizes without fine-tuning. In the adversarial setting, the same framework transfers without architectural changes and improves win rate over non-communicating baselines.

摘要（中文）: 自主无人机（UAV）集群正日益被用作可快速部署的空中中继站和传感平台，但实际部署必须在部分可观测性和间歇性点对点链路下运行。我们提出了一种基于图的多智能体强化学习框架，该框架采用集中式训练和分散式执行（CTDE）进行训练：集中式评论家和全局状态仅在训练期间可用，而每个无人机利用本地观测和来自附近邻居的消息执行共享策略。我们的架构使用智能体-实体注意力模块对本地智能体状态和附近实体进行编码，并通过距离限制的通信图使用邻居自注意力聚合UAV间的消息。我们主要在一个协同中继部署任务（DroneConnect）上进行评估，其次在一个对抗性交战任务（DroneCombat）上进行评估。在DroneConnect中，所提出的方法在受限通信和部分观测条件下实现了高覆盖率（例如，使用 M = 5 架无人机和 N = 10 个节点时，覆盖率为 74%），同时保持了与基于混合整数线性规划（MILP）优化的离线上限的竞争力，并且它推广到未见过的团队规模而无需微调。在对抗性环境中，相同的框架在没有架构更改的情况下进行了迁移，并提高了相对于非通信基线的胜率。

TLDR: This paper introduces a communication-aware multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for UAV swarm deployment, demonstrating strong performance in cooperative relay and adversarial engagement tasks under partial observability and communication constraints.

Authors: Enguang Fan, Yifan Chen, Zihan Shan, Matthew Caesar, Jae Kim

Game-Theory-Assisted Reinforcement Learning for Border Defense: Early Termination based on Analytical Solutions

Abstract (English): Game theory provides the gold standard for analyzing adversarial engagements, offering strong optimality guarantees. However, these guarantees often become brittle when assumptions such as perfect information are violated. Reinforcement learning (RL), by contrast, is adaptive but can be sample-inefficient in large, complex domains. This paper introduces a hybrid approach that leverages game-theoretic insights to improve RL training efficiency. We study a border defense game with limited perceptual range, where defender performance depends on both search and pursuit strategies, making classical differential game solutions inapplicable. Our method employs the Apollonius Circle (AC) to compute equilibrium in the post-detection phase, enabling early termination of RL episodes without learning pursuit dynamics. This allows RL to concentrate on learning search strategies while guaranteeing optimal continuation after detection. Across single- and multi-defender settings, this early termination method yields 10-20% higher rewards, faster convergence, and more efficient search trajectories. Extensive experiments validate these findings and demonstrate the overall effectiveness of our approach.

摘要（中文）: 博弈论为分析对抗性博弈提供黄金标准，并保证了强大的最优性。然而，当完美信息等假设被违反时，这些保证往往会变得脆弱。相比之下，强化学习（RL）具有自适应性，但在大型复杂领域中可能存在样本效率低下的问题。本文介绍了一种混合方法，该方法利用博弈论的洞察力来提高强化学习的训练效率。我们研究了一种具有有限感知范围的边境防御博弈，其中防御者的表现取决于搜索和追击策略，这使得经典的微分博弈解法不适用。我们的方法采用阿波罗尼斯圆（AC）来计算检测后的均衡，从而能够在不学习追击动态的情况下提前终止RL episode。这使得RL能够专注于学习搜索策略，同时保证检测后最优的后续行动。在单防御者和多防御者环境中，这种提前终止方法产生了10-20%更高的回报、更快的收敛速度和更有效的搜索轨迹。大量的实验验证了这些发现，并证明了我们方法的整体有效性。

TLDR: This paper presents a hybrid game theory and reinforcement learning approach for border defense, using an Apollonius Circle-based analytical solution for post-detection behavior to improve RL training efficiency for search strategies.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种用于边界防御的博弈论和强化学习混合方法，使用基于阿波罗尼斯圆的分析解来处理检测后的行为，从而提高强化学习在搜索策略方面的训练效率。

Authors: Goutam Das, Michael Dorothy, Kyle Volle, Daigo Shishika

Real-Time Decoding of Movement Onset and Offset for Brain-Controlled Rehabilitation Exoskeleton

Abstract (English): Robot-assisted therapy can deliver high-dose, task-specific training after neurologic injury, but most systems act primarily at the limb level-engaging the impaired neural circuits only indirectly-which remains a key barrier to truly contingent, neuroplasticity-targeted rehabilitation. We address this gap by implementing online, dual-state motor imagery control of an upper-limb exoskeleton, enabling goal-directed reaches to be both initiated and terminated directly from non-invasive EEG. Eight participants used EEG to initiate assistance and then volitionally halt the robot mid-trajectory. Across two online sessions, group-mean hit rates were 61.5% for onset and 64.5% for offset, demonstrating reliable start-stop command delivery despite instrumental noise and passive arm motion. Methodologically, we reveal a systematic, class-driven bias induced by common task-based recentering using an asymmetric margin diagnostic, and we introduce a class-agnostic fixation-based recentering method that tracks drift without sampling command classes while preserving class geometry. This substantially improves threshold-free separability (AUC gains: onset +56%, p = 0.0117; offset +34%, p = 0.0251) and reduces bias within and across days. Together, these results help bridge offline decoding and practical, intention-driven start-stop control of a rehabilitation exoskeleton, enabling precisely timed, contingent assistance aligned with neuroplasticity goals while supporting future clinical translation.

摘要（中文）: 机器人辅助疗法可以在神经损伤后提供高剂量、任务特异性的训练，但大多数系统主要作用于肢体层面，仅间接地激活受损的神经回路，这仍然是真正具有偶然性、针对神经可塑性康复的关键障碍。我们通过实施上肢外骨骼的在线、双状态运动想象控制来弥补这一差距，从而能够直接从非侵入式脑电图启动和终止目标导向的触及动作。八名参与者使用脑电图来启动辅助，然后有意地在轨迹中停止机器人。在两次在线会话中，启动的组平均命中率为 61.5%，停止的组平均命中率为 64.5%，表明即使在仪器噪声和被动手臂运动的情况下，也能可靠地传递启动-停止命令。在方法论上，我们揭示了一种由常见基于任务的重新居中引起的系统性、类别驱动的偏差，该偏差使用了非对称裕度诊断，并且我们引入了一种类别无关的基于注视的重新居中方法，该方法可以在不采样命令类别的情况下跟踪漂移，同时保留类别几何形状。这大大提高了无阈值可分离性（AUC 增益：启动 +56%，p = 0.0117；停止 +34%，p = 0.0251），并减少了日内和日间偏差。总之，这些结果有助于桥接离线解码和康复外骨骼的实用、意图驱动的启动-停止控制，从而实现与神经可塑性目标相一致的精确计时的、偶然性辅助，同时支持未来的临床转化。

TLDR: This paper presents a brain-controlled exoskeleton using EEG to decode movement onset and offset, improving real-time control for rehabilitation by addressing biases in recentering methods and enhancing separability between movement states. Authors: Kanishka Mitra, Satyam Kumar, Frigyes Samuel Racz, Deland Liu, Ashish D. Deshpande, José del R. Millán

Scalable Inspection Planning via Flow-based Mixed Integer Linear Programming

Abstract (English): Inspection planning is concerned with computing the shortest robot path to inspect a given set of points of interest (POIs) using the robot's sensors. This problem arises in a wide range of applications from manufacturing to medical robotics. To alleviate the problem's complexity, recent methods rely on sampling-based methods to obtain a more manageable (discrete) graph inspection planning (GIP) problem. Unfortunately, GIP still remains highly difficult to solve at scale as it requires simultaneously satisfying POI-coverage and path-connectivity constraints, giving rise to a challenging optimization problem, particularly at scales encountered in real-world scenarios. In this work, we present highly scalable Mixed Integer Linear Programming (MILP) solutions for GIP that significantly advance the state-of-the-art in both runtime and solution quality. Our key insight is a reformulation of the problem's core constraints as a network flow, which enables effective MILP models and a specialized Branch-and-Cut solver that exploits the combinatorial structure of flows. We evaluate our approach on medical and infrastructure benchmarks alongside large-scale synthetic instances. Across all scenarios, our method produces substantially tighter lower bounds than existing formulations, reducing optimality gaps by 30-50% on large instances. Furthermore, our solver demonstrates unprecedented scalability: it provides non-trivial solutions for problems with up to 15,000 vertices and thousands of POIs, where prior state-of-the-art methods typically exhaust memory or fail to provide any meaningful optimality guarantees.

摘要（中文）: 检测规划问题关注的是计算最短的机器人路径，以利用机器人的传感器检测给定的感兴趣点（POI）集合。这个问题出现在从制造业到医疗机器人的广泛应用中。为了减轻问题的复杂性，最近的方法依赖于基于采样的方法来获得更易于管理的（离散）图检测规划（GIP）问题。不幸的是，GIP在大规模情况下仍然难以解决，因为它需要同时满足POI覆盖和路径连通性约束，从而产生一个具有挑战性的优化问题，尤其是在现实世界场景中遇到的规模下。 在这项工作中，我们提出了高度可扩展的混合整数线性规划（MILP）解决方案，用于解决GIP问题，并在运行时间和解决方案质量方面显著提升了现有技术水平。我们的关键在于将问题的核心约束重新定义为网络流，从而实现有效的MILP模型和一个专门的分支定界求解器，该求解器利用了流的组合结构。我们评估了我们的方法，使用了医疗和基础设施的基准以及大规模的合成实例。在所有场景中，我们的方法产生了比现有公式更严格的下界，在大规模实例上将最优性差距降低了30-50%。此外，我们的求解器展示了前所未有的可扩展性：它为具有多达15,000个顶点和数千个POI的问题提供了非平凡的解决方案，而之前的最先进的方法通常会耗尽内存或无法提供任何有意义的最优性保证。

TLDR: The paper presents a scalable Mixed Integer Linear Programming (MILP) solution for the Graph Inspection Planning (GIP) problem, achieving significant improvements in runtime and solution quality by reformulating core constraints as a network flow and developing a specialized Branch-and-Cut solver.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种可扩展的混合整数线性规划(MILP)解决方案，用于图检验规划(GIP)问题。通过将核心约束重构为网络流，并开发专门的分支切割求解器，可以在运行时和解决方案质量方面实现显著改进。

Authors: Adir Morgan, Kiril Solovey, Oren Salzman

Controlling Fish Schools via Reinforcement Learning of Virtual Fish Movement

Abstract (English): This study investigates a method to guide and control fish schools using virtual fish trained with reinforcement learning. We utilize 2D virtual fish displayed on a screen to overcome technical challenges such as durability and movement constraints inherent in physical robotic agents. To address the lack of detailed behavioral models for real fish, we adopt a model-free reinforcement learning approach. First, simulation results show that reinforcement learning can acquire effective movement policies even when simulated real fish frequently ignore the virtual stimulus. Second, real-world experiments with live fish confirm that the learned policy successfully guides fish schools toward specified target directions. Statistical analysis reveals that the proposed method significantly outperforms baseline conditions, including the absence of stimulus and a heuristic "stay-at-edge" strategy. This study provides an early demonstration of how reinforcement learning can be used to influence collective animal behavior through artificial agents.

摘要（中文）: 本研究探讨了一种利用强化学习训练的虚拟鱼引导和控制鱼群的方法。我们使用在屏幕上显示的二维虚拟鱼，以克服实体机器人代理固有的耐用性和运动约束等技术挑战。为了解决真实鱼类缺乏详细行为模型的问题，我们采用了一种无模型的强化学习方法。首先，仿真结果表明，即使模拟的真实鱼类经常忽略虚拟刺激，强化学习也能获得有效的运动策略。其次，对活鱼进行的真实世界实验证实，学习到的策略成功地引导鱼群朝着指定目标方向移动。统计分析表明，所提出的方法显著优于基线条件，包括无刺激和启发式“边缘停留”策略。这项研究初步展示了如何利用强化学习通过人工代理影响集体动物行为。

Authors: Yusuke Nishii, Hiroaki Kawashima

Featurized Occupation Measures for Structured Global Search in Numerical Optimal Control

Abstract (English): Numerical optimal control is commonly divided between globally structured but dimensionally intractable Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) methods and scalable but local trajectory optimization. We introduce the Featurized Occupation Measure (FOM), a finite-dimensional primal-dual interface for the occupation-measure formulation that unifies trajectory search and global HJB-type certification. FOM is broad yet numerically tractable, covering both explicit weak-form schemes and implicit simulator- or rollout-based sampling methods. Within this framework, approximate HJB subsolutions serve as intrinsic numerical certificates to directly evaluate and guide the primal search. We prove asymptotic consistency with the exact infinite-dimensional occupation-measure problem, and show that for block-organized feasible certificates, finite-dimensional approximation preserves certified lower bounds with blockwise error and complexity control. We also establish persistence of these lower bounds under time shifts and bounded model perturbations. Consequently, these structural properties render global certificates into flexible, reusable computational objects, establishing a systematic basis for certificate-guided optimization in nonlinear control.

摘要（中文）: 数值最优控制通常分为全局结构化但维度上难以处理的 Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) 方法，以及可扩展但局部的轨迹优化方法。我们引入了特征化占用测度 (FOM)，这是一个有限维的原始-对偶接口，用于处理占用测度公式，统一了轨迹搜索和全局 HJB 型验证。FOM 具有广泛性且在数值上易于处理，涵盖了显式弱形式方案以及基于隐式模拟器或 rollout 的采样方法。在此框架内，近似 HJB 子解充当内在的数值证书，可直接评估和指导原始搜索。我们证明了其与精确的无限维占用测度问题渐近一致，并表明对于分块组织的有效证书，有限维近似通过分块误差和复杂度控制保留了经过验证的下界。我们还建立了这些下界在时间偏移和有界模型扰动下的持续性。因此，这些结构性属性使全局证书成为灵活、可重用的计算对象，为非线性控制中的证书引导优化奠定了系统的基础。

TLDR: The paper introduces a novel method called Featurized Occupation Measure (FOM) for numerical optimal control, bridging globally structured HJB methods and scalable trajectory optimization with intrinsic numerical certificates for improved performance and global guarantees.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为 Featurized Occupation Measure (FOM) 的新型数值最优控制方法，它弥合了全局结构化的 HJB 方法和可扩展的轨迹优化，并使用内在的数值证书来提高性能和提供全局保证。

Authors: Qi Wei, Jianfeng Tao, Haoyang Tan, Hongyu Nie

Gaze-Aware Task Progression Detection Framework for Human-Robot Interaction Using RGB Cameras

Abstract (English): In human-robot interaction (HRI), detecting a human's gaze helps robots interpret user attention and intent. However, most gaze detection approaches rely on specialized eye-tracking hardware, limiting deployment in everyday settings. Appearance-based gaze estimation methods remove this dependency by using standard RGB cameras, but their practicality in HRI remains underexplored. We present a calibration-free framework for detecting task progression when information is conveyed via integrated display interfaces. The framework uses only the robot's built-in monocular RGB camera (640x480 resolution) and state-of-the-art gaze estimation to monitor attention patterns. It leverages natural behavior, where users shift focus from task interfaces to the robot's face to signal task completion, formalized through three Areas of Interest (AOI): tablet, robot face, and elsewhere. Systematic parameter optimization identifies configurations that balance detection accuracy and interaction latency. We validate our framework in a "First Day at Work" scenario, comparing it to button-based interaction. Results show a task completion detection accuracy of 77.6%. Compared to button-based interaction, the proposed system exhibits slightly higher response latency but preserves information retention and significantly improves comfort, social presence, and perceived naturalness. Notably, most participants reported that they did not consciously use eye movements to guide the interaction, underscoring the intuitive role of gaze as a communicative cue. This work demonstrates the feasibility of intuitive, low-cost, RGB-only gaze-based HRI for natural and engaging interactions.

摘要（中文）: 在人机交互（HRI）中，检测人类的视线有助于机器人解读用户的注意力和意图。然而，大多数视线检测方法依赖于专用的眼动追踪硬件，限制了其在日常环境中的部署。基于外观的视线估计方法通过使用标准RGB摄像头消除了这一依赖，但其在HRI中的实用性仍未得到充分探索。我们提出了一种无需校准的框架，用于检测通过集成显示界面传递信息时的任务进展。该框架仅使用机器人内置的单目RGB摄像头（640x480分辨率）和最先进的视线估计技术来监测注意力模式。它利用了用户将注意力从任务界面转移到机器人脸部以表示任务完成的自然行为，并通过三个感兴趣区域（AOI）进行形式化：平板电脑、机器人脸部和其他区域。系统化的参数优化确定了平衡检测精度和交互延迟的配置。我们在一个“工作第一天”的场景中验证了我们的框架，并将其与基于按钮的交互方式进行了比较。结果显示，任务完成检测准确率为77.6%。与基于按钮的交互相比，所提出的系统表现出略高的响应延迟，但保留了信息记忆，并显著提高了舒适性、社交存在感和感知自然度。值得注意的是，大多数参与者表示他们没有有意识地使用眼部运动来引导交互，这突显了视线作为一种交流线索的直观作用。这项工作展示了利用直观、低成本、仅依赖RGB的基于视线的HRI实现自然且引人入胜的交互的可行性。

TLDR: This paper presents a calibration-free, RGB-camera-based gaze detection framework for task progression in HRI. It achieves 77.6% accuracy in a 'First Day at Work' scenario, offering improved comfort and naturalness compared to button-based interaction.

Authors: Linlin Cheng, Koen Hindriks, Artem V. Belopolsky

SOMA: Unifying Parametric Human Body Models

Abstract (English): Parametric human body models are foundational to human reconstruction, animation, and simulation, yet they remain mutually incompatible: SMPL, SMPL-X, MHR, Anny, and related models each diverge in mesh topology, skeletal structure, shape parameterization, and unit convention, making it impractical to exploit their complementary strengths within a single pipeline. We present SOMA, a unified body layer that bridges these heterogeneous representations through three abstraction layers. Mesh topology abstraction maps any source model's identity to a shared canonical mesh in constant time per vertex. Skeletal abstraction recovers a full set of identity-adapted joint transforms from any body shape, whether in rest pose or an arbitrary posed configuration, in a single closed-form pass, with no iterative optimization or per-model training. Pose abstraction inverts the skinning pipeline to recover unified skeleton rotations directly from posed vertices of any supported model, enabling heterogeneous motion datasets to be consumed without custom retargeting. Together, these layers reduce the $O(M^2)$ per-pair adapter problem to $O(M)$ single-backend connectors, letting practitioners freely mix identity sources and pose data at inference time. The entire pipeline is fully differentiable end-to-end and GPU-accelerated via NVIDIA-Warp.

摘要（中文）: 参数化人体模型是人体重建、动画和模拟的基础，但它们仍然互不兼容：SMPL、SMPL-X、MHR、Anny以及相关模型在网格拓扑、骨骼结构、形状参数化和单位约定方面各不相同，这使得在单个流程中利用它们的互补优势变得不切实际。我们提出了SOMA，一个统一的人体层，通过三个抽象层弥合了这些异构表示。网格拓扑抽象将任何源模型的身份以每个顶点恒定的时间映射到共享的规范网格。骨骼抽象从任何体型（无论是在静止姿势还是任意姿势配置下）以单次闭合形式恢复一整套身份自适应的关节变换，无需迭代优化或每个模型的训练。姿势抽象反转了蒙皮流程，直接从任何支持模型的姿势顶点恢复统一的骨骼旋转，从而无需自定义重定向即可使用异构运动数据集。总之，这些层将$O(M^2)$的每对适配器问题简化为$O(M)$的单后端连接器，使得从业者可以在推理时自由混合身份来源和姿势数据。整个流程是完全可微分的端到端，并通过NVIDIA-Warp进行GPU加速。

TLDR: The paper introduces SOMA, a unified body layer that bridges different parametric human body models (SMPL, SMPL-X, etc.) using mesh, skeletal, and pose abstraction, enabling their interoperability and reducing complexity.

VIGIL: Towards Edge-Extended Agentic AI for Enterprise IT Support

Abstract (English): Enterprise IT support is constrained by heterogeneous devices, evolving policies, and long-tail failure modes that are difficult to resolve centrally. We present VIGIL, an edge-extended agentic AI system that deploys desktop-resident agents to perform situated diagnosis, retrieval over enterprise knowledge, and policy-governed remediation directly on user devices with explicit consent and end-to-end observability. In a 10-week pilot of VIGIL's operational loop on 100 resource-constrained endpoints, VIGIL reduces interaction rounds by 39%, achieves at least 4 times faster diagnosis, and supports self-service resolution in 82% of matched cases. Users report excellent usability, high trust, and low cognitive workload across four validated instruments, with qualitative feedback highlighting transparency as critical for trust. Notably, users rated the system higher when no historical matches were available, suggesting on-device diagnosis provides value independent of knowledge base coverage. This pilot establishes safety and observability foundations for fleet-wide continuous improvement.

TLDR: The paper introduces VIGIL, an edge-extended agentic AI system for enterprise IT support that achieves faster diagnosis and self-service resolution on user devices while maintaining transparency and user trust.

Authors: Sarthak Ahuja, Neda Kordjazi, Evren Yortucboylu, Vishaal Kapoor, Mariam Dundua, Yiming Li, Derek Ho, Vaibhavi Padala, Jennifer Whitted, Rebecca Steinert

Collaborative Temporal Feature Generation via Critic-Free Reinforcement Learning for Cross-User Sensor-Based Activity Recognition

Abstract (English): Human Activity Recognition using wearable inertial sensors is foundational to healthcare monitoring, fitness analytics, and context-aware computing, yet its deployment is hindered by cross-user variability arising from heterogeneous physiological traits, motor habits, and sensor placements. Existing domain generalization approaches either neglect temporal dependencies in sensor streams or depend on impractical target-domain annotations. We propose a different paradigm: modeling generalizable feature extraction as a collaborative sequential generation process governed by reinforcement learning. Our framework, CTFG (Collaborative Temporal Feature Generation), employs a Transformer-based autoregressive generator that incrementally constructs feature token sequences, each conditioned on prior context and the encoded sensor input. The generator is optimized via Group-Relative Policy Optimization, a critic-free algorithm that evaluates each generated sequence against a cohort of alternatives sampled from the same input, deriving advantages through intra-group normalization rather than learned value estimation. This design eliminates the distribution-dependent bias inherent in critic-based methods and provides self-calibrating optimization signals that remain stable across heterogeneous user distributions. A tri-objective reward comprising class discrimination, cross-user invariance, and temporal fidelity jointly shapes the feature space to separate activities, align user distributions, and preserve fine-grained temporal content. Evaluations on the DSADS and PAMAP2 benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art cross-user accuracy (88.53\% and 75.22\%), substantial reduction in inter-task training variance, accelerated convergence, and robust generalization under varying action-space dimensionalities.

摘要（中文）: 基于可穿戴惯性传感器的活动识别是医疗健康监测、健身数据分析和情境感知计算的基础，但其部署受到异构生理特征、运动习惯和传感器放置方式造成的跨用户差异性的阻碍。现有的领域泛化方法要么忽略了传感器数据流中的时间依赖性，要么依赖于不切实际的目标领域标注。我们提出了一种不同的范式：将可泛化的特征提取建模为一个由强化学习驱动的协同序列生成过程。我们的框架CTFG（协同时间特征生成）采用基于Transformer的自回归生成器，以增量方式构建特征令牌序列，每个序列都以先前的上下文和编码后的传感器输入为条件。生成器通过组相对策略优化进行优化，这是一种无批评器的算法，它针对同一输入采样出的一组备选序列来评估每个生成的序列，通过组内归一化而不是学习到的价值估计来得出优势。这种设计消除了基于批评器的方法中固有的分布依赖性偏差，并提供了自我校准的优化信号，这些信号在异构用户分布中保持稳定。包含类别区分、跨用户不变性和时间忠实性的三目标奖励共同塑造了特征空间，以区分活动、对齐用户分布并保留细粒度的时间内容。在DSADS和PAMAP2基准上的评估表明，该方法具有最先进的跨用户准确率（88.53%和75.22%），显著降低了任务间训练方差，加速了收敛，并且在不同的动作空间维度下具有鲁棒的泛化能力。

TLDR: This paper introduces CTFG, a critic-free reinforcement learning framework for generating generalizable features from wearable sensor data for cross-user human activity recognition. They demonstrate state-of-the-art results on two benchmark datasets.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为CTFG的无批评家强化学习框架，用于从可穿戴传感器数据中生成可泛化的特征，以进行跨用户的人类活动识别。他们在两个基准数据集上展示了最先进的结果。

Authors: Xiaozhou Ye, Feng Jiang, Zihan Wang, Xiulai Wang, Yutao Zhang, Kevin I-Kai Wang

When the City Teaches the Car: Label-Free 3D Perception from Infrastructure

Abstract (English): Building robust 3D perception for self-driving still relies heavily on large-scale data collection and manual annotation, yet this paradigm becomes impractical as deployment expands across diverse cities and regions. Meanwhile, modern cities are increasingly instrumented with roadside units (RSUs), static sensors deployed along roads and at intersections to monitor traffic. This raises a natural question: can the city itself help train the vehicle? We propose infrastructure-taught, label-free 3D perception, a paradigm in which RSUs act as stationary, unsupervised teachers for ego vehicles. Leveraging their fixed viewpoints and repeated observations, RSUs learn local 3D detectors from unlabeled data and broadcast predictions to passing vehicles, which are aggregated as pseudo-label supervision for training a standalone ego detector. The resulting model requires no infrastructure or communication at test time. We instantiate this idea as a fully label-free three-stage pipeline and conduct a concept-and-feasibility study in a CARLA-based multi-agent environment. With CenterPoint, our pipeline achieves 82.3% AP for detecting vehicles, compared to a fully supervised ego upper bound of 94.4%. We further systematically analyze each stage, evaluate its scalability, and demonstrate complementarity with existing ego-centric label-free methods. Together, these results suggest that city infrastructure itself can potentially provide a scalable supervisory signal for autonomous vehicles, positioning infrastructure-taught learning as a promising orthogonal paradigm for reducing annotation cost in 3D perception.

摘要（中文）: 为自动驾驶构建稳健的3D感知仍然严重依赖大规模的数据采集和人工标注，但随着部署扩展到不同的城市和地区，这种模式变得不切实际。与此同时，现代城市越来越多地配备路侧单元（RSU），即部署在道路沿线和交叉路口的静态传感器，用于监控交通。这引出一个自然的问题：城市本身能否帮助训练车辆？我们提出了一种基础设施引导的、无标签的3D感知方法，在这种模式下，RSU充当静态的、无监督的教师，用于自我车辆。利用其固定的视角和重复的观察，RSU从无标签数据中学习局部3D检测器，并将预测广播给经过的车辆，这些预测被聚合为伪标签监督，用于训练独立的自我检测器。最终的模型在测试时不需要基础设施或通信。我们将这个想法实例化为一个完全无标签的三阶段流水线，并在基于CARLA的多智能体环境中进行概念和可行性研究。利用CenterPoint，我们的流水线在车辆检测方面实现了82.3%的AP，相比之下，完全监督的自我上界为94.4%。我们进一步系统地分析了每个阶段，评估了其可扩展性，并展示了与现有的以自我为中心的无标签方法的互补性。总而言之，这些结果表明，城市基础设施本身有可能为自动驾驶汽车提供可扩展的监督信号，从而将基础设施引导的学习定位为一种有前景的正交范例，以降低3D感知中的标注成本。

TLDR: The paper proposes a novel paradigm for training 3D perception in autonomous vehicles using roadside unit (RSU) infrastructure to provide label-free supervision, achieving promising results in a simulated environment.

Authors: Zhen Xu, Jinsu Yoo, Cristian Bautista, Zanming Huang, Tai-Yu Pan, Zhenzhen Liu, Katie Z Luo, Mark Campbell, Bharath Hariharan, Wei-Lun Chao

$x^2$-Fusion: Cross-Modality and Cross-Dimension Flow Estimation in Event Edge Space

Abstract (English): Estimating dense 2D optical flow and 3D scene flow is essential for dynamic scene understanding. Recent work combines images, LiDAR, and event data to jointly predict 2D and 3D motion, yet most approaches operate in separate heterogeneous feature spaces. Without a shared latent space that all modalities can align to, these systems rely on multiple modality-specific blocks, leaving cross-sensor mismatches unresolved and making fusion unnecessarily complex.Event cameras naturally provide a spatiotemporal edge signal, which we can treat as an intrinsic edge field to anchor a unified latent representation, termed the Event Edge Space. Building on this idea, we introduce $x^2$-Fusion, which reframes multimodal fusion as representation unification: event-derived spatiotemporal edges define an edge-centric homogeneous space, and image and LiDAR features are explicitly aligned in this shared representation.Within this space, we perform reliability-aware adaptive fusion to estimate modality reliability and emphasize stable cues under degradation. We further employ cross-dimension contrast learning to tightly couple 2D optical flow with 3D scene flow. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real benchmarks show that $x^2$-Fusion achieves state-of-the-art accuracy under standard conditions and delivers substantial improvements in challenging scenarios.

摘要（中文）: 估计稠密2D光流和3D场景流对于动态场景理解至关重要。最近的研究结合了图像、激光雷达和事件数据来联合预测2D 和 3D 运动，但大多数方法在分离的异构特征空间中进行操作。由于缺乏所有模态可以对齐的共享潜在空间，这些系统依赖于多个模态特定的模块，导致跨传感器不匹配问题无法解决，并且使融合变得不必要地复杂。事件相机自然地提供时空边缘信号，我们可以将其视为一种内在边缘场，用于锚定一个统一的潜在表示，我们称之为事件边缘空间。基于这个想法，我们引入了$x^2$-Fusion，它将多模态融合重新定义为表示统一：事件导出的时空边缘定义了一个以边缘为中心的同构空间，并且图像和激光雷达特征在这个共享的表示中显式对齐。 在这个空间中，我们执行可靠性感知自适应融合，以估计模态可靠性并强调退化下稳定的线索。我们进一步采用跨维度对比学习来紧密耦合2D光流和3D场景流。在合成和真实基准上的大量实验表明，$x^2$-Fusion在标准条件下实现了最先进的精度，并在具有挑战性的场景中提供了显著的改进。

TLDR: The paper introduces $x^2$-Fusion, a novel multimodal fusion approach for optical and scene flow estimation, using event-derived spatiotemporal edges to create a unified latent representation for image and LiDAR data, achieving state-of-the-art results.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了$x^2$-Fusion，一种用于光流和场景流估计的新型多模态融合方法，使用事件导出的时空边缘来为图像和激光雷达数据创建统一的潜在表示，实现了最先进的结果。

Authors: Ruishan Guo, Ciyu Ruan, Haoyang Wang, Zihang Gong, Jingao Xu, Xinlei Chen

Advancing Visual Reliability: Color-Accurate Underwater Image Enhancement for Real-Time Underwater Missions

Abstract (English): Underwater image enhancement plays a crucial role in providing reliable visual information for underwater platforms, since strong absorption and scattering in water-related environments generally lead to image quality degradation. Existing high-performance methods often rely on complex architectures, which hinder deployment on underwater devices. Lightweight methods often sacrifice quality for speed and struggle to handle severely degraded underwater images. To address this limitation, we present a real-time underwater image enhancement framework with accurate color restoration. First, an Adaptive Weighted Channel Compensation module is introduced to achieve dynamic color recovery of the red and blue channels using the green channel as a reference anchor. Second, we design a Multi-branch Re-parameterized Dilated Convolution that employs multi-branch fusion during training and structural re-parameterization during inference, enabling large receptive field representation with low computational overhead. Finally, a Statistical Global Color Adjustment module is employed to optimize overall color performance based on statistical priors. Extensive experiments on eight datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance across seven evaluation metrics. The model contains only 3,880 inference parameters and achieves an inference speed of 409 FPS. Our method improves the UCIQE score by 29.7% under diverse environmental conditions, and the deployment on ROV platforms and performance gains in downstream tasks further validate its superiority for real-time underwater missions.

摘要（中文）: 水下图像增强在为水下平台提供可靠的视觉信息方面起着至关重要的作用，因为水相关环境中强烈的吸收和散射通常导致图像质量下降。现有的高性能方法通常依赖于复杂的架构，这阻碍了在水下设备上的部署。轻量级方法通常以牺牲质量换取速度，并且难以处理严重退化的水下图像。为了解决这一局限性，我们提出了一种具有精确色彩恢复的实时水下图像增强框架。首先，引入了一种自适应加权通道补偿模块，以绿色通道为参考锚，实现红、蓝通道的动态色彩恢复。其次，我们设计了一种多分支重参数化空洞卷积，在训练期间采用多分支融合，在推理期间采用结构重参数化，从而以低计算开销实现大感受野表示。最后，采用统计全局颜色调整模块，基于统计先验优化整体颜色表现。在八个数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的方法在七个评估指标上均实现了最先进的性能。该模型仅包含 3,880 个推理参数，推理速度达到 409 FPS。我们的方法在各种环境条件下将 UCIQE 分数提高了 29.7%，并且在 ROV 平台上的部署以及下游任务中的性能提升进一步验证了其在实时水下任务中的优越性。

TLDR: This paper presents a real-time underwater image enhancement framework with accurate color restoration, achieving state-of-the-art performance with a lightweight architecture suitable for deployment on underwater devices.

Authors: Yiqiang Zhou, Yifan Chen, Zhe Sun, Jijun Lu, Ye Zheng, Xuelong Li

DualPrim: Compact 3D Reconstruction with Positive and Negative Primitives

Abstract (English): Neural reconstructions often trade structure for fidelity, yielding dense and unstructured meshes with irregular topology and weak part boundaries that hinder editing, animation, and downstream asset reuse. We present DualPrim, a compact and structured 3D reconstruction framework. Unlike additive-only implicit or primitive methods, DualPrim represents shapes with positive and negative superquadrics: the former builds the bases while the latter carves local volumes through a differentiable operator, enabling topology-aware modeling of holes and concavities. This additive-subtractive design increases the representational power without sacrificing compactness or differentiability. We embed DualPrim in a volumetric differentiable renderer, enabling end-to-end learning from multi-view images and seamless mesh export via closed-form boolean difference. Empirically, DualPrim delivers state-of-the-art accuracy and produces compact, structured, and interpretable outputs that better satisfy downstream needs than additive-only alternatives.

摘要（中文）: 神经重建常常以牺牲结构为代价来换取逼真度，从而产生具有不规则拓扑和弱部件边界的密集且非结构化网格，这阻碍了编辑、动画和下游资产重用。我们提出了DualPrim，一个紧凑且结构化的3D重建框架。与仅采用加性方式的隐式或基元方法不同，DualPrim 使用正负超二次曲面来表示形状：前者构建基础，后者通过可微算子雕刻局部体积，从而实现对孔洞和凹面的拓扑感知建模。这种加减式设计在不牺牲紧凑性或可微性的前提下，提高了表征能力。我们将 DualPrim 嵌入到体素可微渲染器中，从而能够从多视角图像进行端到端学习，并通过闭式布尔差分实现无缝网格导出。经验表明，DualPrim 提供了最先进的精度，并产生了紧凑、结构化且可解释的输出，比仅采用加性方式的替代方案更能满足下游需求。

TLDR: DualPrim introduces a novel 3D reconstruction method using both positive and negative superquadrics for compact and structured mesh generation, improving upon existing methods that often lack structure and have weak part boundaries.

简要说明: DualPrim 提出了一种新的 3D 重建方法，它使用正和负超二次曲面来生成紧凑且结构化的网格，改进了现有方法，这些方法通常缺乏结构并且部分边界较弱。

Authors: Xiaoxu Meng, Zhongmin Chen, Bo Yang, Weikai Chen, Weixiao Liu, Lin Gao

CLRNet: Targetless Extrinsic Calibration for Camera, Lidar and 4D Radar Using Deep Learning

Abstract (English): In this paper, we address extrinsic calibration for camera, lidar, and 4D radar sensors. Accurate extrinsic calibration of radar remains a challenge due to the sparsity of its data. We propose CLRNet, a novel, multi-modal end-to-end deep learning (DL) calibration network capable of addressing joint camera-lidar-radar calibration, or pairwise calibration between any two of these sensors. We incorporate equirectangular projection, camera-based depth image prediction, additional radar channels, and leverage lidar with a shared feature space and loop closure loss. In extensive experiments using the View-of-Delft and Dual-Radar datasets, we demonstrate superior calibration accuracy compared to existing state-of-the-art methods, reducing both median translational and rotational calibration errors by at least 50%. Finally, we examine the domain transfer capabilities of the proposed network and baselines, when evaluating across datasets. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance at: https://github.com/tudelft-iv.

摘要（中文）: 本文研究了相机、激光雷达和4D雷达传感器的外参标定问题。由于雷达数据的稀疏性，雷达的精确外参标定仍然是一个挑战。我们提出了CLRNet，一种新型的多模态端到端深度学习（DL）标定网络，能够处理相机-激光雷达-雷达的联合标定，或这些传感器中任意两两之间的成对标定。我们结合了等距柱状投影、基于相机的深度图像预测、额外的雷达通道，并利用具有共享特征空间和闭环损失的激光雷达。在使用View-of-Delft和Dual-Radar数据集的大量实验中，我们证明了与现有最先进方法相比，CLRNet具有卓越的标定精度，至少降低了50%的平移和旋转标定误差的中位数。最后，我们研究了所提出的网络和基线在跨数据集评估时的领域迁移能力。代码将在被接收后公开，网址为：https://github.com/tudelft-iv。

TLDR: CLRNet is a deep learning approach for camera, lidar, and 4D radar extrinsic calibration, achieving state-of-the-art accuracy and good domain transfer.

简要说明: CLRNet 是一种用于相机、激光雷达和 4D 雷达外参标定的深度学习方法，实现了最先进的精度和良好的领域迁移。

Read Paper (PDF)

Authors: Marcell Kegl, Andras Palffy, Csaba Benedek, Dariu M. Gavrila

Discovery of interaction and diffusion kernels in particle-to-mean-field multi-agent systems

Abstract (English): We propose a data-driven framework to learn interaction kernels in stochastic multi-agent systems. Our approach aims at identifying the functional form of nonlocal interaction and diffusion terms directly from trajectory data, without any a priori knowledge of the underlying interaction structure. Starting from a discrete stochastic binary-interaction model, we formulate the inverse problem as a sequence of sparse regression tasks in structured finite-dimensional spaces spanned by compactly supported basis functions, such as piecewise linear polynomials. In particular, we assume that pairwise interactions between agents are not directly observed and that only limited trajectory data are available. To address these challenges, we propose two complementary identification strategies. The first based on random-batch sampling, which compensates for latent interactions while preserving the statistical structure of the full dynamics in expectation. The second based on a mean-field approximation, where the empirical particle density reconstructed from the data defines a continuous nonlocal regression problem. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed framework, showing accurate reconstruction of both interaction and diffusion kernels even from partially observed. The method is validated on benchmark models, including bounded-confidence and attraction-repulsion dynamics, where the two proposed strategies achieve comparable levels of accuracy.

摘要（中文）: 我们提出了一种数据驱动的框架，用于学习随机多智能体系统中的交互核。我们的方法旨在直接从轨迹数据中识别非局部交互和扩散项的函数形式，而无需任何关于底层交互结构的先验知识。从离散随机二元交互模型出发，我们将逆问题 форму 制定为一系列稀疏回归任务，这些任务存在于由紧支撑基函數（例如分段线性多项式）张成的结构化有限维空间中。 特别是，我们假设智能体间交互并非直接可观察，并且仅能获得有限的轨迹数据。为了应对这些挑战，我们提出了两种互补的识别策略。第一种基于随机批次抽样，它补偿了潜在的交互，同时在期望上保留了完整动力学的统计结构。第二种基于平均场近似，其中从数据重建的经验粒子密度定义了一个连续的非局部回归问题。数值实验证明了所提出框架的有效性和鲁棒性，即使从部分观测数据中也能准确重建交互核和扩散核。该方法在基准模型上进行了验证，包括有界置信度和吸引-排斥动力学，在这两种模型中，所提出的两种策略都达到了相当的精度水平。

TLDR: This paper introduces a data-driven method to learn interaction and diffusion kernels in multi-agent systems from trajectory data using sparse regression. It could enable better behavior cloning for multi-agent RL.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的方法，通过稀疏回归从轨迹数据中学习多智能体系统中作用和扩散核。 这可以为多智能体强化学习实现更好的行为克隆。

TLDR-CN: 本文提出了一种带有新型基于排序的表型特征方案的代理辅助遗传规划(GP)方法，用于解决动态多模式资源约束项目调度问题(DMRCPSP)。实验表明，该方法在寻找高质量启发式规则方面比标准GP更有效。

From Workflow Automation to Capability Closure: A Formal Framework for Safe and Revenue-Aware Customer Service AI

Authors: Cosimo Spera, Garima Agrawal, Riccardo De Maria

TLDR-CN: 该论文提出了一个安全且感知收益的客户服务AI的形式化框架，旨在解决由账单、服务、支付和履约中组合的AI代理所产生的突发安全风险。

Learning Human-Object Interaction for 3D Human Pose Estimation from LiDAR Point Clouds

Authors: Daniel Sungho Jung, Dohee Cho, Kyoung Mu Lee

TLDR-CN: 本文介绍了一种人-物交互学习（HOIL）框架，通过使用对比学习和部分引导池化来解决交互区域中的空间模糊和类别不平衡问题，从而提高从LiDAR点云中进行3D人体姿势估计的准确性。

NanoGS: Training-Free Gaussian Splat Simplification

Authors: Butian Xiong, Rong Liu, Tiantian Zhou, Meida Chen, Zhiwen Fan, Andrew Feng

TLDR-CN: NanoGS是一种无需训练、CPU高效的方法，通过局部成对合并来简化3D高斯溅射表示，从而在保持渲染逼真度的同时，显著减少了图元的数量。

Long-Horizon Traffic Forecasting via Incident-Aware Conformal Spatio-Temporal Transformers

Authors: Mayur Patil, Qadeer Ahmed, Shawn Midlam-Mohler, Stephanie Marik, Allen Sheldon, Rajeev Chhajer, Nithin Santhanam

TLDR-CN: 本文提出了一种新颖的基于事件感知的时空Transformer模型(STT)与自适应共形预测(ACP)相结合的长程交通预测方法。该方法融合了动态图构建和与事件相关的严重程度信号，以提高预测精度和不确定性校准。

Physics-integrated neural differentiable modeling for immersed boundary systems

Authors: Chenglin Li, Hang Xu, Jianting Chen, Yanfei Zhang

TLDR-CN: 该论文介绍了一种物理集成神经可微框架，用于对浸没边界流进行长时程预测，与传统方法和纯数据驱动方法相比，实现了显著的加速和更高的稳定性。

Minimum-Action Learning: Energy-Constrained Symbolic Model Selection for Physical Law Identification from Noisy Data

Authors: Martin G. Frasch

TLDR-CN: 该论文介绍了最小作用量学习 (MAL)，这是一种通过最小化结合轨迹重建、稀疏性和能量守恒的三重作用泛函，从噪声数据中识别物理定律的方法。它在可解释性和能量约束方面，展示了优于 SINDy、哈密顿神经网络和拉格朗日神经网络的性能。

SE(3)-LIO: Smooth IMU Propagation With Jointly Distributed Poses on SE(3) Manifold for Accurate and Robust LiDAR-Inertial Odometry

Authors: Gunhee Shin, Seungjae Lee, Jei Kong, Youngwoo Seo, Hyun Myung

TLDR-CN: 本文介绍了SE(3)-LIO，一种激光雷达惯性里程计系统，通过在SE(3)流形上表示姿态并使用感知不确定性的运动补偿来改进IMU传播，从而实现更准确和鲁棒的里程计。它解决了现有IMU传播方法在运动预测和补偿方面的局限性。

Efficient Brood Cell Detection in Layer Trap Nests for Bees and Wasps: Balancing Labeling Effort and Species Coverage

Authors: Chenchang Liu, Felix Fornoff, Annika Grasreiner, Patrick Maeder, Henri Greil, Marco Seeland

TLDR-CN: 本文提出了一种深度学习方法，结合一种新颖的损失函数（CFPL），以高效地检测和分类层叠陷阱巢中的蜂巢单元，解决了密集堆叠、类别不平衡和高标注成本的挑战。

Data-driven forced response analysis with min-max representations of nonlinear restoring forces

Authors: Akira Saito, Hiromu Fujita

TLDR-CN: 本文提出了一种数据驱动方法，利用由min-max函数建模的分段线性弹簧来识别和表示机械系统中的非线性恢复力，并在模拟和实验数据上验证了其用于强迫响应分析的有效性。
